150. neytal
[pakaṟkuṟi vantukaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkum talaimakaṉait tōḻi talaimakaḷai iṭattuyttu vantu,
ceṟippaṟivuṟīi varaivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9-14. tār ārmārpa - mālai poruntiya mārpaṉē,nī taṇanta ñāṉṟu - nī
pirintakālai, kaṭum celalvāḷ cuṟā vaḻaṅkum - virainta celaviṉaiyum vāḷpōṉṟa
kompiṉaiyumuṭaiya cuṟā mīṉ iyaṅkum,vaḷaimēy perumtuṟai - caṅkukaḷ mēyum
periyatuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya, kaṉaitta neytal kaṇpōl māmalar -itaḻ ceṟinta neytalatu kaṇpōlum
periya malar,mālai maṇi itaḻ kūmpa - mālaip pōtil aḻakiyaitaḻkaḷ kuviya; kālai -
viṭiyalil, naṉaittaceruntip pōtuvāy aviḻa - arumpiyaceruntiyiṉ pōtukaḷ itaḻa
viriya, kaḷ nāṟukāviyoṭu - tēṉ nāṟukiṉṟa kuvaḷaiyuṭaṉ, taṇ eṉamalarum - taṭpam uṟa
malarukiṉṟa, kaḻiyum kāṉalumkāṇtoṟum - kaḻiyaiyum cōlaiyiṉaiyum kāṇuntōṟum, pala
pulantu -palavum eṇṇi veṟuttu;vārārkol eṉap paruvarum nam talaivar vārārōeṉat talaivi
varuntuvaḷ, ataṉmēlum;
1-9. yāy -aṉṉaiyāṉavaḷ, piṉṉuviṭa neṟitta kūntalum -piṉṉumpaṭi (vaḷarntu) neḷinta
kūntalaiyum,poṉ eṉa ākattu arumpiya cuṇaṅkum - poṉ pōla mārpilē tōṉṟiya
tēmalaiyum, vampu viṭak kaṇ uruttu eḻutarummulaiyum - kaccu aṟak kaṇṇuṭaṉ uruppeṟṟu
eḻuntamulaiyiṉaiyum, nōkki - pārttu, peritu elliṉaieṉa - peritum aḻakuṭaiyai
eṉṟu, pal māṇ kūṟi - palamuṟaiyum kūṟi, perum tōḷ aṭaiya muyaṅki - periyatōḷai
muḻuvatum muyaṅki, nīṭu niṉaintu -neṭituniṉaintu, aruṅkaṭippaṭuttaṉaḷ - (talaiviyai)
ariya kāvalil vaittaṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) tārār mārpa! talaivi, nī taṇanta ñāṉṟu kaḻiyuṅkāṉaluṅ kāṇṭoṟum vārārkol
leṉapparuvarum; (ataṉ mēluma) yāy kūntalum cuṇaṅkummulaiyum nōkki, elliṉai eṉak
kūṟi, muyaṅkiaruṅ kaṭippaṭuttaṉaḷ.
(vi - rai.) uruttu - vekuṇṭu eṉṟumām. vāṭcuṟā vaḻaṅkumkaḻiyeṉavum,
peruntuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya kaḻiyeṉavumiyaiyum. ceruntiyum neytaluṅ kāviyumkālaiyil
malarum eṉka. kaḻiyuṅkāṉalumiyaṟkaippuṇarccik kālattut talaivaṉ cūḷuṟṟaiṭamākaliṉ
avaṟṟaik kāṇṭoṟum veṟuttāḷeṉṟalumām.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'kaḷavalarāyiṉum'eṉṉum (cevili kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟu kūṟum)
cūttirattu,aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum eṉṉum tuṟaikkaṇ, icceyyuḷ tōḻi cevili
kūṟiyataik koṇṭu kūṟiyatueṉṟu kūṟiṉar; nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
2'iṉpattaiveṟuttal' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'ētam āytal'eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟku,
kūṭṭattiṟku varumiṭaiyūṟu uṇṭeṉṟu palavum ārāytal. atu, notumalarvaraiyak karutuvar
kol leṉavum, pirintōrmaṟantu iṉi vārārkol leṉavum tōṉṟum uḷḷanikaḻcci eṉṟu
uraittu, atu, 'vārār kolleṉap paruvarum, tārār mārpa nī taṇanta ñāṉṟē'eṉa varum
eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, pērā.

151. pālai
[talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 5-15. tōḻi - vāḻi-, kāl viripu uṟuvaḷi eṟitoṟum- iṭamelām
virintu mikka kāṟṟu vīcuntoṟum,kalaṅkiya - kalaṅkiyatum, poṟivarik kalaimāṉtalaiyil -
puḷḷikaḷaiyum varikaḷaiyumuṭaiyakalaimāṉiṉ talaiyiṉ, mutaṉmutal kavartta -aṭiyiṭattiṟ
kappuviṭṭa; kōṭu kavaṭṭa kuṟuṅkāluḻiñcil - kompu pōṉṟa kavaṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya
kuṟiyakāliṉaiyuṭaiya vākaimarattiṉ, ciṉai viḷainta -kiḷaiyil viḷaintatum ākiya,
tāṟuneṟṟam -neṟṟiṉ kulai, āṭumakaḷ arikōl paṟaiyiṉ - āṭumkūttiyaratu kōlāl
aritteḻum oliyuṇṭākkum paṟaipōla, aiyeṉa olikkum - viyappuṇṭākaolikkum,
patukkaittu āya cetukkai nīḻal -patukkai yiṉaiyuṭaiyatāya kuṟainta niḻalaiyuṭaiya,kaḷḷimuḷ
araiporunti – kaḷḷiyiṉ muṭporuntiya aṭiyil taṅki, cellunarkkuuṟuvatu kūṟum -
vaḻic celvārkku uṇṭākumnikaḻccikaḷaik kūṟum, ciṟu cennāviṉ - ciṟiyacevviya
naraviṉatu, maṇi ōrttaṉṉa - maṇiyolikēṭṭāṟ pōlum, teṇ kural - teḷintakuralaikkoṇṭa,
kaṇivāyp palliya kāṭuiṟantōr - cōtiṭam kūṟum vāyaiyuṭaiyapallikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭaik
kaṭantu ceṉṟa namtalaivar;
1-5. tam nayantuuṟaivōrt tāṅki - tammai virumpiyiruppōraip purantu, tām nayantaiṉ
amarkēḷiroṭu ēm uṟakkeḻīi - tām virumpiyaiṉimai poruntiya naṭṭāroṭu iṉpam
mikaiyaintu, nakutal āṟṟār nalkūrntōr eṉa -makiḻntiruttalai māṭṭārāvar vaṟumai
yuṟṟōreṉṟu kūṟi, mikuporuḷ niṉaiyum neñcamoṭu -mikka poruḷai īṭṭa niṉaiyum
neñcattāl, maṉaruḷ piṟituāpa - nampāl mikavum aruḷuṭaiyavarallar āvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! kāṭiṟantōr, tāṅki, keḻīi.nakutal āṟṟār eṉap poruḷ
niṉaiyumneñcamoṭu aruḷ piṟitāya: eṉceyvām.
(vi - rai.) tamnayan tuṟaivōr - tam kuṭikkaṇ iḷaiyārumpaṇiyāḷarum pāṇar
mutalāyiṉārumām. tām nayantaeṉṉum peyareccattu akaram tokkatu. kēḷir -naṭṭār.
neñcamoṭu - neñcattāl. poruḷaiyēkātalippavar aruḷaik kaiviṭuvar eṉṉumkaruttiṉai,
1'poruḷēkātalar kātal, aruḷē kātalar eṉṟi nīyē'eṉṟataṉāṉ aṟika. kāl viripu
eṉpuḻik kāliṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. kalaṅkiya neṟṟameṉavum,ciṉaiviḷainta neṟṟam eṉavum
taṉittaṉi iyaiyum.mutal iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu aṭi, piṉṉatu iṭam eṉṉumporuḷaṉa:
kōṭalaṅkavaṭṭa; allum ammumcāriyaikaḷ. neṟṟam: am, acai. pakutipporuḷvikuti
eṉṟalumām. cennāviṉ kural eṉavummaṇiyōrttaṉṉa kural eṉavum iyaiyum. uṟuvatu
kūṟumciṟu cennāviṉ eṉṟamaiyāl, kaṇivāy eṉṟatupallikku iyaṟkai yaṭai.

152. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟivantu nīṅkuntalaimakaṉtaṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē.
1-3. neñcu naṭuṅkuarumpaṭar tīra vantu - nam neñcamnaṭuṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa
piṟitoṉṟāltīrtaṟkariya tuṉpamāṉatu tīra vantu kūṭi, kuṉṟu uḻainaṇṇiya cīṟūr āṅkaṇ celī
iya peyarvōḷ -kuṉṟiṭattup poruntiya taṉatu ciṟiya ūrkkuccella mīḷvōḷāya nam
talaiviyiṉ, vaṇar curiaimpāl - vaḷaintu curuṇṭa kūntalkaḷ;
4-14. nuṇ kōlakavunarp puranta pēricai - nuṇṇiya kōlaiyuṭaiyapāṇaraip puranta periya
pukaḻaiyum, ciṉam keḻu tāṉai- ciṉam mikka paṭaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ- tittaṉ
veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, iraṅkum nīrpparappiṉ kāṉal am peruntuṟai -
olikkumnīrpparappiṉaiyuṭaiya kāṉalam peruntuṟai eṉṉumpaṭṭiṉattē, taṉam tarunaṉkalam
citaiyattākkum -poṉṉaik koṇṭuvarum nalla marakkalamcitaiyumāṟu tākkukiṉṟa, ciṟu veḷ
iṟaviṉ kuppaiaṉṉa - ciṟiya veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉiṉ tokutipōṉṟa, uṟupakai tarūum moymūcu
piṇṭaṉ - mikkapakaiyait tarum valimikka piṇṭaṉ eṉpāṉatu, muṉaimuraṇ uṭaiyak kaṭanta
veṉvēl - pōrceyyummāṟupāṭu citaiya veṉṟa veṟṟi vēlaiyum, icainalīkaik kaḷiṟu vīcu
vaṇmakiḻ - pukaḻ mēviya nallaīkaiyiṉaiyum kaḷiṟukaḷai vaḻaṅkum vaṇmaiyāṉākiyakaḷippiṉaiyum
uṭaiya, pārattut talaivaṉ āramnaṉṉaṉ - pāram eṉṉum ūrkkut talaivaṉākiyaārampūṇṭa naṉṉaṉ
eṉpāṉatu, ēḻil neṭuvaraippāḻiccilampil - ēḻil eṉṉum nīṇṭamalaiyiṉ pāḻi yeṉṉum
pakkamalaiyiṭattuḷḷa,kaḷimayil kalāvattu aṉṉa - kaḷikkum mayiliṉtōkaiyai ottaṉa:
14-24. tōḷē - ivaḷtōḷkaḷ, valvil iḷaiyar perumakaṉ naḷḷi -valiya villaiyuṭaiya
vīrarkaṭkut talaivaṉāṉa naḷḷi eṉpāṉatu, cōlai aṭukkattu -cōlaikaḷ mikka pakka malaiyiṭattē
curumpu uṇavirinta kaṭavuḷ kāntaḷ uḷḷum - vaṇṭu uṇṇavirinta kaṭavuḷ cūṭutaṟkuriya
kāntaṭ pūvuḷḷum,iṟumpūtu kañaliya āymalar pala uṭaṉ nāṟi, viyappumikka aḻakiya malar
palavum oruṅku nāṟuvatu pōlanāṟivalliṉum vallārāyiṉum ceṉṟōrkku -paricil karuti
vantārkku avar kalviyilvallunarāyiṉum allārāyiṉum, cāl aviḻ -miṭāc cōṟṟai,
neṭuṅkuḻi niṟaiya vīcum -maṇṭaiyiṉ neṭiya kuḻi niṟaiyumpaṭiyāka aḷikkum,māal yāṉai
āay kāṉattu - periya yāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiyaāy eṉpāṉatu kāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya, talaiyāṟṟu
nilaiiyacēy uyar piṟaṅkal - talaiyāṟu eṉṉumiṭattunilaipeṟṟa mika uyarnta malaiyiṭattuḷḷa,
vēyamaikaṇ iṭaipuraii - mūṅkiliṟ poruntiyakaṇukkaḷiṉ naṭu viṭattai ottu, cēya
āyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarum - cēymaikkaṇ uḷḷaṉavāyiṉum nāmnaṭuṅkattakka tuyariṉait
tarum.
(muṭipu) neñcē! cīṟūrāṅkaṭ celīiya peyarvōḷ aimpāl naṉṉaṉ pāḻic cilampiṉ
mayiṟkalāvattaṉṉa; tōḷ naḷḷi aṭukkattu āymalarnāṟi, āay kāṉattup piṟaṅkal
vēyamaikkaṇṇiṭai puraii, naṭuṅkutuyar tarum
tittaṉveḷiyaṉ peruntuṟai naṉkalam citaiyat tākkumiṟaviṉ kuppaiyaṉṉa uṟupakai tarūum
piṇṭaṉatu muraṇuṭaiyakkaṭanta naṉṉaṉ eṉka.
(vi - rai.) naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē eṉpataṉai muṉṉuṅ kūṭṭi aimpālkalāvattaṉṉavāy
naṭuṅkutuyar tarum eṉṟuuraittuk koḷka. cāl - miṭā. cālaviḻ niṟaiyavīcum eṉavum,
neṭuṅkuḻi māal yāṉaiyeṉavuṅkūṭṭiyuraittalumām; kuḻi - īṇṭu yāṉaiyaiakappaṭuttum
payampu. yāṉaiyaiyuṭaiya āay eṉka;āay kāṉam eṉṟalumām. eṉṉa? 1'...aṇṭiraṉ,kuṉṟam
pāṭiṉa kollō, kaḷiṟumika vuṭaiya ikkaviṉpeṟu kāṭē' eṉpavākaliṉ.

153. pālai
[makaṭ pōkkiyacevilittāy coṟṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 2-4. aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷāya eṉ makaḷ,amtīm kiḷavi āyamoṭu keḻīi -
aḻakiya iṉiyacoṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya āyattāruṭaṉ porunti,pantuvaḻip paṭarkuvaḷ āyiṉum - pantāṭu
miṭattucciṟitu ceṉṟu varuvāḷāyiṉum, naṉi nontu vempummaṉ - mikavum nontu
peritum varuntuvāḷ;
13-9. ōṅku varaiaṭukkattu - uyarnta malaic cāraliluḷḷa, tēmpāyntu ārkkum teri
iṇark kōṅkiṉ -vaṇṭukaḷ pāyntu āravārikkum viḷakkamuṟṟakottukkaḷaiyuṭaiya kōṅkiṉatu,
uyarnta ceṉṉi -uyarnta ucciyil, elli - iraviṉkaṇ, mīṉoṭupolinta vāṉil tōṉṟi -
mīṉoṭu viḷaṅkumvāṉeṉat tōṉṟip (piṉ) , kāl uṟak kaḻaṉṟakaṇ kamaḻ putu malar - kāṟṟu
aṭittaliṉ kaḻaṉṟuviḻum iṭaṉellām kamaḻum putiya malar, kaiviṭucuṭaril tōṉṟum -
kaiyiṉāl viṭappeṟumcuṭarppoṟi pōlat tōṉṟum , mai paṭu māmalaivilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkam
poruntiya periya malaikuṟukkiṭṭa curaneṟiyil:
4-13. vēṉil teṟukatir ulaiiya - vēṉiṟkālattuk kāykiṉṟañāyiṟṟiṉ katir keṭutta,
vem kāṭṭu -veppam mikka kāṭṭilē, uṟu vaḷi - viraintuaṭikkum mikka kāṟṟu, oli
kaḻaikkaṇ uṟuputīṇṭaliṉ - taḻaitta mūṅkiliṉ kaṇukkaḷaipporuntit tākkaliṉ, eṭutta -
eḻuppappaṭṭa, poṅkū -viḷaṅkukiṉṟa, poṟi pitirpu eḻu var eri -poṟiyaic citaṟi
eḻukiṉṟa mikka neruppiṉāl,paitu aṟu cimaiyam payam nīṅku - pacumaiyaṟṟaucciyaiyuṭaiya
payaṉ oḻintavum, nal aṭikkuamainta alla - taṉ nalla aṭikaṭku ēṟṟaṉaallavumākiya, ār
iṭai - ariya vaḻikaḷai, iṉi -ippoḻutu, melliya - meṉmaittaṉmaiyuṭaiya avaḷ,vaṉkaṇāḷaṉ
mārpu uṟa vaḷaii - koṭumaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉtaṉ mārpiṉālē muṟṟa vaḷaittu, iṉ
colpiṇippa - iṉiya collālē kaṭṭiviṭa, nampi -ataṉai virumpi, nam kaṇ uṟutaru
viḻumam uḷḷāḷ -nammiṭattē uṟum tuṉpattiṉai niṉaiyāḷāki,oyyeṉa vallunaḷ kollō -
viraiyac cellavallamaiyuṭaiyaḷ āvaḷō;
1. nōtakum uḷḷam- eṉ uḷḷam ataṉai niṉaintu varuntukiṉṟatu ; nōku- yāṉum
nōkiṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) aḷiyaḷpantuvaḻip paṭarkuvaḷ āyiṉum naṉinontuvempummaṉ; iṉi melliyaḷ, malai
viḷaṅkiyacuraṉil, vēṉil veṅkāṭṭup payam nīṅkuāriṭaiyil oyyeṉac cella vallunaḷ
kollō!uḷḷam nōtakum; yāṉnōku.
(vi - rai.) nōkō - ō: acainilai, oḻiyicaiyākak koṇṭuuraittalumām. pantuvaḻi -
pantuviḷaiyāṭumiṭam.vempummaṉ; maṉ mikutip poruṭṭu; kaḻivumām.oyyeṉac cella eṉa
viritturaikka. kaiviṭu cuṭar-paraṇiṉmītuḷḷa kāṉavar kaiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuttapantam.

154. mullai
[viṉaimuṟṟiya talaimakaṉtērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13. valava - pākaṉē!
1-10. paṭumaḻaipoḻinta payam miku puṟaviṉ - mikka maḻaicorintamaiyāl payaṉ mikka
mullainilattē,neṭunīr avala pakuvāyt tērai - āḻamākiyanīriṉaiyuṭaiya paḷḷaṅkaḷiluḷḷa
piḷantavāyiṉaiyuṭaiya tēraikaḷ, neṭu neṟi – nīṇṭa vaḻiyelām, ciṟu palliyattiṉ kaṟaṅka -
ciṟiyapalavākiya vācciyaṅkaḷaippōla olikkavum,kuṟumputal piṭaviṉ neṭuṅkāl alari -
kuṟiyaputarākiya piṭaviṉ nīṇṭa kāmpiṉaiyuṭaiyamalarkaḷ, cemnila maruṅkiṉ nuṇ ayir
varippa -cemmaṇṇākiya nilattiṭattu nuṇṇiya maṇalilutirntu kōlam ceyyavum, vemciṉa
araviṉ paiaṇantaṉṉa - koṭiya ciṉamuṭaiya pāmpiṉ paṭam mēlēnivantāl pōṉṟa, taṇ kamaḻ kōṭal
tātupiṇiaviḻa - taṇṇeṉa maṇakkiṉṟa kāntaṭ pūviṉ tātukaṭṭaviḻntu viriyavum,
tirimaruppu iralaiteḷaṟal paruki - muṟukkiya kompiṉaiyuṭaiyaāṇmāṉ teḷinta nīraik
kuṭittu, kāmar tuṇaiyoṭuēmuṟa vatiya - viruppam vāynta piṇaiyuṭaṉiṉpam mikat
taṅkavum, kāṭu kaviṉpeṟṟa taṇpatapperuvaḻi - kāṭu aḻakupeṟṟa taṇṇiya cevvivāynta
periya vaḻiyilē;
11-3. ōṭuparimeliyāk koycuval puravi - ōṭum vēkam kuṟaiyātakoyyappaṭṭa piṭari
mayiriṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraiyiṉ,tāḷ tāḻ tārmaṇi tayaṅkupu iyampa - tāḷiṉ aḷavutāḻnta
mālaiyiṭattu maṇi viḷaṅki olikka, tērūrmati - tērai ōṭṭuvāyāka;
13-5. cīrmikupu -cīrmikku, nam vayiṉ purinta koḷkai -nammiṭattu virumpiya
koḷkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, ammāarivaiyai - aḻakiya māmai niṟattaiyuṭaiya namtalaiviyai, viraintu
tuṉṉukam - viraintu ceṉṟuaṭaivōm.
(muṭipu) valava! tērai kaṟaṅka, alari varippak kāntaṭpū tātupiṇi aviḻa iralai ēmuṟak
kāṭu kaviṉpeṟṟa peruvaḻi,puravit tār maṇi iyampu tēr ūrmati, arivaiyaiviraintu
tuṉṉukam.

(vi - rai) tēraiyiṉ oliyum ciṟu palliyattiṉ oliyum orutaṉmaiyavātal, 2'tērai
yoliyiṉmāṉaccīramaittuc cillari' 'kaṟaṅkum ciṟupal iyattōṭu' eṉappiṉ varuvataṉālum
aṟiyappaṭum. 'araviṉ paiaṇantaṉṉa taṇkamaḻ kōṭal tātu piṇi aviḻa'eṉṉum ikkaruttu,
1'araviṉaṇaṅkuṭai aruntalaik paivirippavai pōl.....paḵṟuṭup peṭutta alaṅkukulai
kāntaḷ'eṉa muṉṉarum vantuḷḷamai kāṇka. arivaiyaittuṉṉutaṟ poruṭṭut tēr viraintu
ūrmati eṉṟu uraittalumām. tōṭār tār maṇi eṉpatupāṭamāyiṉ, tokuti koṇṭa tārmaṇi
eṉka. tār- kutiraiyiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭum mālai.

155. pālai
[talaimakaṉ piriviṉ kaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6. tōḻi-,
1-4. puṉai yiḻai -aḻakiya aṇiyuṭaiyāḷē!, eṉṟum aṟaṉ kaṭaip paṭāavāḻkkaiyum -
eññāṉṟum pāvaneṟiyiṟcellāta vāḻkkaiyum, piṟaṉkaṭaic celā accelvamum - piṟaṉ
maṉaivāyiliṟ ceṉṟu nillāta mēmpāṭum āya, iraṇṭum - ivviraṇṭum, poruḷiṉ
ākumeṉṟu - poruḷāṉē yākum eṉṟu kūṟi, nam iruḷ ēraimpāl nīviyōr - namatu iruṇṭa
aḻakiyakūntalait taṭaviya talaivar;
6-12. pal vayiṉpayam nirai cērnta pāṇāṭṭāṅkaṇ - palaviṭattum pāṟpacuk kūṭṭam
cērntuḷapāṇāṭeṉṉum avviṭattē, neṭu viḷik kōvalartōṇṭiya kūval - nīṇṭa cīḻkkai
yoliyaiyuṭaiyakōvalar tōṇṭiya kiṇaṟṟiṉiṉṟum mukanta, koṭuvāyp pattal nīr vārntu uku
ciṟukuḻi -vaḷainta vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya pattaliṉiṉṟu nīr vaṭintucellum ciṟiya kuḻi, kāy
varuttamoṭu -nīriṉṟik kāyntataṉālāya varuttattuṭaṉ,cērvu iṭam peṟātu - nīr vēṭkai
taṇittaṟkuccērattakka vēṟiṭam peṟāmal, peruṅkaḷiṟumititta aṭiyakattu - periya kaḷiṟu
mitittēkiyaaṭic cuvaṭṭil, irumpuli otuṅkuvaṉa kaḻinta - periyapulikaḷ aṭivaittu
naṭantu ceṉṟa, cetumpal īrvaḻi- cēṟṟunilamāya īramuṭaiya vaḻikaḷ;
13-6. ceyir tīrnāviṉ vayiriyar - kuṟṟamaṟṟa nāviṉai yuṭaiyakūttar, piṉṟai acaitta -
tōḷiṉ piṉṉēkaṭṭiya, maṇ ār muḻaviṉ kaṇakattu – mārccaṉai amainta mattaḷattiṉ
kaṇṇiṭattē, viral ūṉṟuvaṭuvil tōṉṟum - viralāl eṟinta vaṭukkaḷpōlat tōṉṟum, maral
vāṭu maruṅkiṉ malaiiṟantōr - maralkaḷ vāṭiya iṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyamalaiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟār;
5-6. nōy nāmuḻakkuvam āyiṉum - avaratu pirivāl nāmnōyuḻappōm āyiṉum, tām tam
ceyviṉaimuṭikka - avar tamatu poruḷīṭṭu viṉaiyiṉai muṭittuvaruvārāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻkkaiyum celvamum iraṇṭumporuḷiṉākum eṉṟu kūṟi nam aimpāl
nīviyōr, maral vāṭu maruṅkiṉmalai yiṟantōr; nāmnōyuḻakkuvam āyiṉum, tām tam
ceyviṉaimuṭikka.
(vi - rai.) aṟaṉkaṭai - pāvam; aṟattiṉ nīkkappaṭṭamaiyiṉaṟaṉkaṭaiyāyiṟṟu eṉpatu
1parimēḻakarurai. pāvamāvatu irantōrkku illai eṉṟal.aṟaṉkaṭaip paṭā vāḻkkai, irantārkku
ītalum,piṟaṉkaṭaip paṭāac celvam - piṟarpāṟ ceṉṟuiravāmaiyum ām eṉka. eṉṟum
eṉpataṉaiiraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭuka. celvam - vīṟu, mēmpāṭu,iruḷ ēr - iruḷaiyotta
eṉṟumām. aimpālnīviyōr eṉṟatu, pirivu karutit talaiyaḷiceytōr eṉṟavāṟu. pāṇāṭu -
pāṇaṉ nāṭeṉka; 'pāṇaṉnaṉṉāṭṭumpar' (11) , 'vaṭāatu, nalvēṟ pāṇaṉnaṉṉāṭṭuḷḷatai'
(325) eṉap piṟāṇṭu varutalkāṇka. pāṇaṉ nāṭṭil āṉiraikaḷ tiraṇṭiruntaṉa-veṉṟum,
avaṟṟiṟku nīrūṭṭaṟkuk kōvalartām tōṇṭiyakūvaliṉiṉṟum pattalāl nīr iṟaittup pala
ciṟukuḻikaḷil nirappuvar eṉṟum, vēṉilvemmaiyāl kūval nīr vaṟṟiṉamaiyiṉ
nīriṟaikkappeṟātu akkuḻikaḷkāyntiruntaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉkaṇ nīruṇṇavanta kaḷiṟukaḷ
nīrillāmaiyālāya varuttattōṭuavaṟṟai mitittu naṭantu ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, piṉāṇṭuc ceṉṟa
pulikaḷ ak kaḷiṟukaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭilē kāl vaittu naṭantu ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟum,ak kaḷiṟṟiṉ
aṭiccuvaṭṭiṟ patinta puliyiṉkāl viraṟ cuvaṭu, muḻaviṉ kaṇṇilē viralūṉṟiyavaṭuppōlat
tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟum koḷka. kaḷiṟukuḻiyil aṭi vaittu ēkiyatum, puli, akkaḷiṟṟaṭiyil
mitittuc ceṉṟatum avvuḻicciṟitu īram iruntamaiyāl eṉka. cetumpu-cēṟu.
alpakutipporuḷ vikuti. nām nōyuḻattalāltalaivar karutiya viṉai muṭiyātu oḻiyuṅkol
eṉṉumkaruttiṉāl, tām tam ceyviṉai muṭikkaveṉaōmpaṭai kūṟiṉāḷeṉka.

(u - ṟai.) yāṉai yaṭiyakattum puli yaṭivaittuc ceṉṟa cuvaṭu -muḻaviṉ kaṇṇilē
viralūṉṟiya vaṭuppōlattōṉṟum vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟār eṉpatu, talaivar poruḷīṭṭutaṟkaṇ eytum
tuṉpattaiyēiṉpamākak karutic ceṉṟār eṉṟapaṭi.

156. marutam
[talai makaḷaiiṭattuyttuvanta tōḻi talaimakaṉai varaivu kaṭāyatu]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. muracu uṭaic celvar puraviccūṭṭu -muracaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya celvaratu
kutiraiyatu talaiyiṉucciyiṭattu, mūṭṭuṟu kavari tūkki aṉṉa -iṇaittut taitta kavariyai
nimirttuvaittālotta, ceḻum cey nelliṉ cēyaripuṉiṟṟuk katir - ceḻumai vāynta
vayaliluḷḷanelliṉ civanta arikayaiuṭaiya iḷaṅkatirai, mutuātiṉṟal añci - mutiya pacu
tiṉvataik kaṇṭuañci, kāvalar - vayaṟkāvalar, karumpu arutti -karumpiṉai uṇpittu,
pākal āykoṭipakaṉṟaiyoṭu parīi - pākaliṉ ciṟanta koṭiyaippakaṉṟaiyiṉ koṭiyuṭaṉ aṟuttu
(avaṟṟāl), kāñciyiṉ akattu yākkum -kāñcimarattiṭattuk kaṭṭivaikkum, tīm puṉalūra -
nīrvaḷam poruntiya ūraiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!
12. peruma - perumāṉē!
13-7. yāy - emaṉṉai, nilaittuṟaik kaṭavuṭku - tuṟaiyiṉiṭattunilaipeṟṟa
teyvattiṟku, kaiyuṟaiyāka - kaiyuṟaipporuḷāka, kaḷḷum kaṇṇiyum -
kaḷḷiṉaiyummālaiyaiyum, nilaikkōṭṭu nālcevi veḷḷaikkiṭāay occi - nimirnta
kompiṉaiyumtoṅkum kātiṉaiyumuṭaiya veḷḷāṭṭuk kiṭāyuṭpaṭac celuttiyum,
taṇimaruṅku aṟiyāḷ aḻa -taṉ makaḷukku uṟṟa nōy taṇiyum upāyamaṟiyāḷāy aḻa, maṇimaruḷ
mēṉi - ittalaiviyiṉnīlamaṇi pōṉṟa mēṉi, poṉ niṟam koḷal -pacalaiyāl poṉ niṟattiṉai
yaṭaital
7-12. kuvaḷai uṇkaṇivaḷum yāṉum - nīlappūppōlum maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya ittalaiviyum
yāṉum, tiṟavitu āka -cevvitāka, kaḻunīr āmpal muḻuneṟip paintaḻai -kaḻunīr āmpal
ivaṟṟiṉ itaḻ oṭiyāta pūkkaḷuṭaṉkūṭiya paciya taḻaiyuṭai, ākattu alaippa -
ākattiṉkaṇalaittiṭa, kāyā ñāyiṟṟu - ñāyiṟu kāytalillātakālaippoḻutil, poytal
āṭi - viḷaiyāṭi,polika eṉa vantu - (piṉ) nī vāḻvāyāka eṉakkūṟi vantu, niṉnākap
piḻaitta tavaṟō - niṉṉuṭaṉpiḻaitta kuṟṟattālāya toṉṟō.
(muṭipu) tīm puṉalūra! peruma! yāy, nilaittuṟaikkaṭavuṭkukkaḷḷum kaṇṇiyum kiṭāyum
uṭpaṭaōccit taṇimaruṅku aṟiyāḷ aḻa, ivaḷ mēṉipoṉṉiṟaṅ koḷal, ivaḷum yāṉum
poytalāṭinī polikeṉa vantu niṉ nakāp piḻaittatavaṟō!
(vi - rai.) mūtā - mutiya erumaiyumām. muḻuneṟi - itaḻoṭikkappaṭāta muḻup pū.
puṉiṟṟākkatirmutal tiṉṟal añci eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku,īṉṟaṇimaiyuṭaiya pacu nelliṉ
katiraiyuṭaiyamutaliṉait tiṉṟalai añci eṉka. poytal āṭip polikeṉa vantu - nīvir poytal
āṭip polikaeṉat tāy viṭukka vantu eṉṟuraittalumām.nilaikkōṭu - aṟātu niṟkum
kompu eṉṟalumām.
(u - ṟai) viḷainta piṉ peritu payaṉpaṭum iḷaṅkatirai ātiṉṟalai añcik karumparuttik
kaṭṭip payaṉkoḷvārpōla, piṉṉē perumpayaṉ tarumkaḷaviṉai aḻittup payaṉtarātu ceyyum
alarkūṟuvār vāyaṭaṅki yoḻiya varaintu koḷvāyākaeṉṟavāṟu.

157. pālai
[pirivuṇarttiyatōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 10-14. tōḻi-, muṉaipulam peyartta pul eṉ maṉṟattu -pōr nikaḻcci
kuṭikaḷai iṭattiṉiṉṟum peyaracceytamaiyiṉ polivaṟṟirukkum maṉṟiṭattē, peyaluṟa
nekiḻntu veyil uṟac cāay -maḻaipeytalāl iḷakiyum veyil uṟutalālkāyntu
nuṇukiyum, viṉai aḻi pāvaiyiṉ – vaṇṇam mutaliyavēlaip pāṭukaḷ aḻinta
pāvaiyaippōla,ulaṟi - mēṉi vāṭi, maṉai oḻintiruntalvalluvōrkku - maṉaiyiṉ kaṇ
talaivaraip pirintutaṉittiruttalai vallārkku;
1-9. ariyalpeṇṭir alkul koṇṭa - kaḷ viṟkum makaḷiriṭaiyiṟ cumantuvanta, pakuvāyp
pāṉai kuvimuṉaicuranta - virinta vāyaiyuṭaiya pāṉaiyiṉ kuvinta muṉai corinta,
ariniṟak kaluḻi āramānti -arikkappeṟṟa niṟamuṭaiya kaḷḷiṉ vaṇṭalai -niṟaiyak kuṭittu,
ceruvēṭṭuc cilaikkum cemkaṇ āṭavar - pōriṉai virumpi āravārikkumcivanta
kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya vīrar, vil iṭa vīḻntōrpatukkaik kōṅkiṉ - villāl eyyavīḻantōratu
kaṟkuviyaliṉ ayalatāya kōṅkiṉmītu paṭarntu, elli malarnta paiṅkoṭi atiral -iravil
malarnta paciya atiraṟ koṭiyai, kāṉa yāṉai- kāṭṭu yāṉaikaḷ, perumpular vaikaṟai - periya
iruḷpularkiṉṟa viṭiyalil, arumpoṭu vāṅki -arumpuṭaṉ iḻuttu, kavaḷam koḷḷum
añcuvaruneṟiiṭai - kavaḷamāka uṇṇum accam varukiṉṟacuraneṟiyil;
9-10. tamiyarceṉmār neñcu uṇa moḻipa - taṉiyarākaccellum talaivar ataṉai avar tam maṉam
virumpiēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi colluvar; maṉ-yāṉavvāṟu taṉittiruttalai māṭṭukilēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! añcuvaru neṟiyiṭait tamiyar ceṉmārviṉaiyaḻi pāvaiyiṉ ulaṟi maṉai
oḻintiruttalvalluvōrkku avar neñcuṇa moḻipa; maṉ.
(vi - rai) alkul - maruṅkul. alkil eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ,taṅkiya illil eṉka.
kuvimuṉai - kaḷḷiṉaiccorivataṟku mūkkup pōl kuvintirukkumvāy. ari - paṉṉāṭaiyāl
arikkappaṭṭa eṉka,ceṅkaṇ - kaḷḷuṇṭalālum ciṉattālum civantakaṇ, maṉ-oḻiyicai.

158. kuṟiñci
[talaimakaṉciṟaippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi cevilittāykkuccolluvāḷāyt talaimakaṉ kēṭpac
colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 7. aṉṉai-, vāḻi-,
1-7. urum uraṟu karuviyaperumaḻai talaii - iṭi muḻaṅkum tokutiyavāya mikkamaḻai
peytuviṭṭu, peyal āṉṟu avinta tūṅku iruḷnaṭu nāḷ - ip peyal nīṅki
oliyaṭaṅkiyaceṟinta iruḷaiyuṭaiya naḷḷiravil, ivaḷ - ivaḷ,miṉṉu nimirntaṉṉa kaṉam kuḻai
imaippa -miṉṉal nimirntālottavāyk kaṉattakuḻaikaḷ oḷiviṭa, piṉṉu viṭuneṟiyil
kiḷaiiyakūntalaḷ - piṉṉal nekiḻntamaiyiṉ neṟippuṭaṉ akaṉṟa kūntalaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, varai
iḻimayiliṉ olkuvaṉaḷ otuṅki - mayilaiṉiṉṟuiṟaṅkum mayilaip pōlat taḷarntu naṭantu,
miṭaiūrpu iḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ eṉa alaiyal - paraṇil ēṟiiṟaṅki varakkaṇṭēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi ivaḷai
varuttātē,vēṇṭu - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉai virumpik kēṭpāyāka;
7-11. cūr uṭaiccilampil - teyvaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya malaiyai yaṭutta, nampaṭappai nam- tōṭṭattiṉ
kaṇ, aṇaṅku - oruteyvam, cuṭarppū vēyntu - oḷi taṅkiya pūvaiccūṭi, tām vēṇṭu
uruviṉ varum - tām avvappōtuvirumpiya uruvaṅkoṇṭu varum, (aṉṟiyum,) naṉaviṉvāyē
pōla - naṉaviṉatu uṇmait tōṟṟampōlavē, tuñcunar āṇṭu kaṉavu maruṭṭalum uṇṭu
-tuyilvōraik kaṉavu avviṭattu mayakkalum uṇṭu;
11-4. ivaḷtāṉ -ivaḷō, cuṭar iṉṟu tamiyaḷum paṉikkum - viḷakkuillāmal taṉiyaḷāka
iruttaṟkum naṭuṅkuvaḷ,veruvara - accam tōṉṟa, maṉṟa marāatta kūkaikuḻaṟiṉum - maṉṟat
tiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa marāmarattiluḷḷa kūkai kuḻaṟiṉālum, neñcu aḻintu araṇam cērum - maṉam
naṭuṅkippātukāppāṉa iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvaḷ;
14-8. ataṉ talai -ataṉmēlum , pulikkaṇattu aṉṉa nāytoṭarviṭṭum - pulikkūṭṭattai
otta nāykaḷtoṭartal nīṅkiṉavāyiṉum, murukaṉ aṉṉacīṟṟattuk kaṭumtiṟal - murukaṉai
yottaciṉattiṉaiyum kaṭiya valiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, entaiyumillaṉ āka - em tantai
illiṭattāṉākavum, ivaḷ- ittalaivi, itu ceyal - iṅṅaṉam paṭappai ceṉṟuvaral, añcuvaḷ
allaḷō - añcuvāḷ ākāḷō.
(muṭipu) aṉṉai! vāḻi! ivaḷ tūṅkiruḷ naṭunāḷ, kaṉaṅkuḻaiimaippa, kūntalaḷ, mayiliṉ
otuṅki miṭaiyūrpuiḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ eṉa alaiyal; vēṇṭu; nampaṭappai, tām vēṇṭuruviṉ
aṇaṅkum varum; kaṉavumaruṭṭalum uṇṭu; ivaḷ tāṉ cuṭariṉṟu tamiyaḷumpaṉikkum; kūkai
kuḻaṟiṉum araṇam cērum; ataṉṟalai,nāy toṭarviṭṭum entaiyum illaṉ āka ivaḷitu ceyal
añcuvaḷ allaḷō?
(vi - rai) miṭai - paraṇ. aṇaṅkumār: mār acai; avvaṇaṅkutuñcunaraik kaṉaviṉ maruṭṭalum
uṇṭu eṉalumām.iṉṟu - iṉṟi; illaiyāka. tamiyaḷum - taṉiyēyiruttaṟkum eṉka.
kuḻaṟiṉum ummai iḻivuciṟappu. nāy toṭartalālum ivaḷ añcuvaḷ; avaiaṅṅaṉam
toṭarātuviṭiṉum eṉa viritturaikka.itaṟku nāy toṭartalviṭa, vēṭṭam oḻintuentaiyum
illaṉāka eṉṟuraittalumām. entaiillaṉ ākavum eṉa ummaiyai māṟutalumām.
(mē - ḷ.) 1"nāṟṟamumtōṟṟamum" eṉṉuñ cūttira uraiyilicceyyuḷaik kāṭṭi, itu
miṭaiyai ēṟi iḻintāḷeṉṟatu kāraṇamāka aiyuṟṟa tāyaik kaṉavu maruṭṭalumuṇṭeṉṟatu
mutalākap poyyeṉa māṟṟi aṇaṅkum varumeṉa meyvaḻik koṭuttatu, itu ciṟaippuṟamākak
kūṟivaraivu kaṭāyatu eṉṟum, 2'kaḷavalarāyiṉum'eṉṉuñ cūttirattu uraiyil, ip
pāṭṭiṉuḷ 'miṭaiyūrpuiḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ ivaḷeṉa, alaiyal vāḻivēṇṭaṉṉai' eṉṟatu talaivi
puṟattuppōkak kaṇṭucevili kūṟiyataṉait tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉar,
nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

159. pālai
[piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭatalaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 12. kātal am tōḻi - kātalaiyuṭaiya tōḻiyē!
1-4. teṇkaḻiviḷainta veṇkal uppiṉ - teḷintakaḻiyiṉkaṇ viḷainta veḷḷiya kal
uppiṉatu,koḷḷai cāṟṟiya - vilaiyaik kūṟi viṟṟa,umaṇ-uppu vāṇikar, koṭuṅaka oḻukai -
vaḷaintanukattaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭikaḷiṟ pūṭṭiya, uraṉ uṭaic cuvalapakaṭu pala parappi -
valiporuntiya piṭariyiṉaiyuṭaiyaerutukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum mēyumpaṭi
aviḻttuviṭṭu,uyirttu iṟanta - (uṇṭu) iḷaippāṟi viṭuttucceṉṟa, oḻikal
aṭuppiṉ - oḻintukiṭantakallākiya aṭuppilē
5-12. vaṭiuṟu pakaḻikkoṭuvil āṭavar - vaṭittal uṟṟa ampiṉaiyumvaḷainta villiṉaiyum
uṭaiya karantai vīrar, aṇaṅkuuṭai nōṉcilai vaṇaṅka vāṅki - māṟṟārkkuvaruttattai viḷaikkum
valiya villiṉai mikavaḷaittu, pal āṉ neṭu nirai taḻīi - palapacukkaḷaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa
niraiyiṉaik kavarntukoṇṭu, kal eṉa - kalleṉum oliyuṭaṉ, varum muṉaialaitta perumpukal
valattar - varum veṭcimaṟavar pōriṉai alaittu ōṭṭiyaperiyacerukkiṉaiyum veṟṟiyaiyum
uṭaiyarāy, kaṉaikuralkaṭum tuṭip pāṇi tūṅki. - mikka kuralaiyuṭaiya kaṭiyatuṭiyiṉ
olikkotta tāḷattoṭu āṭi, uvalaikkaṇṇiyar ūṉ puḻukku ayarum - taḻaiyuṭaṉ
kaṭṭiyakaṇṇiyaic cūṭiṉarāy ūṉaippuḻukki uṇṇum, kavalai- kavartta neṟikaḷilē, kātalar
iṟantaṉar eṉa - namkātalar ceṉṟaṉareṉṟu, naṉi avalam koḷḷal -mikavum tuṉpuṟātē;
13-21. vicumpiṉnal ēṟu cilaikkum cēṇ cimai - vāṉiṭattēciṟanta iṭi olikkum
neṭiya ucciyai yuṭaiyatum,naṟum pū cāral - naṟiya pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya cāralkaḷaiyuṭaiyatumāṉa,
kuṟumpoṟaik kuṇāatu -kuṟumpoṟai eṉṉum malaikkuk kiḻakkiṉkaṇuḷḷatum, vilkeḻu
taṭakkai velpōr vāṉavaṉ-viṟporuntiya periya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya pōrvellum cēraṉatu,
miñiṟu mūcukavuḷa ciṟu kaṇ yāṉai -vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum (matam vaḻiyum) kaṉṉattiṉaiyuṭaiya
ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ,toṭi uṭai taṭa maruppu oṭiya nūṟi - pūṇ pūṇṭa
periyakōṭṭiṉai oṭiyumpaṭi aḻittu, koṭumuṭi kākkum -koṭumuṭi eṉpāṉ kāttu
varukiṉṟa, kurūukkaṇneṭumatil - viḷaṅkiya iṭattaiyuṭaiya neṭiya matilcūḻnta, cēṇ
viḷaṅku ciṟappiṉ - neṭuntūramceṉṟu viḷaṅkum ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya,
āmūreytiṉum-āmuraiyē aṭaivatāyiṉum, niṉ pūṇ tāṅkuākam poruntutal maṟantu -
niṉatu pūṇ cumantamārpiṉaip poruntutalai maṟantu, āṇṭu amaintuuṟaiyunar allar - aṅkē
maṉam poruntittaṅkiviṭuvā rallar.
(muṭipu) kātalam tōḻi! umaṇ uyirttiṟanta oḻikalaṭuppiṉ koṭuvil lāṭavar
ūṉpuḻukkayarumkavalaiyaik kātalar iṟantaṉareṉa avalaṅkoḷḷal;(avar) āmūr eytiṉum niṉ ākam
poruntutalmaṟantu, āṇṭu amaintu uṟaiyunar allar.
(vi - rai.) arumuṉai alaitta eṉap piritturaittalumām.koḷḷaṉmā: mā, acai.
kuṟumpoṟai - oru malaiyiṉpeyar. koṭumuṭi -āmūrkkaṇṇirunta oru talaivaṉ.

160. neytal
[tōḻi varaivu malintucolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3-8. niṟaic cūl yāmai - niṟaivāya cūluṟṟayāmai,aṭumpu koṭi citaiya
vāṅki - aṭumpiṉaik koṭicitaiya iḻuttu, koṭum kaḻi kuppai veṇmaṇalpakkam cērtti -

putaitta - maṟaiya īṉṟu putaitta,kōṭṭu vaṭṭu uruvaṉ pulavu nāṟu muṭṭai -
yāṉaikkompiṉāṟ

-ṇ yāmai, pārppu iṭaṉ -kum aḷavai -ataṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu kuñcu veḷippaṭum aḷavu,

koṇṭamaiyiṉ, ceḻunīr taṇkaḻi nīntaliṉ - ceḻumai vāynta nīriṉaiyuṭaiyakuḷirnta kaḻiyiṉaik
kaṭakkuṅkāl, āḻinutimukam kuṟainta poti mukiḻ neytal -attēr uruḷaiyiṉ kūriya

irākkālaṅkaḷil vantu koṇṭiruntatu;
16-9. pāypariciṟantu - (iṉṟu attēr) pāyum kutiraivēkattāṟ ciṟappuṟṟu,
karavātu - maṟaiyāmal, oleṉa olikkum iḷaiyaroṭu - olleṉa āravāramceyyum ēval
iḷaiyaroṭu, valvāy aravac ciṟuūr kāṇa - valiya vāyiṉālē alarākiya oliyaicceyyum
ciṟiya ūrp peṇṭir kāṇa, pakalvantaṉṟu - pakalilē vantatu; (ataṉāl) ;
1-2. eṉ niṟai ilneñcam naṭuṅkiṉṟu - eṉatu niṟaiyillāta neñcamnaṭuṅkiyatu,
aḷittu - atu iraṅkattakkatu, oṭuṅkuīr ōti - oṭuṅkiya kariyakūntalaiyuṭaiya
tōḻiyē!niṉakkum aṟṟō - niṉakkum avvāṟu neñcamnaṭuṅkiyatō?
(muṭipu) kāṉalañ cērppaṉ tēr itukāṟum iraviṉkaṇvantaṉṟu; ippoḻutu pakal vantaṉṟu,
eṉ neñcamnaṭuṅkiṉṟu; aḷittu ! oṭuṅku īr ōti! niṉakkumaṟṟō?
(vi - rai.) yāmaiyāṉatu muṭṭaiyai maṟaiviṭattil īṉṟuputaittup pōkumeṉavum, āṇ
yāmaiyāṉatu ammuṭṭaiyaik kuñcu veḷippaṭum aḷavu pātukākkumeṉavum koḷka. ivvuṇmai,
1'tāyiṉmuṭṭai pōla uṭkiṭantu, cāyi ṉallatu piṟitevaṉuṭaittō, yāmaippārppiṉ aṉṉa,
kāmamkātalar kaiyaṟaviṭiṉē.' 2'tīmperumpoykai yāmaiyiḷam pārppu, tāymuka
nōkkivaḷarntici ṉāṅku' eṉṉuñ ceyyuṭkaḷāṉum uṇarka.

muḷtāṟu. kāṭṭi - kāṭṭa eṉattirikka. muṉpellām iravantaṉṟu eṉavum ippoḻutu pakal
vantaṉṟu eṉavum viritturaikka.ippoḻutu karavātu iḷaiyaroṭu cīṟūr kāṇappariciṟantu pakal
vantaṉṟu eṉṟamaiyāl,muṉpellām karavilē iḷaiyariṉṟic cīṟūrkāṇāmalum, pariciṟavāmalum
iravil vantateṉka.ippoḻutu tēr iṅṅaṉam vantatu eṉṟatu varaivuvēṇṭi vanta teṉṟapaṭi. eṉ
neñcam naṭuṅkiṉṟu;oṭuṅkīrōti! niṉakkum aṟṟō eṉṟatu talaiviyainōkki, varaivu malintu
nakaiyāṭic colliyatu.
(u - ṟai.) 'niṟaiccūl yāmaiyaik kāmam niṟaintatalaiviyākavum, atu aṭumpaṅ koṭiyai
yaṟuttu ataṉaiveṇmaṇaṟ pakkattē cērttatu, talaivi taṉ tāyartaṉṉaiyarpāluḷḷa aṟput
taḷaiyaiyaṟuttuavvaṉpait talaivaṉ pakkalilē cērttutaṉmaiyākavum, atu tāṉ īṉṟa muṭṭaiyai
maṟaittupputaittatu, talaivi tāṉ oḻukiya kaḷavoḻukkattaippuṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmal
maṟaittuvaittataṉmaiyākavum, ammuṭṭai pulavu nāṟiyatu, ompiyatu, akkaḷavoḻukkattait
talaimakaṉ varaiyavantumāṭcimaip paṭuttum tuṇaiyum iṭaiyīṭiṉṟikkuṟivaḻi yoḻukit

161. pālai
[pirivuṇarttiyatōḻi, talaimakaḷatuvēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu muṉṉamēuṇarntāḷ namperumāṭṭiyeṉṟu
talaimakaṉaiccelavu vilakkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9-14. (talaiva !) curumpu tōṭṭu āḻpu uṇa - vaṇṭukaḷmalaritaḻuṭpukkut
tēṉaiyuṇṇa, olivarum irum palkūntal - taḻaitta kariya palavāya kūntalaiyum, am māmēṉi -
aḻakiya māmai niṟamuṭaiya mēṉiyaiyum, āy iḻai- āynta aṇikaḷaiyu muṭaiya, kuṟumakaḷ -
iḷaiyaḷāyatalaiviyiṉ, cuṇaṅku cūḻ ākattu - titalai paṭarntamārpiṉkaṇ, aṇaṅku eṉa
urutta - vīṟṟutteyvameṉa urukkoṇṭa, nal varal iḷamulai naṉaiya -nalla vaḷarcciyaiyuṭaiya
iḷaiya mulaikaḷ naṉaiya, palitaḻ uṇkaṇ - pala itaḻaiyuṭaiya nīlap pūppōṉṟamaiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ, paṉi
parantaṉa - nīrparantoḻukiṉa;

koṭiya maṟavar,aṭi amaipakaḻi āra vāṅki - kutai yamainta ampiṉaimuḻutum
iḻuttuviṭuttu, vampalarc cekutta añcuvarukavalai - vaḻic cellum putiyaraik koṉṟa
accamtōṉṟum kavartta neṟiyil, paṭumuṭai nacaiiyavāḻkkaic cem cevi eruvaic
cēval,poruntummuṭai naṇumpulālai virumpiyuṇṇumvāḻkkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya civanta
ceviyaiyuṭaiyaparuntiṉ cēval, īṇṭu kiḷai payirum - neruṅkiyataṉ cuṟṟattiṉai
aḻaikkum, veruvaru kāṉam -accam tōṉṟum kāṉattai, poruḷ purintu -poruḷai
virumpi, nīnti irappa eṇṇiṉireṉpatu - kaṭantu cella eṇṇiṉir eṉpatai tāṉciṟappak
kēṭṭaṉaḷ kol - avaḷ muṉṉarēnaṉkukēṭṭu ḷāḷō? (āyiṉa) ;

cellutalyāṅṅaṉam iyalvatākum? iyalātukāṇ.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ ākattu mulai naṉaiya kaṇ paṉi parantaṉa, veruvarukāṉam poruḷ purintu
iṟappa eṇṇiṉireṉpatu, tāṉ ciṟappak kēṭṭaṉaḷ kollō?(āyiṉ) viṉaivayiṟ piritalyāvatu?
(vi - rai.) cēval muṭaiyai uṇṭaṟkuk kiḷaiyaip payirum eṉka.aṇaṅku - vīṟṟut teyvam.
atumulaiyiṭattirukkum meṉpatu.

162. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟikkaṇtalaimakaḷaik kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkiya talaimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē-,
1-6. koḷak kuṟaipaṭā- koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟaipaṭātatum, kōṭu vaḷarkuṭṭattu - caṅkukaḷ
perukum āḻattiṉaiyuṭaimaiyāl,aḷappu aritākiya - aḷattaṟku ariyatākiyatum, kuvaiiru
tōṉṟal - tiraṇṭa kariya tōṟṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyatum ākiya, kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa - kaṭalaikkaṇṭālotta,
māka vicumpiṉ - vāṉil,aḻaṟkoṭi aṉṉa miṉṉu vacipu nuṭaṅka - tīyiṉkoṭiyai yotta
miṉṉal mēkattaip piḷantukoṇṭu acaintu cella, kaṭitu iṭi urumoṭu -
kaṭumaiyākaiṭikkum iṭiyuṭaṉ, kataḻ uṟai citaṟi - viraintanīraic citaṟi, viḷivu iṭaṉ
aṟiyā - muṭiviṭamaṟiyāvāṟu, vāṉ umiḻ naṭu nāḷ - mēkampeytalaic ceyyum naṭu
iravil;
7-9. aruṅ kaṭikkāvalar ikaḻ patam nōkki - ariya kāttaṟṟoḻilaiyuṭaiya kāvalar
nekiḻntiruntacevviyai nōkki, paṉi mayaṅku acai vaḷi alaippa -kuḷir poruntiya
acaintu varum vāṭaikkāṟṟuvarutta, tantai neṭunakar oru ciṟai niṉṟaṉeṉ āka -taṉ
tantaiyiṉ nīṇṭa māḷikaiyiṉ oru puṟattēniṉṟu koṇṭiruntēṉāka;
16- 25. vāymoḻi -vāymaic colliṉaiyum, nal icai tarūumiravalarkku uḷḷiya nacai
piḻaippu aṟiyā - nallakīrttiyiṉai aṭaivikkum iravalarkku avareṇṇiya viruppam piḻaittal
aṟiyappaṭātaīkaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya, kaḻal toṭi atikaṉ - kaḻalumvīra vaḷaiyum aṇinta atikaṉ
eṉpāṉatu, kōḷaṟavuaṟiyāp payam keḻu palaviṉ - kāyttalillaiyātalai aṟiyāta payaṉuṭaiya
palāmarattiṉōṭu,vēṅkai cērnta veṟpakam poliya -vēṅkaimaramum poruntiyuḷḷa malaiyiṭam
polivuṟa,vil keḻutāṉai pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ - villaiyuṭaiyacēṉaiyaiyuṭaiya pacum pūṇ
pāṇṭiyaṉatu, kaḷiṟu aṇi velkoṭi kaṭuppa - kaḷiṟṟiṉ mītu eṭutta vellumkoṭiyai
yoppa, kāṇvara oḷiṟuvaṉa iḻi tarumuyarntu tōṉṟu aruvi - aḻakupeṟa
miḷirvaṉavāyiḻiyum uyarntu kāṇappeṟum aruvikaḷaiyuṭaiya,nēr koḷ neṭuvaraik kavāṉ -
nērāṉa neṭiyamalaiyiṉ cāraliluḷḷa, cūr aramakaḷiril peṟaṟkuariyōḷ - accam tarum
teyvappeṇkaḷaippōlap peṟutaṟku ariyaḷākiya nam talaivi;
10-6. aṟal eṉaavirvarum kūntal - aṟalpōl viḷaṅkumkūntaliṉaiyum, malar eṉa vāḷ
mukattu alamarum āyitaḻ maḻaikkaṇ - nīlamalareṉa oḷiporuntiyamukattil cuḻalum aḻakiya
itaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya kuḷirntakaṇṇiṉaiyum, māvīḻ mukai niraittaṉṉa veṇ pal -vaṇṭukaḷ virumpum
mullai arumpiṉai varicaiyāka niṟuttiṉālottaveṇ palliṉaiyum,nakai māṇṭu ilaṅku nalam
keḻu tuvarvāy -puṉṉakaiyāl māṇpuṟṟu viḷaṅkum naṉmai vāyntapavaḷam pōṉṟa vāyiṉaiyum
uṭaiyaḷāy, kōl amaiviḻuttoṭi viḷaṅka vīci - aḻaku vāynta ciṟantavaḷaiyal viḷaṅkak kaiyai
vīci, kāl uṟu taḷiriṉnaṭuṅki - kāṟṟu vīcappeṟṟa taḷireṉa nuṭaṅkivantu, nōy acā vīṭa
āṉātu muyaṅkiṉaḷ - namatunōyākiya varuttam nīṅka amaiyātu muyaṅkiṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! vāṉ umiḻ naṭunāḷ tantai neṭunakar oruciṟai niṉṟaṉeṉāka; cūrara
makaḷiriṟ peṟaṟkariyōḷ vīci naṭuṅki vantu nōyacā vīṭamuyaṅkiṉaḷ, vicumpiṉ miṉṉu
vacipu nuṭaṅka,vāṉam urumoṭu uṟaicitaṟip peyalumiḻ naṭu nāḷeṉavum, veṟpakam poliya
velkoṭi kaṭuppauyarntu tōṉṟu aruvi neṭuvaraik kavāṉiluḷḷa cūraramakaḷir eṉavum koḷka.
(vi - rai.) kōṭu - karaiyumām. māka vicumpu - irupeyaroṭṭu.karumaiyum parappum
paṟṟik kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉaeṉṟār. viḷi viṭaṉ taṅku miṭamumām. aruṅkaṭieṉṟataṉāṟ kāvaliṉ
arumai kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaḷiacaippa oru ciṟai niṉṟataṉāṟ kāvaliṉ arumaikūṟappaṭṭatu. vaḷi
acaippa oru ciṟai niṉṟaṉeṉeṉṟataṉāl, talaivipāluḷḷa vēṭkai mikutiyāltalaivaṉ
iravukkuṟikkaṇ niṉṟa arumaikuṟikkappaṭṭatu. kōlam - kōl eṉak kuṟaintuniṉṟatu; kōl -
tiraṭciyumām. naṭuṅkiya -accattāl naṭuṅki eṉṟalumām. piḻaippariyaīkaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya
eṉa viritturaikka. kōḷaṟavu aṟiyāeṉṟatu, eññāṉṟum kāyttalaiyuṭaiya eṉṟapaṭi.pacumpūṭ
pāṇṭiyaṉ, peyar. neṭuvaraikkavāṉ -nīṇṭa mūṅkilaiyuṭaiya pakka malaiyumām.

163. pālai
[piriviṉkaṇvaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ āṟṟāmaimītūrac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-6. viravu malar kuḻaiya - palavakaiyāya malarkaḷumkuḻaintiṭa, viṇ atirpu
talaiiya - vāṉiṉ kaṇmuḻaṅkik kūṭiya, taṇmaḻai poḻinta tāḻipeyal kaṭaināḷ - kuḷirnta
mēkam corintupōṉakuṟainta peyalaikkoṇṭa kūtiriṉ kaṭai nāḷil,tuṉi uḷamkūra - maṉattē
veṟuppu mika, emiyamākacceṉṟōr uḷḷi - yām tamiyēmākap pirintuceṉṟa talaivarai
eṇṇi, cilvaḷai nekiḻa – eñciya cilavāya vaḷaikaḷum kaḻaṉṟu pōka, perunacai
uḷḷamoṭuvaruticai nōkki - periya ārvam koṇṭauḷḷattōṭu avar mīṇṭuvarum ticaiyai
nōkki(varunti) , viḷiyum evvamoṭu aḷiyaḷ eṉṉātu -iṟattaṟkētuvāya tuṉpattōṭu
kūṭiyiruttaliṉaḷikkattakkāḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu niṉaiyātu;
7-10. kaḷiṟuuyirttaṉṉa kaṇ aḻi tuvalai - yāṉai (nīraipparuka) uyirppatu pōlum iṭam
maṟaiya vīcumpaṉittuḷiyāl, muḷari kariyum muṉpaṉippālnāḷ - tāmaraimalar karintiṭum
muṉpaṉikkālattup pātiyiravil, kuṉṟu nekiḻppu aṉṉakuḷirkoḷ vāṭai - malaiyaiyum
naṭuṅkac ceyvatupōṉṟa kuḷiraikkoṇṭa vāṭaikkāṟṟē, eṉakkēvantaṉai pōṟi - nī eṉpālē
naliya vantaṉai pōlkiṉṟāy;
10-4. puṉal kālayiriṭu kuppaiyiṉ - nīr ōṭukāliṭattuḷḷanuṇmaṇalālāya mēṭu
karaintiṭalpōla, neñcunekiḻntu aviḻa - neñcam karaintu iḷaka,koṭiyōr ceṉṟa tēettu -
koṭiyarāya attalaivar ceṉṟa ticaiyil, maṭiyātu iṉaiyai ākicceṉmati - ayarātu iṉṉa
taṉmaiyiṉai ākiccelvāyāka; (celliṉ) , viṉai vituppu uṟunaruḷḷalum uṇṭu -
poruḷīṭṭum toḻiliṟperuvēṭkai yuṭaiyār eṉṉai niṉaittu varutalumuṇṭu.
(muṭipu) tāḻpeyaṟ kaṭaināḷ; ceṉṟōr uḷḷievvamoṭu (iruttaliṉ) aḷiyaḷ
eṉṉātu,kuḷirkoḷ vāṭaiyē! eṉakkē vantaṉai pōṟi;koṭiyōr ceṉṟa tēettuc ceṉmati;
viṉaivituppuṟunar uḷḷalumuṇṭu.
(vi - rai.) viḷiyum evvamoṭu eṉpataṟkut tuṉpattāliṟantu paṭuvaḷ eṉṟuraittalumām.
viḷiyāevvamoṭu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ keṭāta tuṉpattiṉoṭu (iruttaliṉ) eṉṟuraikka.
eṉṉātu vantaṉai eṉak kūṭṭuka.

164. mullai
[pācaṟaikkaṇ iruntatalaimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ) neñcē!
11-4. pakaivar matilmukam murukkiya toṭi citai maruppiṉ – pakaivaratu matiṟkataviṉaic
citaitta pūṇcitaintakōṭṭiṉaiyum, kantu ocikkum kāl yāṉai -kaṭṭut taṟiyiṉai oṭikkum
kāliṉaiyumuṭaiyayāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya, vem ciṉa vēntaṉviṉaiṭippeṟiṉ - koṭiya
ciṉattiṉaiyuṭaiya namvēntaṉ ip pōriṉai muṭikkap peṟiṉ;
1-7. ñāyiṟu katirkaiyāka vāṅki - ñāyiṟu taṉ katirē kaiyākaīrattiṉaik kavarntu, paitu
aṟa teṟutaliṉ -pacumaiyaṟak kāytaliṉ, payam karantu māṟi - nīrillaiyāki oḻiya,
viṭuvāyp paṭṭa - piḷattalporuntiya, viyaṉ kaṇ mānilam - mikka iṭam vāynta periya
puviyilē, kāṭukaviṉ etira - kāṭu (paṇṭai) aḻakiṉai eyta, kaṉaipeyal poḻitaliṉ -
mikka maḻaipoḻintamaiyāl, poṟi vari iṉavaṇṭu ārppa -poṟikaḷaiyum varikaḷaiyum uṭaiya
kūṭṭamāya vaṇṭukaḷāravāram ceyya, naṟuvī mullaiyoṭu tōṉṟi palauṭaṉ tōṉṟa - naṟiya
mullaippūvuṭaṉ ceṅkāntaḷpūvum vēṟu pala pūkkaḷum oruṅku malara, kāṉam vī kamaḻveṟi
ēṉṟaṉṟu - kāṭu malar nāṟum nāṟṟamporuntiyatu;
8-11. avarnilaievaṉkol eṉa mayaṅki - nam talaivar nilaieṉṉāyiṟṟō eṉa mayaṅki, iku paṉi
uṟaikkumkaṇṇoṭu - tāḻum nīriṉaic cintāniṟkum kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ, āṅku iṉaipu iṉṉātu
uṟaivi -maṉaiyiṉkaṇ varunti iṉṉāmaiyuṭaṉ vativāḷāyatalaivi, tol nalam peṟūum nalkālam
itukāṇṭiciṉ - (nammaik kaṇṭu) paḻaiya nalattiṉaiaṭaiyum nalla kālam ituvākum
kāṇpāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! veñciṉa vēntaṉ viṉaiviṭap peṟiṉ,mānilam kāṭu kaviṉ etirap peyal
poḻitaliṉkāṉam veṟi ēṉṟaṉṟu; avar nilai yātō eṉamayaṅki iṉaipu āṅku iṉṉātu uṟaivi
toṉṉalampeṟūum naṟkālam itukāṇ.
(vi - rai.) payam - vaḷamumām. viṭuvāy - piḷappu. nalam peṟūumnaṟkālam itu eṉa
māṟuka. veñciṉa vēntaṉviṉaiviṭap peṟiṉ eṉṟamaiyāl, vēntaṉveṟṟi yeytiyum ciṉa
mikutiyāṟ cantiṟkuoruppaṭātuḷaṉ eṉpatu pōtarum.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'ēṉōrmaruṅkiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, (icceyyuḷ) piritaṟ pakutiyākiya
pācaṟaip pulampal eṉiṉumnilam paṟṟi mullaiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟuraittaṉar, iḷampūraṉār.

165. pālai
[makaṭpōkkiya tāyatunilaimai kaṇṭār colliyatu.]
kayantalai maṭappiṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉak

(co - ḷ.) 1-4. kayantalai maṭappiṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉa -melliya talaiyaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya
peṇyāṉai kuḻiyilakappaṭṭatāka, kaḷiṟu viḷippaṭutta kampalai verīi- kaḷiṟṟuyāṉai kūppīṭu
ceyta āravārattiṟkuañci, oyyeṉa eḻunta cevvāyk kuḻavi -virainteḻunta civanta
vāyiṉatāya ataṉ kaṉṟu, tātueru maṟukiṉ mūtūr āṅkaṇ - tātākiya
eruviṉaiyuṭaiyaterukkaḷaiyuṭaiya paḻaiya ūrāya avviṭattu, erumai nalāṉ peṟu mulai
māntum - erumaiyāya nallamāṭṭiṉiṉṟum peṟukiṉṟa. mulaippālai uṇṇum iṭamāya, nāṭu
pala iṟanta naṉṉa rāṭṭikku - pala nāṭukaḷaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa naṉmaiyai yuṭaiyāḷ poruṭṭu ;
7. āyamum aṇi iḻantu aḻuṅkiṉṟu - āyamum poliviḻantu varuntukiṉṟatu;
7-13. tāyum - tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir eṉa - eṉuyir keṭuvatāka eṉṟu nontu kūṟi,
tarumaṇal kiṭanta pāvai - koṇarntu parappiya maṇalil kiṭanta makaḷatu pāvaiyai eṭuttu,
kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi - ataṉ kaṇṇaiyum neṟṟiyaiyum taṭavi, taṭavu nilai nocci vari niḻal
taṇ eṉa acaii - vaḷainta nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nocciyiṉ vari variyākavuḷḷa niḻalil taṭpamuṟak
kiṭatti, tāḻik kuvaḷai vāṭumalar cūṭṭi - tāḻikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa kuvaḷaiyiṉ vāṭiya malaraic
cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē - eṉ ariya makaḷē, iṉ tōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum - niṉatu
iṉiya tōḷait taruvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟit taḻuvi aḻāniṟpaḷ.
(muṭipu) nāṭu pala iṟanta naṉṉarāṭṭikku āyamum apaṅkiṉṟu; tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir
eṉakkūṟi, pāvaiyaik kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi, niḻal acaii, malar cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē
iṉtōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum.
(vi - rai.) kaṟpukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭu kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟāḷākaliṉ - 'naṉṉarāṭṭi' eṉṟār. āyamum
aṇiyiḻan taḻuṅkiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāl tāyatu nilaimaiyōṭu āyattiṉ nilaimaiyaiyum kaṇṭār
colliṉar eṉṟu koḷka. āyamum tāyum eṉṉum ummaikaḷ eccapporuḷaṉa. īṉṟōṭṭārāy eṉpatu
pāṭamāyiṉ, tāy pāvaiyai nōkki, niṉṉai īṉṟavaḷākiyaeṉ makaḷaik kāṭṭuvāyāka eṉṟu
kūṟiṉāḷeṉakkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. taṇṇeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ eṉṟuiyaittalumām. talaivi yiruntu nīr
vākkappeṟāmaiyāl, tāḻik kuvaḷaiyiṉ malarvāṭuvatāyiṟṟu.

(mē - ḷ.) 1'taṉṉumavaṉum avaḷum cuṭṭi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu(icceyyuḷ) 'tāy
nilaiyum āyattu nilaiyumkaṇṭōr kūṟiyavāṟuṇarka' eṉṟum 2'pālkeḻukiḷavi nālvarkku
murittē' eṉṉuñcūttirattu, 'tarumaṇaṟ kiṭanta pāvaiyeṉ, arumakaḷēyeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ

166. marutam
[parattaiyoṭu puṉalāṭiya talaimakaṉ talaimakaḷiṭaip pukku yāṉ-āṭiṟṟilaṉ eṉṟu cūḷuṟṟāṉ
eṉpatu kēṭṭa 3parattaitaṉ pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 11. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. nalmaram kuḻīiya- nalla maraṅkaḷ cūḻnta (kaḷ viṟkum illiluḷḷa) , pal nāḷ aritta
naṉaimutir cāṭi - palanāḷumvaṭikkappeṟṟa kaḷ niṟainta cāṭiyai, kōoyuṭaippiṉ -

aṉaiyēṉ āyiṉaṇaṅkuka eṉ eṉa - yāṉ puṉalāṭi vantēṉāyiṉ eṉṉaivaruttuvatāka eṉṟu ;

teḷiyavaippāṉāyiṉ;

vatuvai īr aṇippolinta - kūṭṭattiṟkuriya periya aṇiyāṟpolivuṟṟa, nammoṭu
āṭiyōr - nammoṭu kūṭippuṉalāṭiṉōr ; yārkol - yārōtāṉ?

167. pālai

(co - ḷ.) 7.neñcē-,
1-5. vayaṅku maṇiporuta - paḷiṅku maṇiyiṉai yotta, vakaiamaivaṉappiṉ - kūṟupāṭamainta
aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya, pacukāḻ alkul māayōḷoṭu - paciya carattālāyamēkalaiyai aṇinta
alkulaiyuṭaiya māmai niṟattiṉaḷāyanam talaiviyuṭaṉ, viṇ poru neṭunakar - vāṉaiaḷāvum
nīṇṭa māḷikaiyil, viṉai vaṉappu eytiyapuṉai pū cēkkai taṅki-aḻakiya vēlaippāṭu
amaintapuṉaiyappeṟṟa pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya paḷḷiyiṉkaṇtaṅkutalāṉē, iṉṟu - iṉṟaiyap pōḻtu,

periya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyayāṉaiyiṉ, verin ōṅku ciṟupuṟamuriña -mutukiṉiṉ ṟuyarntu
piṭari urāytaliṉ, olkiiṭṭikai neṭuñcuvar viṭṭam vīḻnteṉa -taḷarntu ceṅkallālāya nīṇṭa

viṭṭoḻinta maramcōrnta māṭattiṉaiyum, eḻutu aṇi kaṭavuḷpōkaliṉ - eḻutappeṟṟa aḻakiya

nikaḻum pali maṟakkappeṟṟa,meḻu kāp puṉ tiṇai - meḻukappaṭāta pulliyatiṇṇaiyil, pāl
nāy tuṉṉiya paṟaikkaṇ ciṟuil - īṉṟaṇimaiyuṭaiya nāy taṅkiya paṟintaiṭattaiyuṭaiya
ciṟṟilaiyum, avviṭattē kuyilkāḻ citaiya maṇṭi - iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kaim maraṅkaḷcitaiyumāṟu

iṟappiṉaiyumuṭaiya ampalattiṉ kaṇ ;

yuṭaiyēmāy, nāḷai - nāḷaip pōḻtil,cēkkuvam kol - taṅki iruppēmō

168. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟi vantatalaimakaḷai iravukkuṟi vilakki varaivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3. vāṉ tōy veṟpa - vāṉai aḷāviya malaiyaiyuṭaiyatalaivaṉē!
1-2. yāmam nummoṭukaḻippi - irāppoḻutai num muṭaṉ kūṭik kaḻittu,nōy mika -
piṉpu iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam añcittuṉpam mikutalāl, paṉivār kaṇṇēm vaikutum -nīr
oḻukuṅ kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyēmāy varuntiyirukkiṉṟēm ākaliṉ;
3-14. pallāṉkuṉṟil paṭuniḻal cērnta - pallāṉ kuṉṟueṉṉum malaiyiṟ poruntum
niḻaliṉkaṭcernta, nal āṉ parappiṉ kuḻumūr āṅkaṇ -nalla āṉiraiyiṉ parappiṉaik
koṇṭakuḻumūriṭattē, koṭaik kaṭaṉ ēṉṟa kōṭāneñciṉ - īkaiyāya kaṭaṉai
ēṟṟukkoṇṭakōṭṭamillāta neñciṉaiyuṭaiya, utiyaṉaṭṭilpōla - utiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu aṭukkaḷai
pōla,oli eḻuntu aruvi ārkkum peruvaraic cilampiṉ- olimikku aruvikaḷ āravārittu
iḻiyum periyavaraiyiṉatu pakka malaiyil, kaḷiṟu īṉṟaṇi irupiṭitaḻīi - kaḷiṟu īṉṟa
aṇimaiyuṭaiya periya piṭiyiṉaittaḻuvikkoṇṭu, taṉ tūṅku naṭaikkuḻavi tuyil
puṟamkāppa - taṉatu acainta naṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kuḻaviuṟaṅkukiṉṟa iṭattaik kāttirukka,
kaṭuṅkaṇvāḷ vari vayappuli - taṟukaṇmaiyuṭaiya vāḷ pōṉṟavariyiṉaiyuṭaiya valiya puli,
oṭuṅku aḷai pulampa pōki- oṭuṅkiya muḻai taṉittiṭap puṟam pōy, kal muḻaiuraṟa -
malaiyiṉ kukaiyiṭattil muḻaṅka, kāṉavarmaṭinta kaṅkul - vēṭṭuvarum tuyilum
naḷḷiravil,māṉ atarc ciṟu neṟi - vilaṅkukaḷ cellumvaḻiyākiya ciṟuneṟiyil, nī
varutal - nī varutalai,iṉi āṉṟal vēṇṭum - iṉi oḻital vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) veṟpa! yāmam nummoṭu kaḻippi, paṉivārkaṇṇēm vaikutum ākaliṉ, cilampil,
kaḷiṟukāppa, puli uraṟa kāṉavar maṭinta kaṅkulil ciṟuneṟikkaṇ nī varutalai iṉi āṉṟal
vēṇṭum.
(vi - rai.) irāp poḻutai nummuṭaṉ kaḻikkum nāḷellāmappoḻutu iṉpamāyiṉum nī
varum vaḻiyiṉētattai niṉaintu peritum tuṉpuṟukiṉṟōm.ākaliṉ, iṉi nī avvāṟu
varutalai oḻital vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi, iravukkuṟi vilakkivaraivukaṭāyiṉāḷeṉka. āṉṟal -
amaital: oḻital.pallāṉ kuṉṟu - oru malaiyiṉ peyar pōlum;vallāṉ kuṉṟu eṉpatu
pāṭamāyiṉ vallāṇmaikkuiṭamākiya kuṉṟu eṉka. aṭṭilpōla - aṭṭilil oliyeḻutalpōla.
oli ; uṇṇavaruvārālāvatu. īṉṟaṇiīṉṟa aṇimai. oṭuṅku aḷai - puli tuyiṉṟa iṭṭiya
muḻaieṉavum, kaṉmuḻai - periya malaiyiṉ kukai eṉavumkoḷka. kaḷiṟu kākkumāṟu puli
uraṟa eṉka. ikkaruttu1'ilaṅku maruppi yāṉai, kuṟumpoṟai maruṅkiṉ amartuṇaitaḻīik,
koṭuvari irumpuli kākkum' eṉṉumceyyuḷil amaintiruttaluṅ kāṇka.
māṉatarcciṟuneṟi eṉṟamaiyāl makkaḷ iyaṅkutaṟkākātaiṭukiya neṟi eṉpatu peṟṟām.

169. pālai
[talaimakaṉ,iṭaiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē!
1-8. maram talaikarintu nilam payam vāṭa - maraṅkaḷ uccikariyavum nilam vaḷam kuṉṟavum,
alaṅku katirvēynta aḻal tikaḻ naṉantalai - acaikiṉṟañāyiṟṟiṉ katir kuḻappeṟṟa vemmai
viḷaṅkumakaṉṟa pālai nilattē, puli tolaittu uṇṭa perumkaḷiṟṟu oḻi ūṉ - puliyāṟ
koṉṟutiṉṉappeṟṟa periya yāṉaiyiṉatu eñcikkiṭantaūṉil, kalikeḻu maṟavar kāḻkōttu
oḻintatai -āravāram poruntiya āṟalaippōr kōliṟkōttuk (koṇṭēkiyatu pōka)
eñciyatai,olitirai kaṭalviḷai amiḻtiṉ kaṇam cāl umaṇar- olikkum alai poruntiya kaṭalil
viḷaiyumuppiṉaik koṇaram kūṭṭam mikka uppuvāṇikar, ñelikōl ciṟu tī māṭṭi -
tīkkaṭaikōlālākkiya ciṟu tīyil vatakki, cuṉai koḷtī nīr cōṟṟu ulai kūṭṭum
-cuṉaiyiṟkoṇṭa iṉiya nīrālāya cōṟṟiṉulaiyil kūṭṭi ākkum, curampala kaṭanta nam vayiṉ-
pala cura neṟikaḷaiyum kaṭantu vantuḷa nammiṭattu,paṭarntu - taṉ uḷḷam paṭartalāl;
8-14. tiruntu iḻai -tiruntiya aṇikaḷaiyuṭaiya nam talaivi, celkatirmaḻukiya
pulampukoḷ mālai - maṟaiyum katir maṅkiyataṉimai koṇṭa mālaiyil, naṉi pacalai
pāyntamēṉiyaḷ - pacalai mikavum paranta mēṉiyaḷāki, neṭituniṉaintu - nīḷa niṉaintu,
mel viral cērttiyanutalaḷ - melliya viralkaḷaic cērttaneṟṟiyiṉaḷāy, kayal umiḻ nīril
kaṇ paṉimalki vāra - kayalmīṉ umiḻum nīrpōlakkaṇkaḷil nīr niṟaintu oḻuka, perum
tōḷnekiḻnta cellaloṭu - periya tōḷmelintatālāya tuṉpattuṭaṉ, varuntum -varuntuvaḷ,
aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! curampala kaṭanta namvayiṉ taṉ uḷḷampaṭar taliṉ, tiruntiḻai, mālaiyilē
neṭituniṉaintu pacalai pāynta mēṉiyaḷāy, viralcērtti nutalaḷāy, kaṇpaṉi vārac
cellaloṭuvaruntum; aḷiyaḷ.
(vi - rai.) karintu - kariya eṉat tirikka. payam - ēṉaivaḷaṅkaḷ. kāḻ - iruppuc
calākai. kaṭalviḷaiamiḻtu - uppu. kaṇam cāl umaṇar eṉṟamaiyiṉumaṇar kūṭṭamākavē varuvar
eṉpatu peṟṟām.ciṉaikoḷ tī nīr eṉṟamaiyāl curattil nīriṉmai peṟappaṭum. paṭarntu -
paṭātalāl.

170. neytal
[talaimakaḷ, kāmamikkakaḻipaṭarkiḷaviyāṟ coṟṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 8. alava - naṇṭē!
4-7. iru kaḻi malarnta- kariya nīrōṭaiyil malarnta, kaṇ pōlneytal kamaḻ itaḻ nāṟṟam -
kaṇṇaippōlumneytaṟ pūviṉ kamaḻum itaḻ nāṟṟattiṉai,amiḻtu eṉa nacaii - amiḻtam pōlum
eṉavirumpic ceṉṟu, taṇ tātu ūtiya - kuḷirntatātiṉai uṇṭa, vaṇṭu iṉam kaḷi
ciṟantu - vaṇṭiṉkūṭṭam kaḷippumikku, paṟaiiya taḷarum tuṟaivaṉai -paṟattaṟku iyalātu
cōrum tuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉukku ;
1-2. kāṉalum kaḻaṟātu -kaṭaṟkaraic cōlaiyum ceṉṟu tūtu kūṟātu, kaḻiyumkūṟātu -
uppaṅkaḻiyum iyampātu, tēṉ imirnaṟumalarp puṉṉaiyum moḻiyātu - vaṇṭukaḷolikkum
naṟiya malariṉaiyuṭaiya puṉṉaiyummoḻiyātu;
3. oru niṉ allatupiṟitu yātumilaṉ - niṉṉaiyē yaṉṟi vēṟuettuṇaiyum illēṉ;
9-14. kaitaiam paṭuciṉai - tāḻai marattiṉ tāḻnta kiḷaiyil, kāmarpeṭaiyoṭu evvamoṭu
acāam - viruppam mikkapeṭaiyuṭaṉ varuttamuṭaṉ taḷarntirukkum, kaṭalciṟu kākkai -
ciṟiya kaṭaṟkākkai, kōṭṭu mīṉvaḻaṅkum vēṭṭam maṭi parappiṉ - cuṟāmīṉiyaṅkum
vēṭṭaiyāṭutal nīṅkiya iṭattiluḷḷa,veḷ iṟā kaṉavum - veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉaip paṟṟiyuṇpatākak
kaṉavu kāṇum, naḷ eṉ yāmattu - iruḷceṟinta naṭuiravil vantu, palkāl niṉ uṟuviḻumam
kaḷaintōḷ - pala nāḷum niṉatu mikkatuyariṉai nīkkiyōḷ, taṉ uṟu viḻumam nīntumōeṉavē -
niṉ pirivāl eytiya taṉatu mikkatuyariṉaik kaṭakka vallaḷō eṉṟu ;
7-8. nīyē collalvēṇṭum - at talaivaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu nīyē kūṟutalvēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) alava! tuṟaivaṉukkuk kāṉaluṅkaḻaṟātu ; kaḻiyuṅ kūṟātu ;puṉṉaiyum
moḻiyātu ; oru niṉṉallatu piṟituyātum ilaṉ; naḷḷeṉ yāmattu vantu pal
nāḷumniṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷaintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumamnīntumō eṉa nīyē collal vēṇṭum.
(vi - rai.) kāṉal, kaḻi, puṉṉai eṉpaṉa cāṉṟākat talaivareṉṉaik kalantu ceṉṟārāyiṉum,
avai yellāmtalaivaṉaip pirintu eytum varuttattai uṇaravallaṉaaṉmaiyāṉum, orukāl
uṇariṉum ceṉṟukūṟamāṭṭātaṉa ākalāṉum nīyē tuṟaivaṉukkuccollalvēṇṭum eṉat talaivi
alavaṉai nōkkikkūṟiṉāḷeṉka. kaṇpōl neytal eṉṟatu, 1'poruḷēuvamam ceytaṉar
moḻiyiṉum' eṉpataṉālamaintatu. tuṟaivaṉai - tuṟaivaṉukku. kākkai peṭaiyoṭuveḷ iṟāk
kaṉavum yāmam eṉka. kōṭṭu mīṉvaḻaṅkum parappu eṉavum, vēṭṭamaṭi parappu eṉavumiyaiyum.
niṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷaintōḷeṉṟamaiyāl nī avaḷatu viḻumattaikkaḷaiyātiruttal naṉṟi kōṟalām
eṉpatupaṭaniṉṟatu. iṉi, paṟaiiya taḷarum eṉpataṉaimuṟṟākkik kāṉal mutaliyaṉa pōlātu vaṇṭu
ceṉṟukūṟutaṟku uriyatēṉum, atu tēṉaiyuṇṭa kaḷippālcōrntu kiṭakkum ātaliṉ nīyē
collalvēṇṭum eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām. kāmam mikkakaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāvatu talaivaṉaip pirintu
varuntumtalaivi, taṉatu vēṭkai mikutiyāl, kēḷātaṉa vaṟṟaikkēṭpaṉavāka viḷittu,
talaivaṉiṭattuc ceṉṟu eṉtuṉpattai moḻiyīrō eṉṟu kūṟutalām. nīntumōeṉṟataṉāl viḻumam
kaṭal eṉṟu kuṟittavāṟām.
(u - ṟai.) tātiṉai uṇṭa vaṇṭiṉam kaḷiciṟantu paṟakkamāṭṭātutaḷarum tuṟaivaṉ eṉṟatu, tāṉ
peṟṟa vēṟuiṉpattil mayaṅkit talaiviyai maṟantirukkumtalaivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
kākkai peṭaiyoṭuyāmattu iṟāk kaṉavum eṉṟatu, talaivi talaivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyiruntu muṉpu
peṟṟa iṉpattai niṉaintu varuntukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē - ḷ.) 2'nōyumiṉpamum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ, 'collāmarapiṉa
colluvaṉavāka aḻukai paṟṟik kūṟiyatu, ivaiuyar tiṇaiyumāyiṉa' eṉṟār.
nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
3'iṉpattaiveṟuttal' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅkaḷaintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumam
nīntumō eṉavē'eṉpatu tuṉpattup pulampal eṉṉum meyppāṭuākumeṉṟum, 4'muṭṭuvayiṟ
kaḻaṟal' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, 'kāṉaluṅkaḻaṟātu....nīyē, collal vēṇṭumāl alava'eṉpatu,
tūtumuṉi viṉmai eṉṉum meyp pāṭuākumeṉṟum kūṟiṉar, pērā.

171. pālai
[talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
6. poruḷ purintuporuḷ īṭṭivaravirumpi, ilaṅku kōl āytoṭinekiḻa - viḷaṅkum
tiraṭci poruntiya aḻakiyaniṉ toṭi kaḻaṉṟu viḻa ;
7-15. alan talaiñemaiyattu atar aṭaintirunta - vāṭiyaucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya ñemai
maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya vaḻiyaiccārntirunta, mālvaraic cīṟūr - periyamalaiyaṭivārattiluḷḷa ciṟiya
ūril, maruḷ palmākkaḷ - maruṇṭa makkaḷāya palar, kōḷval ēṟṟaiōcai ōr mār - kollutal
valla karaṭiiēṟṟiṉoliyiṉai uṇarvārāy, tiruttik koṇṭaampiṉar - ceppam ceytukoṇṭa
ampiṉarāy,nōṉcilai eruttattu irīi - valiya villaittōḷiṟ koṇṭu, iṭamtōṟum paṭartaliṉ
-iṭamelām paravi rutaliṉ, kīḻappaṭu tārama uṇṇā -taraiyiṟ kiṭakkum uṇaviṉai uṇṇāvāy,
mēlciṉai paḻampōl cēṟṟa tīm puḻal uṇīiya -mēliṭattē kiḷaikaḷiluḷḷa paḻaṅkaḷaip
pōlaiṉikkum cāṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉiya tuḷaiyaiyuṭaiyapūkkaḷai uṇṇa, karumkōṭṭu iruppai
ūrum – kariya kompiṉaiyuṭaiya iruppaimarattiṉ mēl ēṟum, perum kai eṇkiṉ
curamiṟantōr - periya kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya karaṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya curaneṟiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟār.
5. puṇarvar -viraivil varuvar ātalāl;
1-5. nutalum nuṇpacappu ivarum - neṟṟiyum nuṇṇiyapacalaiparakkappeṟum, tōḷum akal
malai iṟumpiṉāyntu koṇṭu aṟutta paṇai eḻil aḻiya vāṭum -tōḷum akaṉṟa malaiyiluḷḷa
kāṭṭilārāyntukoṇṭu aṟutta mūṅkil tuṇṭai yottaaḻaku oḻiya vāṭṭamuṟum, nāḷum
niṉaival avar paṇpueṉṟu - nāṭōṟum avar aruṭpaṇpiṉai yāṉniṉaivēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi, ōvātu
iṉaiyal - oḻiyātuvaruntātēkoḷ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! poruḷ purintu, toṭi nekiḻaccuraṉ iṟantōr puṇarvar;
ātaliṉ, nutalum pacappuivarum, tōḷum vāṭum, nāḷum niṉaival eṉaiṉaiyal.
mākkaḷōcaiyōrmār cilai irī ip paṭartaliṉ, eṇkukīḻp paṭutāram uṇṇā, puḻal uṇī
iyairuppaiyūrum curam eṉka.
(vi - rai.) niṉaival mātu : mātu, acai. alaintalai : vikāram -ñemaiyattu : attu,
cāriyai. maruḷ - karaṭiyiṉ ōcaiyaimutalil iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyātu maruṇṭa eṉka. tāram -paṇṭam
(uṇavup poruḷ.) puḻal, āku peyar. kaṭiyummarattiṉ kōṭṭil iyaṅka

172. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi talaimakaḷai iṭattuuyttuvantu talaimakaṉai varaivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. vāraṇam uraṟum nīrtikaḻ cilampil - yāṉaimuḻaṅkum taṉmai vāynta pakka
malaiyil,piracamoṭu virai iya vayaṅku veḷ aruvi - tēṉuṭaṉkalantu varumviḷakkamuṟṟa
veḷḷiya aruvi, iṉicaiimiḻ iyam kaṭuppa - iṉṉoliyuṭaṉ muḻaṅkummattaḷattai yoppa, im
eṉa kalmukai viṭar akamcilampa vīḻum - immeṉum oliyuṭaṉ malaiyiṉkukaikaḷum
piḷappiṭaṅkaḷum olikka viḻum, kāmputalaimaṇanta ōṅku malaic cāral - mūṅkilneruṅkiya
uyarnta malaiccāralil uḷḷa ;
5-14. irumpuvaṭittaṉṉa karuṅkaik kāṉavaṉ - irumpiṉaivārttuc ceytāṟ pōṉṟavaliya
kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyavēṭṭuvaṉ, viri malar marāam porunti -virinta malariṉaiyuṭaiya
veṇkaṭampiṉaic cārntuniṉṟu, kōl terintu - ampiṉai āyntukoṇṭuvarinutal yāṉai aru
niṟattu aḻutti - variporuntiya neṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ ariyamārpiṟ celutti, ikal
aṭu muṉpiṉ veṇkōṭukoṇṭu - pakaiyiṉaik kollum valiyiṉaiyuṭaiyaataṉ veḷḷiya
kompiṉaik koṇṭuvantu, taṉpulvēy kurampai pulara ūṉṟi - taṉatu ūkam pullālvēynta
kuṭicaiyil pulāl nāṟṟam vīca ūṉṟutalceytu, muṉṟil nīṭiya muḻavu uṟaḻ palaviṉ
piḻimakiḻ uvakaiyaṉ - ak kuṭicai muṉṟilil uyarntamuḻavu pōlum palāk kaṉiyiṉiṉṟu
piḻanteṭuttamatuviṉai uṇṭa kaḷippiṉaṉāki, kiḷaiyoṭu kaliciṟantu - cuṟṟattuṭaṉ
āravārammikku, cāntañekiḻiyiṉ uṉpuḻukku ayarum- cantaṉaviṟakālāyatīyil ūṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya
cōṟṟai aṭṭuṇṇum iṭamākiya,kuṉṟanāṭa - kuṉṟam poruntiya nāṭṭaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!
14-18. nī aṉpilaiākutal aṟiyēṉ yāṉ - nī aṉpilāy ātalai yāṉmuṉpu aṟintilēṉ,
aḵtu aṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ - ataṉai yāṉ aṟintuḷēṉāyiṉ, aṇiiḻai āy itaḻ uṇkaṇ
kuṟumakaḷ - aḻakiyaaṇikalaṉaiyum aḻakiya itaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya maiyuṇṭakaṇkaḷaiyum uṭaiya iḷaiyaḷāya
eṉ talaiviyiṉ, maṇiēr māṇ nalam citaiya - nīlamaṇi pōlum ciṟantaaḻakukeṭa, poṉ nēr
pacalai pāiṉṟu maṉ - poṉṉaiotta pacalai peritum parattal illaiyākum.
(muṭipu) kuṉṟa nāṭa! nī aṉpilai yākutal aṟiyēṉ : aḵtuaṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ kuṟumakaḷ
māṇalam citaiya pacalaimaṉpā iṉṟu. aruvi immeṉa vīḻum cāralilkāṉavaṉ yāṉaikkōṭu koṇṭu
kurampai ūṉṟiccānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻukku ayarum kuṉṟameṉka.
(vi - rai.) nīr - cuṉai nīrumām. mukaiyākiya viyareṉṟalumām. aruvi vīḻum malaiccāral
eṉavum,kāmpu talaimaṇanta malaic cāral eṉavum taṉittaṉikūṭṭuka. yāṉaiyaik koṉṟu ataṉ
kōṭu koḷḷumvaliya kai yeṉpatu tōṉṟa irumpu vaṭittaṉṉa karuṅkaieṉappaṭṭatu. marāam
poruntutal, yāṉai taṉiruppaiyaṟintu iriyātavāṟu patuṅki niṟṟal.iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam
añcit talaivivaruntutalaiyum, ūril alar eḻutalaiyum karutiviraintu varaintu koḷḷātu
kaḷavoḻukkattilēyētāḻntiruttalpaṟṟit talaivaṉai 'aṉpilai'eṉṟum, nī ivvāṟu oḻukuti
eṉpataṉai muṉṉaraṟintiruppēṉāyiṉ, yāṉ kūṭṭattiṟkuuṭaṉpaṭṭirē ṉākaliṉ, varaivu
nīṭṭittalkāraṇamākat talaivi nalaṉcitaiyap pacalai paṭartalillaiyākum eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ
eṉka.
(u - ṟai.) piracamoṭu viraiiya aruvi viṭarakam cilampa vīḻumeṉṟatu, talaiviyuṭaṉ kūṭiya
talaivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkamūreṅkum alaruṇṭāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.yāṉai veṇkōṭu koṇṭu
kāṉavaṉ kurampai pularaūṉṟi uvakaiyaṉ kaliciṟantu cānta ñekiḻiyiṉūṉpuḻuk kayarum
eṉṟatu, talaivaṉ ariyaḷāya talaiviyaieḷiyaḷ eṉa matittuk kaḷavoḻukkamākiyaiṉpattilē
tarukkiyiruttal eṉṟavāṟu.

173. pālai
[talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 8, tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. aṟamtalaippiriyātu oḻukalum - aṟaneṟiyiṉiṉṟumnīṅkātu ilvāḻkkai naṭattalum,
ciṟantakēḷir kēṭu pala ūṉṟalum - ciṟappuṟṟacuṟṟattāratu palavakaiyāṉa tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum
tāṅkalum,nāḷum varuntā uḷḷamoṭu iruntōrkku il eṉa -eññāṉṟum (muyaṟciyāl) mey
varuntāmaikkuētuvāya (ūkkamillāta) uḷḷamoṭuiruntōrkkum illaiyākum eṉṟu,
ceyviṉaipurinta neñciṉar - poruḷ īṭṭum viṉaiyaivirumpiya neñciṉarāya nan talaivar;
8-18. palapuri vārkayiṟṟu oḻukai - pala purikaḷālāya nīṇṭa kayiṟṟāṟpiṇitta vaṇṭiyiṉai,
pakaṭu nōṉ cuval koḷīi -pakaṭukaḷiṉ valiya piṭariyil pūṭṭi, tuṟai ēṟṟattu -ēṟṟamāṉa
tuṟaikaḷil ōṭṭumiṭattu, umaṇ viḷiverīi - uppu vāṇikar avaṟṟai urappum ōcaiyaikkēṭṭu
añci, uḻaimāṉ ampiṇai iṉaṉ irintu ōṭa- āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum ākiya iṉaṅkaḷ
keṭṭuōṭavum, kāṭu kaviṉ aḻiya - kāṭukaḷ aḻaku keṭavum,kōṭai uraii - kōṭai paravi,
niṉṟu tiṉa viḷintaam paṇai neṭuvēy - nilaipeṟṟu nīriṉaiuṟiñcutalāl vaṟṟiya aḻakiya
periya neṭiyamūṅkiliṉ, kaṇviṭat teṟikkum maṇṇāmuttam -kaṇukkaḷ piḷakkatteṟittu
viḻum kaḻuvappeṟātamuttukkaḷ, kaḻaṅku uṟaḻ tōṉṟala. - kaḻaṅkiṉai yotta
tōṟṟamuṭaiyaṉavāki, paḻaṅkuḻi tāam - (aṅkuḷḷārkaḻaṅkāṭiya) paḻaiya kuḻiyilē viḻum
(iṭamākiya), iṉkaḷi naṟaviṉ iyaltēr naṉṉaṉ - iṉiyakaḷippaittarum kaḷḷiṉaiyum
iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēriṉaiyumuṭaiya naṉṉaṉatu, viṇporu neṭuvaraikkavāṉ - vāṉai aḷāviya nīṇṭa
mūṅkilaiyuṭaiya pakkamalaiyiṉaiyum, poṉpaṭu maruṅkiṉ malai iṟantōr- poṉ poruntiya
iṭaṅkaḷaiyumuṭaiya malaiyiṉaikkaṭantu ceṉṟōrāya nam talaivar;
4-8. naṟunutal mai īrōti - naṟiya neṟṟiyaiyum kariya periya kūntalaiyumuṭaiyāḷē,
arumpaṭar uḻattal - ariya tuṉpattālvaruntutalai, cilnāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu
-cilakālam poṟuttuk kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟi,niṉ nal māṇ elvaḷai tiruttiṉarāyiṉ -
niṉatunalla māṇpuṟṟa oḷi poruntiya vaḷaiyiṉaittiruttit talaiyaḷi ceytaṉarātalāl, varuvar
-viraivil vantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! ceyviṉai purinta neñciṉarāy,malaiyiṟantōrākiya nam talaivar,
arumpaṭaruḻattal ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉelvaḷai tiruttiṉarātaliṉ, (viraivil)
varuvar.
(vi - rai.) naṟunutal, ōti eṉpavaṟṟiṟku, nutalai nīvutalumōtiyaik kōtutalum ceytaṉar
eṉak kuṟippiṟporuḷ koḷḷalumām; 1'valimuṉpiṉ' eṉṟa pālaikkaliyil, 'tāḻ
katuppuaṇikuvar kātalar 'oḷḷiḻai tiruttuvar kātalar,oṇṇutal nīvuvar kātalar' eṉa varutal
kāṇka.tiruttiṉarāyiṉ - tiruttiṉarātaliṉ eṉka.ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi
iṅṅaṉamtalaiyaḷi ceytu akaṉṟārātaliṉ, avar niṉṉainiṉaintu viraivil vārā
tiruppārallar, nīvaruntātēkoḷ eṉṟu, talaiviyait tōḻivaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷ eṉka. umaṇ -
umaṇar; ākupeyar.kōṭaitiṉa eṉpatu, 2'uṇṭaṟkuriyavallāpporuḷai, yuṇṭaṉapōlak kūṟalu
marayē'eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ummaiyāl amaikkappeṟum.muttam tāam kavāṉ eṉa iyaiyum.
kaḻaṅku uṟaḻtōṉṟala eṉṟatu muttiṉ parumai kuṟittapaṭi.

174. mullai
[pācaṟaikkaṇ talaimakaṉtaṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. iru peru vēntar māṟu koḷ viyaṉkaḷattu -pēraracar iruvar māṟupāṭu
koṇṭu porum periyapōrkkaḷattē, oru paṭai koṇṭu varupaṭaipeyarkkum - tamatu
oppaṟṟapaṭaikkalattaikkoṇṭu etir varum paṭaikaḷaippiṟakkiṭac ceyyum, celvam
uṭaiyōrkkuviṟal niṉṟaṉṟu eṉa - veṟṟiyākiya celvamuṭaiyōrkku ipperumai nilaipeṟṟatu
eṉṟu kūṟi,pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumai vilakki - taṇṇumaimuḻakkuṭaṉ porunta aracaṉ
aḷikkumpūkkōḷiṉaiyum ēlātu tavirttu, celvēm ātalaṟiyāḷ - nām taṉpāl viraintu
cellutalaiaṟiyāḷāy;
10-14. vēṅkai ūḻ uṟunaṟuvī kaṭuppak kēḻ koḷa - vēṅkaiyiṉmalartaluṟṟa naṟiya malarai oppa
naṉṉiṟam koḷḷa,ākattu arumpiya mācu aṟu cuṇaṅkiṉaḷ - mēṉiyiṟṟōṉṟiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa
tēmalaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, nal maṇalviyaliṭai naṭanta - nalla maṇalaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟaviṭattēnaṭanta cilmel
otukkiṉ māayōḷ - cilavāyamelliya naṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya māmai niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya namtalaivi ;
5-10. nēr kālmullai mutukoṭi kuḻaippa - nirampiya kālkaḷiṟpaṭarnta mutiya
mullaikkoṭi taḷirkka, kālaivāṉattuk kaṭuṅkural koṇmū - kīḻtticaiyilvāṉatteḻunta
kaṭiya kuralaiyuṭaiya mēkam, nīrcorintu muḻaṅkutoṟum - nīraip peytu muḻaṅkuntōṟum,
nappulantu kaiyaṟṟu oṭuṅki - nammaiveṟuttuc ceyalaṟṟu oṭuṅki, paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa
pacalaimēṉiyaḷ - tuṉpiṉai eytiya pacalai pāyntamēṉiyaḷāy, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kāl -
ennilaiyiṉaiaṭaivāḷō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! nām pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumai vilakkiccelvēmātalai aṟiyāḷāya māyōḷ,
koṇmū nīrcorintu muḻaṅku toṟum kaiyaṟṟu oṭuṅkinammaip pulantu pacalai mēṉiyaḷāki
yāṅkākuvaḷkollō!
(vi - rai.) varupaṭai peyarkkum celvam eṉṟamaiyālcelvameṉpatu veṟṟiyai uṇarttiṟṟu.
viṟal -mēṉmai. māṟṟārai veṉṟa veṟṟivīrarkkuaracaṉ poṟpū aḷittuc ciṟappuc
ceyvaṉ;appūviṉaip peṟutal pūkkōḷākum. taṇṇumai ēyapūkkōḷ eṉa māṟuka. pakaiyai veṉṟu
aracaṉaḷikkum ciṟappiṉaip peṟutaṟkum tāḻttunillātu nām celvēmāyiṉum kuṟitta
paruvamvantamaiyiṉ talaivi eṅṅaṉam ākuvaḷō eṉṟutalaivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc collic
celavuvituppuṟukiṉṟāṉ eṉka. kālai vāṉam - ñāyiṟutōṉṟum kīḻtticai vāṉam eṉka. tāṉ
avaḷaippiriyum ñāṉṟu maṇaṟparappilē avaḷ kuṟukaaṭiyiṭṭu naṭanta meṉṉaṭai avaṉ
neñcamviṭṭakaṉṟila tākaliṉ, 'naṉmaṇal viyaliṭainaṭanta, ciṉmel lotukkiṉ māayōḷ'eṉṟāṉ.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'ēvaṉmarapiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷmīḷvāṉ taṉ neñciṟku uraittatu
eṉṟum,itaṉuḷ pūkkōḷēya taṇṇumai vilakkic celvēmeṉṟaliṉ, aracaṉāṟ ciṟappup peṟṟa
-talaivaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟum, 2'oṉṟāttamariṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ākit tōṉṟumpāṅkōr
pāṅkiṉum eṉṉun tuṟaikku, tamakkuākkam ciṟanta naṭpuṭaiyōrākit tōṉṟumnaṭpuṭaiyōrkku
uṟṟuḻi yutavac cēṟaṟkaṇṇumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, ic ceyyuḷ ataṟkuutāraṇamākum eṉṟum
kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

175. pālai
[piriviṉkaṇvaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷ
tōḻikkuccoṟṟatū umām.]

(co - ḷ.) 9. tōḻi-,
1-8. vīṅku viḷimpuurīiya - tamatu taṭitta tōḷiṉ viḷimpiṉai uraciya,vicai amai nōṉ
cilai - vēkam amainta valiyavillil vaittu, vāṅku toṭai piḻaiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar - iḻuttu
viṭum ampu kuṟi tapputal illātataṟukaṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya maṟavar, viṭutoṟum
viḷikkumvevvāy vāḷi - eyyuntōṟum olittuccellum koṭiya vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya ampu,
āṟu celvampalar uyir celap peyarppiṉ -vaḻiccellum putiyaratu uyirkeṭappōkkutalāl,
pāṟu kiḷai payirntu paṭumuṭai kavarum -paruntu taṉ kiḷaiyiṉai aḻaittu mikka
pulālaiuṇṇum, vem curam iṟanta kātalar - koṭiya curaneṟiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam kātalar,
neñcu uṇara -tamatu neñcu uṇara, ariya vañciṉam colliyum -kaṭiya cūḷ uraittum, palmāṇ
terivaḷai muṉkaipaṟṟiyum - paṉmuṟai ārāynta vaḷaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉatumuṉ kaiyiṉaip paṟṟit
talaiyaḷi ceytum ;
10-18. taṇ peyaleḻili - taṇṇiya maḻaiyaip peyyum mēkam, kāliyal neṭu tēr kaivaṇ
ceḻiyaṉ - kāṟṟuppōlaiyalum neṭiya tēriṉaiyum kaivaṇmaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiyapāṇṭiyaṉ
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, ālaṅkāṉattu amarkaṭantu uyartta - talaiyālaṅkāṉattup pōraiveṉṟu uyartta,
vēliṉum pal ūḻ miṉṉi -vēṟpaṭaikaḷaik kāṭṭil paṉmuṟai miṉṉi, muracu eṉamāiru
vicumpil kaṭiiṭi payiṟṟi - kariyaperiya vāṉiṉkaṇ (avaṉatu veṉṟu eṟi) muracam
eṉakkaṭiya iṭiyiṉaip palakālum tōṟṟu vittu, nērkatir niraitta nēmi am celvaṉ -
nirampiyakatirkaḷiṉ oḻuṅkiṉaik koṇṭa āḻiyaiyuṭaiyatirumāliṉ, pōr aṭaṅku akalam
poruntiyatārpōl - pakaivar pōr oḻitaṟkuk kāraṇamāyamārpiṉiṭattē taṅkiya mālaipōla,
urukeḻutiruvil tēettuk kulaii - paṉṉiṟam vāyntaaḻakiya villai avvicumpiṉiṭattē
vaḷaittu, maṇpayam pūppap pāay - nilam payaṉaittarap paravi,tāḻnta pōḻtu - iṟaṅkip
peyyuṅ kālattu;
8-9. viṉai muṭittuvarutum eṉṟaṉar aṉṟē - tāmcellum viṉaiyai muṭittukkoṇṭu
mīṇṭum varuvēm eṉṟāraṉṟō?(aṅṅaṉam vantilarē ; eṉ ceyval!)
(muṭipu) veñcuramiṟanta kātalar, vañciṉam colliyum, muṉkaipaṟṟiyum ; taṇpeyal eḻili
tāḻnta pōḻtuvarutum eṉṟaṉarē? (aṅṅaṉam vantilarē; eṉceyval!)
(vi - rai.) viḷimpu - tōliṉ mēṟpuṟam, peyarppiṉ -peyarttalāl. avar kūṟiya
vañciṉattiṟku avarneñcē cāṉṟāka amaiyum eṉpāḷ, 'neñcuṇara'eṉṟāḷ;
1'neñcattukkuṟukiyakariyillai eṉap piṟacāṉṟōr kūṟutalum kāṇka.muṉkai paṟṟi yeṉpataṉai
vēṟu pirittu vañciṉamcolli eṉpataṉōṭu kūṭṭi yuraittalumām. muṉkaipaṟṟi vañciṉam
kūṟum vaḻakkuṇmai. 2'nēriṟaimuṉkai paṟṟic, cūrara makaḷiro ṭuṟṟa cūḷē'eṉpataṉāl
aṟiyappaṭum. kariya vāṉukku nēmiyañcelvaṉatu akalamum, avvāṉiṟṟōṉṟiya
villukkuavvakalattiṟ poruntiya tārum uvamam. tēem -iṭam, vāṉiṉ iṭam kārpparuvat
toṭakkattuvarutum eṉṟavar vārāmaiyāl, yāṉ eṉceyvēṉeṉat tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ kūṟiṉāḷ.

176. marutam
[tōḻi, talaimakaṉai vāyiṉmaṟuttatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-12. kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - kaṭalaikkaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa iṭamakaṉṟa
nīrpparappil,nilampaka vīḻnta vērmutir kiḻaṅkiṉ- nilampiḷakkumāṟu iṟaṅkiya vēril
mutirntakiḻaṅkiṉaiyum, kaḻai kaṇṭaṉṉa tūmpu uṭai tiraḷkāl - mūṅkilaik kaṇṭālotta
tuḷaiyuṭaiya tiraṇṭataṇṭiṉaiyum, kaḷiṟṟuc cevi aṉṉa pācaṭaimaruṅkil - kaḷiṟṟiṉ
cevipōṉṟa paciyailaiyiṭattē, kaḻu nivantaṉṉa koḻumukai iṭaiiṭai - kaḻuuyarntu tōṉṟalai
yotta ceḻittamoṭṭukkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē, muṟuval mukattiltayaṅkap pūtta paṉmalar -
puṉcirippiṉaiyuṭaiyamukam pōla viḷaṅkap pūtta pala malarkaḷaiyumuṭaiya,tāmarai puḷ imiḻ
paḻaṉattu - tāmaraiyiṉaiyuṭaiyapuṭkaḷ olikkum vayalil uḷḷa, vēppu naṉai aṉṉaneṭukaṇ nīr
ñeṇṭu - vēmpiṉ arumpiṉaiyotta nīṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya nīr ñeṇṭāṉatu(parattamaikkup
pirintu pōyatu.) irai tēr veṇkuruku añci - iraiyiṉai āyntu pārttiruntaveḷḷiya
nāraiyiṉaik kaṇṭu añci, ayalatu olittapakaṉṟai iru cēṟṟu aḷḷal - ayaliluḷḷa
taḻaittacivataiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kariya cēṟṟup piḻampil,titalaiyiṉ varippa ōṭi - tēmalpōla
variyuṇṭākaōṭicceṉṟu, viraintu taṉ īr mali maṇ aḷaicceṟiyum ūra - viraintu taṉatu
īram mikka maṇaḷaiyuṭ patuṅkum ūraṉē!
13-8. maṉainaku maraṉivar vayalai koḻuṅkoṭi - maṉaiyiṉ kaṇ viḷaṅkummarattiṟ paṭarum
vayalaiyākiya koḻuviya koṭiyiṉai,arimalar āmpaloṭu ārtaḻai taii - viḷaṅkummalarkaḷaiyuṭaiya
āmpaloṭu cērttuk kaṭṭiyataḻaiyuṭaiyai uṭuttu, viḻuvu āṭu makaḷiroṭu
taḻūuaṇipolintu - viḻāviṉ kaṇ āṭum makaḷiroṭu taḻuviāṭum aṇiyāṟ polivuṟṟu,
malar ēr uṇkaṇmāṇ iḻai kuṟuntoṭi muṉkai tuṭakkiya - malaraiyotta maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaiyum
māṇpuṟṟaaṇiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya niṉ parattai taṉatu kuṟiyavaḷaiyaṇinta muṉkaiyiṉāl paṇitta,
neṭu toṭarviṭuttatu - neṭiya piṇippiṉai viṭuttuc ceṉṟaaḷaviṟkē, uṭaṉṟaṉaḷ -
vekuṇṭaṉaḷāy;
21-6. taṉ mukattu eḻutueḻil citaiya - taṉ mukattiṉatu eḻutuvataṟku ētuvākiyaaḻaku
keṭa, aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki - aḻutu ēkkamuṟṟu, vaṭittueṉa urutta titti - poṉṉai urukki
vārttāloppa urukkoṇṭa tēmalaiyum, pal ūḻnoṭitteṉa civanta melviral -
paṉmuṟaimuṟittuk koḷḷaliṉ civanta melliyaviraliṉaiyum, tirukupu kūrnuṉai maḻukiya
eyiṟṟaḷ -tirukik kaṭittalāṉ kūriya muṉai maḻuṅkiyaeyiṟṟaiyumuṭaiyaḷāy, ūr muḻutum
nuvalum niṉkāṇiya ceṉmē - ūr eṅkum alar kūṟappaṭumniṉṉaik kāṇṭaṟkut tēṭic
celvāḷ (ākaliṉ);
18-21. niṉ kātali -niṉ kātaliyākiya avaḷ, empōl pul uḷaikkuṭumip putalvaṉ
payantu - emmaippōlap pulliyauḷai pōlum kuṭumiyaiyuṭaiya putalvaṉaippeṟṟu, neluṭai
neṭunakar niṉ iṉṟu uṟaiya - nelluṭaiya nīṇṭamaṉaiyil niṉṉaip pirintu taṅka
eṉṉakaṭattaḷō - eṉṉa kaṭappāṭu uṭaiyavaḷō?
(muṭipu) ūra! māṇiḻai taṉ muṉkai toṭakkiya neṭuntoṭarviṭuttatu uṭaṉṟaṉaḷāy aḻutaṉaḷ
ēṅki, ūr muḻutumnuvalum niṉṉaikkāṇiya ceṉmē ākaliṉ niṉkātaliyāya avaḷ empōṟ
putalvaṟ payantuneṭunakar niṉiṉṟu uṟaiya eṉṉa kaṭattaḷō?
(vi - rai.) kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa eṉpataṉālum, tāmaraiyiṉ kiḻaṅku,taṇṭu, ilai, mukai, malar eṉṟa
ivaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟiyauvamaikaḷālum marutanilattiṉ koḻumaiyum nīrmikutiyum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
neṭuṅkaṇ -neṭitāy nivantu tōṉṟum kaṇ. vayalaik koṭimaṉaiyiṉkaṇ vaittu
vaḷarkkappaṭutalāl, maṉainakuvayalai eṉappaṭṭatu. uṇ kaṇṇiṉaiyummāṇiḻaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya niṉ
kātaliyeṉakkūṭṭiyuraittalu mām. pōlum : oppil pōli.maṟṟu, viṉaimāṟṟu. eḻutu
eḻil - ōviyar pārttueḻututaṟku ētuvākiya aḻaku. viralkaḷaip palamuṟainoṭittalum,
eyiṟṟiṉait tirukutalumciṉattiṉāl nikaḻntaṉa. avaḷkaippiṇippiṉaic ciṟitu viṭutta
aḷaviṟkēuṭaṉṟu niṉṉait tēṭit tirivāḷ eṉṟa maiyiṉ'avaḷatu poṟumaiyiṉmaiyaiyum. yām
maṉaiyiṉkaṇniṉṉai yiṉṟiyē uṟaikkiṉṟōm eṉṟataṉāl,tamatu poṟumaiyaiyum talaivi
pulappaṭuttiṉāḷ eṉka.nelluṭai neṭunakar eṉa viruntōmpaṟkuiṉṟiyamaiyāta neluluṭaimai
kūṟiya ataṉāṉē, yāmputalvaṟ payantum viruntōmpiyumniṉakkumirumaikkum ētuvākiya
maṉaiyaṟattiṉ vaḻuvātoḻukukiṉṟēm eṉṟu talaivi kuṟittāḷām,putalvaṟ payantu eṉṟatu,
mutumaiyai uḷḷip parattaiyiṟ pirintāṉ eṉappulappaṭuttapaṭiyumām. ūr muḻutum nuvalum
eṉṟamaiyāl, piṟar kūṟum paḻi karuti ippoḻutuīṇṭu varukiṉṟāyaṉṟi aṉpiṉāl
varukiṉṟāyallai eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi vāyil maṟuttāḷām. ceṉmē - cellum;
īṟṟumicaiyukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭatu.
talaivikkum taṉakkum uḷḷa orumaippāṭṭiṉāl tōḻi,empōl eṉat talaiviyaik kūṟiṉāḷ.
itaṉaittalaivi kūṟṟākavē koḷvatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum pērāciriyarkkum karuttātal
tolkāppiya uraiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
(u - ṟai.) puṉimiḻ paḻaṉattu ñeṇṭu kurukañci iruñcēṟṟaḷḷal titalaiyiṉ varippa ōṭi
maṇṇaḷaicceṟiyum eṉṟatu, parattaiyar cērikkaṇ avarnalattiṉai nukarntirunta talaivaṉ alar
eḻutalaiañci apparattaiyar ceyta kuṟi, taṉ mārpilē kiṭappattaṉ maṉaikkaṇ viraintu
varalāyiṉaṉ eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'avaṉaṟivāṟṟa aṟiyu mākaliṉ' eṉṉum cūttirattu, 'pukaṉṟa
uḷḷamoṭu....īrattu maruṅkiṉum' eṉṉumtuṟaikkaṇ, ic ceyyuḷai utāraṇamākak
kāṭṭi,maṟuppāḷpōla nayantāḷāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
2'iḷivēyiḻavē...aḻukai nāṉkē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattueḻuteḻil citaiya aḻutaṉaḷ
ēṅki...niṟkāṇiyaceṉmē' eṉpatu, taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya iḷivaralporuḷāka avalaccuvai piṟantatu
eṉṟum, 3'teyvamañcal' eṉṉum cūttirattu, 'vēmpu naṉaiyaṉṉa... ceṟiyum ūra'
eṉpatu,talaimakaṉ vāyil vēṇṭac ceṉṟāṉaip piṟarkūṟum paḻikku vantāy eṉṟamaiyiṉ, iḵtu
uḷḷatuvarttal (eṉum meyppāṭu) āyiṟṟu eṉṟumkūṟiṉar, pērā.

177. pālai
[piriviṭaivēṟupaṭṭatalaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6-12. tōḻi-, kāṇpu iṉ kaḻai amalcilam piṉ vaḻaitalaivāṭa - kāṇṭaṟku iṉiya
mūṅkil niṟaintapakkamalaiyiluḷḷa curapuṉṉaiyiṉ ucci vāṭumpaṭi,katir katam kaṟṟa -
ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirciṉattaip payiṉṟa, ē kal neṟiyiṭai – perukiya kaṟkaḷ poruntiya
curaneṟiyil,paiṅkoṭip pākal ceṅkaṉi nacaii – paciya pākaṟkoṭiyiṉ civanta paḻattiṉai
virumpi,kāṉamaññai kamam cūl mā peṭai - kāṭṭiluḷḷamayiliṉ niṟainta karuviṉaik koṇṭa
kariya peṭai,ayiri yāṟṟu aṭaikarai vayiriṉ naralum,ayiriyāṟṟiṉatu aṭaikaraiyiṉkaṇ
ūtukompeṉaolikkum-kāṭu iṟantu akaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum- kāṭṭiṉait tāṇṭic ceṉṟa
talaivar (kuṟittaparuvaṅ kaṭantu) tāḻttu vārātiruppiṉum;
3-6. ciṟukaṇ iru piṭitaṭakai māṉa - ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya periyapeṇyāṉaiyiṉ periya
kaiyiṉai oppa, oruṅkupiṇittu iyaṉṟa ney aruntu veṟikoḷ aimpāl- cērakkaṭṭi viṭṭa
ney pūcappeṟumnaṟunāṟṟamuṭaiya kūntaṟkaṇṇē, tēm kamaḻveṟimalar peymmār - tēṉ maṇakkum
naṟiyamalaraip peytalaiyum;
13-20. veṇvēlilainiṟam peyara ōcci - veṉṟi vēlai ilaimuṉainiṟam māṟic
cenniṟamaṭaiyac celutti,māṟṟōr malai maruḷ yāṉai maṇṭu amaroḻitta -pakaivaruṭaiya
malaipōlumyāṉaikaḷai mikkapōriṉkaṇ aḻitta, kaḻalkāl paṇṇaṉ -vīrakkaḻal taritta kāliṉaiyuṭaiya
paṇṇaṉatu, kāvirivaṭavayiṉ - kāviriyiṉ vaṭapāluḷḷa, niḻal kayam taḻīiyaneṭukāl māviṉ -
kuḷirnta kuḷattiṉai yaṭuttuḷḷaneṭiya aṭimarattiṉaiyuṭaiya māmarattiṉ, taḷir ērākam
takaipeṟa mukainta- taḷirai yotta ākattilaḻakupeṟa arumpiya, aṇaṅku uṭai niṉ vaṉamulai
tāya -varuttum iyalpuṭaiya niṉ aḻakiya mulaiyiṟ paranta,cuṇaṅkiṭai toyyilai varittal
niṉaintē -cuṇaṅkiṉiṭaiyē toyyil eḻututalaiyum niṉaintu;
vallē varuvar -viraintu vantaruḷvar ātaliṉ;
1-3. tolnalamcitaiyac cāay - paḻaiya aḻaku citaintiṭa varunti,alkalum - nāṭōṟum,
iṉṉum vārār - iṉṉumvantilar, iṉi evaṉ ceyku eṉa - iṉi eṉṉaceyvēṉ eṉṟu, perum
pulampu uṟutal ōmpumati -mikka varuttam aṭaitalaip parikarippāyāka:
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṭiṟantakaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum,niṉatu aimpāl veṟimalar peytalaiyum,
niṉvaṉamulait tāaya cuṇaṅkiṭait toyyileḻututalaiyum niṉaintu vallēvaruvar: ātaliṉ,
nalamcitaiyac cāay, alkalum perum pulampu uṟutalōmpumati.
(vi - rai.) taṭakkai māṉap piṇittiyaṉṟa aimpāl eṉka,arunti eṉpatu aruntu eṉat
tirintateṉakkoṇṭu, arunti veṟikoḷ eṉṟalumām. katamkaṟṟa - vemmai muṟukiya eṉṟapaṭi.
e kal neṟi: ē -perukka mātalai, 1'ēpeṟṟākum' eṉpataṉ uraiyāl aṟika; ēkal neṟi
-uyarcciyaiyuṭaiya neṟiyeṉṟumām. 1'ēkalveṟpaṉ' eṉpatu kāṇka. peṭai vayiriṉ
naraluṅkāṭu eṉka. ayiriyāṟu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉvaṭakkaṇṇuḷḷatōr yāṟu eṉpatu
2'vaṭukarperumakaṉ, pēricai yerumai naṉṉāṭṭuḷḷatai,ayiriyā ṟiṟantaṉar āyiṉum'
eṉpataṉālaṟiyappaṭum. pōlum: oppil pōli, malaimaruḷ:maruḷ, uvamaccol. kāviri
vaṭavayiṉ ciṟukuṭieṉṉum ūriluḷḷa māviṉ taḷir eṉaviritturaikka. paṇṇaṉatu ūr
ciṟukuṭi eṉpatu, 3'taṉakkeṉavāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ, paṇṇaṉ ciṟukuṭippaṭappai'
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.

178. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi varaivu malintucolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16-, tōḻi-,
1-13. vayirattu aṉṉavai ēntu maruppiṉ - vayirattai otta kūriyamēṉōkkiya kōṭṭiṉaiyum,
vetirvēr aṉṉaparūu mayirppaṉṟi - mūṅkil vēriṉai ottaparutta mayiriṉaiyumuṭaiya
paṉṟiyāṉatu, paṟaikkaṇaṉṉa niṟaic cuṉai paruki - paṟaiyiṉ kaṇṇiṉai ottaniṟainta cuṉai
nīraik kuṭittu, nīlattu aṉṉa akalilaic cēmpiṉ - nīlamaṇiyai yottaniṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟa
ilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya cēmpiṉ,piṇṭam aṉṉa koḻu kiḻaṅku mānti - piṇṭittuvaittāṟpōṉṟa
vaḷaviya kiḻaṅkiṉai niṟaiyattiṉṟu, piṭi maṭintaṉṉa kalmicai - peṇyāṉaikiṭantālotta
kalliṉmītu, ūḻ iḻipu -varuttamiṉṟi muṟaiyāka iṟaṅkivantu, yāṟucērntaṉṉa ūṟu nīr paṭā
ar – yāṟṟiṉai aṭuttiruppatu pōṉṟa nīr ūṟum iṭattiluḷḷaciṟutūṟākiya, paimputal
nāḷiciṉai kuruku iruntaṉṉa -paciya putariluḷḷa ceṟinta kiḷaikaḷil veṇṇārai
yiruntālotta, vaṇpiṇi aviḻntaveṇ kūtāḷattu alaṅku kulai alari tīṇṭi -
veḷḷiyakūtaḷañ ceṭiyiṉ acaiyum kottiluḷḷavaḷamporuntiya mukai virinta
malariṉaipporunti, tātu uka - ataṉ pūmpoṭi mēlēutirtalāl, poṉ urai kaṭṭaḷai kaṭuppak
kāṇvara- poṉ uraittu māṟṟu aṟiyum kaṭṭaḷaikkalliṉai oppa aḻakupeṟat tōṉṟi, kiḷai
amalciṟutiṉai viḷaikural mēyntu - kiḷaittal mikkaciṟiya tiṉaiyiṉ viḷainta katiriṉai
mēyntu, kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum nalmalai nāṭaṉoṭu - iṉitu kaṇṇuṟaṅkum iṭamākiya nalla
malaināṭṭaiyuṭaiyāṉoṭu;
14-6. uṇarntaṉaipuṇarnta nīyum - ūṭip piṉ avvūṭalai nīṅkippuṇarntiṭum nīyum, niṉ
tōḷ paṇaikkaviṉaḻiyātu - niṉatu tōḷiṉ mūṅkil pōlum aḻakuoḻiyātu, eṉṟum tuṇai
puṇarntu - niṉ talaivaṉuṭaṉeṉṟum cērntu vāḻntu piṉ, taval ilulakattu uṟaiiyar -
eṉṟuṅkeṭāta maṟumai yulakattut taṅkivāḻ vīrāka;
17-20. ellaiyumiravum eṉṉātu - pakalum iravum eṉṟillātu, kaleṉa koṇṭal vāṉ maḻai
poḻinta vaikaṟai -kalleṉum oliyuṭaṉ mēkam periya maḻaiyaiccorinta viṭiyaṟ kālattē,
taṇpaṉi aṟciram -kuḷirnta paṉiyaiyuṭaiya aṟcirak kālam,tamiyōrkku aritu eṉa -
taṉittiru pōrkkuttāṅkaṟkari tākumeṉavuṇarntu. kaṉaviṉum pirivuaṟiyalaṉ -
kaṉaviṉkaṇṇum piritalai aṟiyāṉ;
20-2. ataṉ talai -ataṉmēlum, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉ - taṉatunaṟkuṇattiṉāl, muṉ tāṉkaṇṭa
ñāṉṟiṉum -mutaṉ mutal tāṉ kaṇṭa nāḷiṉum, piṉ perituaḷikkum - piṉ enta nāḷiṉum
mikavum aruḷceyvāṉāvaṉ,
(muṭipu) tōḻi! paṉṟi kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum naṉmalaināṭaṉoṭu, eṉṟum puṇarntu
uṟaiiyarō; aṟciramtamiyōrkku ariteṉak kaṉaviṉum pirivuaṟiyalaṉ; ataṉtalai taṉ
paṇpiṉālē, muṉ kaṇṭañāṉṟiṉum piṉ peritu aḷikkum.
(vi - rai.) vayirattaṉṉa maruppu, vai maruppu, ēntu maruppu, eṉattaṉittaṉi iyaiyum.
kuḻintu vaṭṭamāyiruttalpaṟṟic cuṉaikkup paṟaikkaṇ uvamai kūṟappaṭṭatu.paṭāar - ciṟu
tūṟu ; paṭukar eṉṟumām. paṭukar -nīrnilai. karuniṟamuṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉ uṭampilkūtaḷattiṉ
poṉṉiṟap pūmpoṭi paṭintiruttal,poṉṉiṉ māṟṟaṟiyum kariya kaṭṭaḷaik kalliṟpoṉ
uraittāṟpōlum eṉa uvamamum poruḷumottu vilaṅkutal kāṇka.
talaivaṉmāṭṭuttavaṟillaiyāyiṉum avaṉpāl iruppatākaēṟaṭṭukkoṇṭu, kaḷavuk kālattum ciṟu
pāṉmaiūṭutalum, piṉṉar avvūṭal nīṅkik kūṭutalumtalaivimāṭṭuṇmaiyiṉ, 'uṇarntaṉai
puṇarnta'eṉṟāḷ. uṇartal - ūṭal nīṅkutal. iṉi,iyaṟkaip puṇarcci mutaliya
kūṭṭaṅkaḷāl,talaivaṉuṭaiya paṇpiṉai iṉituṇarnta nīyumeṉṟuraittalumām. nīyum eṉṟa
ummaiyāltalaivaṉum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. taval il ulakam -keṭutalillāta iṉpattaiyuṭaiya
maṟumaiyulakam.ivvulakilē taṇavāk kātaluṭaṉ kūṭi illaṟam iṉitunaṭātti, naṉmakap peṟṟu
vāḻntōr,maṟumaiyilum piriviṉṟi iṉpam tuyppareṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, eṉṟum
tavaliṉulakattu uṟaiiyar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka; 1'immaimāṟi maṟumai yāyiṉum, nīyā kiyareṉ
kaṇavaṉai, yāṉākiyarniṉ ṉeñcunēr pavaḷē' 2'immaiyulakat ticaiyoṭum viḷaṅki, maṟumai
yulakamummaṟuviṉ ṟeytupa, ceṟunarum viḻaiyum ceyīrtīrkāṭcic, ciṟuvarp payanta cemma
lōreṉa'eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl, talaivaṉ varaiya varutalai yuṇarntu tōḻi varaivumalintu
kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatupeṟṟām. aṅṅaṉam talaivaṉ varaital orutalai eṉavaṟpuṟuttutaṟkuk kaṉaviṉum
pirivu aṟiyalaṉ eṉṟum, muṉtāṉ kaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum piṉperitaḷikkum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
iyaṟkaippuṇarcci toṭaṅki orukālaik korukāltalaivimāṭṭu aṉpu perukit
talaiyaḷiceytuvarutalaik kaṇṭuḷāḷ. ākaliṉ, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉē aḷikkum eṉṟaṉaḷ.
(u - ṟai.) paṉṟi cuṉai parukik kiḻaṅku māntit tiṉaiviḷaikural mēyntu iṉitu
kaṇpaṭukkum nāṭaṉeṉṟatu, talaivaṉ tāṉ karutiya poruḷelām peṟṟut talaiviyai maṇantu
iṉpantuyttu iṉitu vāḻvāṉ eṉṟapaṭiyām.

179. pālai
[pirivuṇarttiyatalaimakaṟkut tōḻi celavu aḻuṅkac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-10. talaiva-, viṇtōy cimaiya viṟal varaik kavāṉ- vāṉai aḷāviya
ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya perumai taṅkiyamalaiyiṉatu pakkamalaikkaṇṇē, veṇtēr ōṭum
kāykaṭamaruṅkil - pēyt tēr ōṭāniṟkum kāyntakaṟkāṭṭiṉ pakkattē, tuṉai eri paranta
tuṉarumviyaṉ kāṭṭuc ciṟukaṇ yāṉai - viraintaneruppup paraviya kiṭṭutaṟkariya
periyakāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉai, neṭu kainīṭṭi vāṉ vāy tiṟantum -
appēyttēriṉpāl taṉ neṭiya kaiyiṉai nīṭṭiyumperiya vāyiṉait tiṟantum - vaṇpuṉal
peṟāatukāṉ pulantu kaḻiyum kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - vaḷaviyanīriṉaip peṟāmal kāṭṭai
veṟuttuk kaḻintupōm iṭam akaṉṟa pālainilattiṉkaṇ, viṭuvāyc ceṅkaṇaik koṭuvil
āṭavar - viṭutalvāynta civanta ampiṉaiyuṭaiya vaḷainta villaik koṇṭa maṟavaratu nalnilai
poṟittakal nilai atara - nalla veṟṟi nilaiyai eḻutiyanaṭukaṟkaḷ nilaikoṇṭa
vaḻikaḷaiyuṭaiyavāya, arampukoḷ pūcal kaḷaiyunar kāṇāc curamcela virumpiṉir āyiṉ -
kuṟumparkaḷ ceyyum pūcalai nīkkunaraik kāṇāta curaneṟiyaik kaṭantu cellavirumpiṉīr
āyiṉ;
10-4. iṉ nakaimuruntueṉat tiraṇṭa muḷeyiṟṟut tuvar vāy -iṉiya nakaiyiṉaiyum
mayiliṟakiṉ aṭiyeṉat tiraṇṭamuṭpōlum kūriya paṟkaḷaiyum civanta vāyiṉaiyum,kuvaḷai nāḷ
malar puraiyum uṇkaṇ - kuvaḷaiyiṉ putiyamalarai yokkum maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaiyum uṭaiya,
imatiēr vāḷ nutal pulampa - inta matiyiṉai yottaoḷi poruntiya
neṟṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiyāḷvarunta, patipeyarntu uṟaital numakku ollumō - ippatiyainīṅkippōyt
taṅkutal umakkupporuntuvatāmō?
(muṭipu) talaiva!curamcela virumpiṉirāyiṉ, vāḷnutal pulampappatipeyarntu uṟaital
numakku ollumō?
veṇtēr ōṭumviyaṉ kāṭṭil, yāṉai, kai nīṭṭiyum vāytiṟantum puṉal peṟātu pulantu
kaḻivatumkoṭuvillāṭavar naṭukal nilaipeṟṟaatarkaḷaiyuṭaiyatum arampukoḷ pūcal
kaḷaiyunarillātatum ākiya curam eṉka.
(vi - rai.) koṭuvil āṭavar, karantai maṟavar eṉka, arampu -kuṟumpar, kāṭṭut talaivar,
'iṉṉakai...vāṇutalpulampa' eṉṟatu, talaiviyiṉ iṉiya nakaiyum kūriyapallumtuvar vāyum
uṇkaṇṇum vāṇutalum āya ivai,nummuṉ tōṉṟi nummai aḻuṅkuvippaṉa vākaliṉ,nīr
pirintuṟaital ollāteṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
