180. neytal
[irantu piṉṉiṉṟatalaimakaṟkuk kuṟainērnta tōḻi talaimakaḷaikkuṟainayappak kūṟiyatu.
talaimakaṉciṟaippuṟattāṉākat tōḻikkuccolliyatūumām.]

(co - ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, takai mika kōtai āyamoṭu kuvavu maṇal ēṟi -aḻaku mika
mālaiyaiyuṭaiya āyattāruṭaṉ (nāṉ) tiraṇṭamaṇal mēṭṭil ēṟi, vī tatai kāṉal vaṇṭal uyara
-malar neruṅkiya cōlaiyil viḷaiyāṭalceyyāniṟka, oruvaṉ - oru talaivaṉ, kataḻparitiṇtēr
kaṭaii vantu - virainta kutiraiyaiyuṭaiyavaliya tēriṉaic celutti vantu, taṇ kayattu
amaṉṟaoḷpū kuvaḷai - taṇṇiya kuḷattē niṟainta oḷḷiyapūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya kuvaḷaiyiṉ arumpu
alaittu iyaṟṟiyacurumpu ār kaṇṇi - arumpiṉai virit tukkaṭṭiyavaṇṭukaḷ moykkum
kaṇṇiyiṉai, piṉṉuppuṟamtāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi - eṉatu piṉṉalaik koṇṭamutukiṉpāl
tāḻntiṭa yāṉ vēṇṭātē talaiyiṟcūṭṭi, nalvaral iḷamulai nōkki - nallavaḷarcciyaiyuṭaiya
iḷaiya mulaiyaip pārttu,neṭituniṉaintu nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ - ētō nīḷaniṉaintu piṉ
tāḻkkātu pōyiṉaṉ;
9-15. ataṟkē -avvaḷaviṟkē, i aḻuṅkal ūr - inta āravāramikkaūr, pulavu nāṟu
irukaḻituḻaii palauṭaṉ puḷiṟai koṇṭa- pulāl vīcum periya kaḻiyiṉait tuḻāvippalavakaip
puṭkaḷum oruṅkē taṅkiyirukkum, muḷuṭai neṭu tōṭṭut tāḻai maṇantu - muḷḷuṭaiya
nīṇṭaitaḻaiyuṭaiya tāḻaiyaic cārntu, ñāḻaloṭu keḻīi -pulinakak koṉṟaiyoṭu porunti,
paṭappai niṉṟamuṭattāḷ puṉṉaip poṉ nēr nuṇtātu nōkki- tōṭṭattil niṟkum vaḷainta
tāḷiṉaiyuṭaiyapuṉṉaiyatu poṉṉai yotta nuṇṇiya tātiṉaippārttu, eṉṉum nōkkum -
eṉṉaiyum nōkkāniṟkum;
1. nakai naṉi uṭaittu -itu mikavum nakaiyiṉait taruva toṉṟākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nāṉ āyamoṭu vaṇṭal ayara, oruvaṉ tērkaṭaii vantu, kaṇṇiyiṉaip
piṉṉuppuṟam tāḻakkoṉṉē cūṭṭi iḷamulai nōkki, neṭitu niṉaintupeyarntaṉaṉ; ataṟkē
inta aḻuṅkalūr puṉṉaittātu nōkki eṉṉum nōkkum; itu nakainaṉiyuṭaittu.
(vi - rai.) yāṉ virumpātirukkac ceytē cūṭṭiṉāṉeṉpāḷ 'koṉṉē cūṭṭi' yeṉṟāḷ.
piṉavaṉōṭu taṉakku yātoru toṭarpum iṉṟeṉpāḷnillātu peyarntaṉaṉ eṉṟāḷ.
ivvūrpuṉṉaiyiṉ tātu nōkki eṉṉaiyum nōkkumeṉṟatu, eṉ. mēṉi puṉṉaiyiṉ
tātupōlum pacalaipaṭarnta teṉak karutukiṉṟatu eṉṟapaṭi; itu kuṟippunuṭpamām.
tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talaivi kuṟai nayappakkaruti, iṅṅaṉam paṭaittu moḻiyāl kūṟuvē, ikkāṉaliṉkaṇ
iṅṅaṉam vantu celvāṉ talaivaṉēyāvāṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷāyk kuṟai nērvāḷāmeṉka.
iraṇṭāvatu tuṟaiyiṉ karuttu talaiviyāṉavaḷtalaivaṉ varaiyātu pirintiruttalāl, taṉ
mēṉipacalai pūttalaiyum ataṉāl ūriṉkaṇ alareḻutalaiyum talaimakaṉ kēṭpat
tōḻikkuccolliṉāḷām eṉpatu.

181. pālai
[iṭaiccurattu oḻiyakkarutiya neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3. neñcē-,
22-6. pakāar paṇṭamnāṟum vaṇṭu aṭar aimpāl - viṟpāratu naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ nāṟukiṉṟa
vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum aimpakutiyākiya kūntaliṉaiyum, paṇaittakai taṭaiiyakāṇpu iṉmeltōḷ -
mūṅkiliṉtakutiyaiyuṭaiyatāy vaḷainta kāṇṭaṟku iṉiyamelliya tōḷiṉaiyumuṭaiya, aṇaṅkucāl
arivaiirunta - aḻaku mikka nam talaiviyirunta, maṇam kamaḻmaṟukiṉ maṇal perum kuṉṟu -
maṇam nāṟumterukkaḷaiyuṭaiya maṇalaiyuṭaiya periya kuṉṟam;
3-7. āay eyiṉaṉ - āyeyiṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, muruku uṟaḻ muṉpoṭu - murukaṉaiotta
valimaiyoṭu niṉṟu, oṉṉār ōmpu araṇkaṭanta vīṅku perum tāṉai - pakaivar
pātukākkumaraṇkaḷai veṉṟu kaṭanta mikka periya cēṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya, aṭupōr miñili
ceruviṟku -pōr aṭutalvalla miñili eṉpāṉ ceruviṉ kaṇ,kaḷam civappap porutu -
kurutiyāl kaḷamcivappuṟap porutu, piṉ uṭaii vīḻnteṉa -tōṟṟu vīḻntaṉaṉāka ;
7-10. ñāyiṟṟu oḷkatiruruppam putaiya - ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷḷiya katirkaḷiṉ veppam (avaṉ
uṭaliṟpaṭātu)maṟaiya, ōrāṅku-orupeṟṟiyē, vampap puḷḷiṉ kampalaipperuntōṭu - putiya
puṭkaḷiṉ oli poruntiyaperiya kūṭṭam, vicumpiṭaitūra āṭi - vicum piṭammaṟaiya
vaṭṭamiṭṭup piṉpu, uṭaṉ mocintu -oruṅkukūṭi;
11-9. pūviri akal tuṟai -pūkkaḷ virinta akaṉṟa tuṟai yiṉaiyuṭaiya, kāvirippēr
yāṟṟu - kāviriyāya periya āṟṟiṉ,
kaṉaivicaik kaṭunīr - mikkavicaiyuṭaṉ kaṭukivarum nīrāṉatu, ayir koṇṭu īṇṭi-
nuṇmaṇalaikkoṇṭu tiraḷumpaṭi, ekkar iṭṭa,kuppai veṇmaṇal - mēṭākkiya
veḷḷiyamaṇaṟkuviyalaiyum, yāṇar vaippiṉ - putuvaruvāyaiyumuṭaiya ūrkaḷaiyuṭaiya, vaḷam
keḻu vēntar -celvam mikka cōḻavēntarāṟ purakkappaṭum,ñālam nāṟum nalamkeḻu nalicai -
ulakamellāmparavum naṉmai poruntiya naṟpukaḻaiyuṭaiya, nālmaṟaimutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ -
nāṉkuvētaṅkaḷāya paḻaiya nūlai aruḷiya mukkaṇṇaiyuṭaiyaparamaṉatu, ālamuṟṟam - ālamuṟṟam
eṉṉumiṭattu, kaviṉpeṟataiiya - aḻakupeṟa iyaṟṟappeṟṟa, poykaicūḻnta poḻil - poykaiyaic
cūḻntuḷapoḻiliṉkaṇṇē, maṉai makaḷir - ciṟṟiliḻaittuviḷaiyāṭum ciṟumikaḷatu, kaicey
pāvait tuṟaikkaṇ- aḻakuṟac peyyappeṟṟa pāvaikaḷaiyuṭaiya tuṟaiyiṉkaṇṇē, iṟukkum - vantu
taṅkuvatum ;
20-2. makara neṟṟivāṉtōy puricai - makarak koṭiyiṉaiucciyiṟkoṇṭa vāṉaittōyum
matilaiyum,cikaram tōṉṟāc cēṇ uyar nalil - muṭiaṟiyappaṭātavāṟu cēṇiṉkaṇ uyarnta
nallamāṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyatum ākiya, pukāarnal nāṭṭatuvē -kāvirip pūm paṭṭiṉattaiyuṭaiya
nallacōḻanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatākum;
1-3. tuṉ arumkāṉamum tuṇital āṟṟāy - cellutaṟku ariyakāṭṭaik kaṭakkavum tuṇivuṟal
ceyyāyāy, piṉniṉṟupeyarac cūḻntaṉai āyiṉ - piṉṉē niṉṟupeyarntu mīṇṭiṭak
karutiṉaiyāyiṉ, eṉnilaiuraimō - eṉatu innilaiyiṉai āṇṭuc ceṉṟu avaṭkuuraippāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! arivai irunta maṇaṟperuṅ kuṉṟu pukāarnaṉṉāṭṭatu; nī kāṉam tuṇital
āṟṟāy; piṉniṉṟu peyarac cūḻntaṉaiyāyiṉ, eṉnilaiāṇṭuc ceṉṟu, avaṭku uraippāyāka.
(vi - rai.) kāṉamum - kāṭṭiṉaik kaṭakkavum; ummai eccapporuṭṭu; acai nilaiyumām.
oṉṉār, ōmparaṇkaṭanta vīṅkuperuntāṉai eṉpataṉāl miñiliyiṉtāṉaip perukkamum,
murukuṟaḻ muṉ poṭu porutueṉpataṉāl eyiṉaṉatupōr valiyum peṟappaṭṭaṉa,kaḷaña civappap
porutu eṉṟamaiyāl, pakaivartāṉaiyaiyellām koṉṟu piṉ vīḻntamaipeṟṟām. kaḷañ civappa
vīḻntaṉaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, pakaivaṉatu vēlāl tākkuṇṭu vīḻntāṉeṉṟuraittalumām. eyiṉaṉ
puḷḷiṉpātukāvalaṉākaliṉ, ivaṉ vīḻnta pōḻtupaṟavaikaḷellām ivaṉmēl ñāyiṟṟiṉkatirpaṭātu
maṟaittaṉa eṉpatu, 1'puḷḷiṟkuēma mākiya perum peyar, veḷḷattāṉai atikaṟ (eyiṉaṟ)
koṉṟu'eṉpataṉālum,1'veḷiyaṉvēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ...miñiliyoṭu, naṇpakaluṟṟa ceruvil
puṇkūrn, toḷvāḷ mayaṅkamarvīḻnteṉap, puḷḷoruṅku aṅkaṇ vicumpiṉviḷaṅku ñāyiṟu,
oṇkatir teṟāmaic ciṟakarilkōli, niḻal ceytu uḻaṟal' eṉpataṉālumaṟiyappaṭum. 'akavunar
vēṇṭiṉ veṇkōṭṭu,aṇṇal yāṉai īyum vaṇmakiḻ, veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉāay eyiṉaṉ' (208)
eṉṟu ivaṉ kūṟap peṟutaliṉīṇṭu 'vaṇmai eyiṉaṉ' eṉṟu kāṇappaṭum pāṭamumporutta
muṭaittē.

eyiṉaṉvīḻnteṉa, uruppam putaiya, puḷḷiṉ peruntōṭuāṭi, mocintu, tuṟaikkaṇ iṟukkum
pukār naṉṉāṭueṉka, mukkaṭcelvaṉatu ālamuṟṟattiṭattē taiiyapoykai cūḻnta poḻiliṉ
makaḷir kaiceypāvaikaḷaiyuṭaiya tuṟaiyeṉṟu iyaiyum. vaḷaṅkeḻuvēntarāl purakkappaṭum
pukār naṉṉāṭu eṉka; ālamuṟṟam eṉalumām. īṇṭi, īṇṭaveṉat tirikka.iṟaivaṉ pukaḻē
meyyāya pukaḻ ākalāṉum, atuvēulakeṅkum parattaṟ kuriyatākalāṉum 'ñālamnāṟum
nalaṅkeḻu nallicai mukkaṭ celvaṉ'eṉṟār. nāṉmaṟai mukkaṇ celvaṉatuvāymoḻiyām eṉpatu,
2'naṉṟāyntanīṇimir caṭai, mutumutalvaṉ vāypōkā, toṉṟupurinta īriraṇṭiṉ, āṟuṇarnta
oru mutu nūl'eṉṟataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. ālamuṟṟam - civapirāṉeḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷatōr
iṭamākum. iṟukkum pukāreṉavum puricaiyiṉaiyum nal illiṉaiyumuṭaiya pukāreṉavum
taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. pakarvār eṉpatu pakāareṉat tirintu niṉṟatu. paṇṭam - pūvum
cāntummutalāyiṉa. aṇaṅkucāl arivai - varuttutalaiyuṭaiyaarivai. eṉṟātal, teyvam
pōlum arivaieṉṟātal kūṟutalumām. kuṉṟam naṉṉāṭṭatu ; cūḻntaṉaiyāyiṉ āṇṭuc ceṉṟu
eṉ nilaiuraiyeṉa viritturaikka.

182. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi irā varuvāṉaippakal vara eṉṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-8. pū kaṇ vēṅkai poṉ iṇar milaintu - aḻakiyaiṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya vēṅkai
marattiṉ poṉpōlumpūṅkottukkaḷaic cūṭi, vāṅku amai nōṉcilaieruttattu irīi - vaḷainta
mūṅkilālāya valiyavillait tōḷil irutti, tīm paḻappala viṉcuḷaiviḷai tēṟal - iṉiya
palāppaḻattiṉcuḷaiyiṉiṉṟum viḷainta tēṉai, vīḷai ampiṉiḷaiyaroṭu mānti - cīḻkkai
yoliyuṭaṉcellum ampiṉaiyuṭaiya ēvaliḷaiyaruṭaṉ niṟaiyakkuṭittu, ōṭṭu iyal piḻaiyā
vayanāy piṟpaṭa -vilaṅkukaḷait turattip paṟṟum iyalputapputalillāta valiya nāy piṉṉēvara,
vēṭṭampōkiya kuṟavaṉ - vēṭṭaikkuc ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ,kāṭṭa kuḷavit taṇputal kurutiyoṭu
tuyalvara -kāṭṭiluḷḷa mallikai yāya taṇṇiya putarutirattoṭu acaintiṭa, muḻavu mā
tolaiccum kuṉṟanāṭa - muḷḷam paṉṟiyaik koṉṟu vīḻttumkuṉṟukaḷai yuṭaiya nāṭṭiṉ
talaivaṉē!
9-12. uravu maḻai aravueṟi urumōṭu oṉṟi - kālvīḻttu - valiya mēkamaraviṉai eṟintu
kollum iṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭik kāliṟakkaṅ koṇṭu, poḻinta pāl nāḷ kaṅkul -nīriṉaic corinta
arai nāḷākiya iravil, taṉiyaivanta āṟu niṉaintu - nī taṉiyaiyāki vanta vaḻiyētattiṉai
niṉaintu, alkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻumivaḷ kaṇṇē - ivaḷ kaṇ eppoḻutum nīr
koṇṭuaḻāniṟkum;
12-8. ataṉāl-,atirkural mutukalai kaṟimuṟi muṉaii - atirumkuraliṉaiyuṭaiya mutiya
mucukkalai miḷakiṉ taḷirai uṇṭu
veṟuttu, uyar cimai neṭu kōṭṭuukaḷa-uyarnta ucciyiṉai yuṭaiya nīṇṭacikaraṅkaḷilē
tāvutalāl, ukka-utirnta, kamaḻitaḻ alari tāay - maṇam nāṟum itaḻiṉaiyuṭaiyapūkkaḷ
parantu, vēlaṉ veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷamkaṭukkum - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum periya
kaḷattiṉaiokkum, peruvarai naṇṇiya cāralāṉ-periya malaiyaiaṭuttuḷḷa cāraṟ kaṇṇē, kaṭum
pakal varutalvēṇṭum - kaṭiya pakaṟ kaṇṇē nī varutal vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) nāṭa! pāṉāṭ kaṅkul taṉiyai vantavāṟu niṉaintu,ivaḷkaṇ alkalum paṉiyoṭu
kaluḻum ; ataṉālperuvarai naṇṇiya cāraṟkaṇṇē nī kaṭumpakal varutalvēṇṭum.
(vi - rai.) putariṭattē patuṅkiyiruntamuḷavumāvaik kollutaliṉ putal
kurutiyoṭutuyalvarutalāyiṟṟu. iṭiyoli kēṭṭu aravuuṭkumeṉṉum vaḻakkuppaṟṟi
araveṟiyurumeṉappaṭṭatu. urumiṉ kaṭumai kūṟiyapaṭiyumām.urumōṭoṉṟi eṉṟataṉāl
āṟṟiṉatu ētamkuṟittāḷ. tuṇaiyoṭum varutaṟkariya ētappāṭuṭaiyavaḻiyil naḷḷiruḷil
taṉimaiyāyt taṉporuṭṭuvarukiṉṟāy eṉpatuṇarntu, ivaḷ āṟṟāmaiperituṭaiya ḷākiṉṟāḷ
eṉpāḷ, 'taṉiyai vantaāṟu niṉaintalkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻu mivaḷ kaṇṇē'eṉṟāḷ. kaṭum
pakal - naṇpakal eṉṟapaṭi. teyya ;acai.
(u - ṟai.) kuṟavaṉ muḷavumāvaik kolla ataṟku iṭamākiya putalkurutiyoṭu acaintu
tōṉṟiyateṉpatu, talaivaṉtalaiviyoṭu kaḷavoḻukkattil oḻuka,avvoḻukkam talaiviyiṉ mēṉi
vēṟupāṭṭāltāy mutaliyōrukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟām.
------
183. pālai
[talaimakaṉ kuṟitta paruvavaravu kaṇṭu talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. tōḻi-, kuvaḷai uṇkaṇ kaluḻavum - eṉatu kuvaḷaimalarpōṉṟa maiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ
aḻavum, tiruntu iḻaititalai alkul avvari vāṭavum - tiruntiyaaṇiyaiyum
tēmalaiyumuṭaiya alkuliṉ aḻakiya varivāṭṭamuṟavum, aruḷār nattuṟantu - aruḷceyyārāy
nammaip pirintu, attamāraḻuvam ceṉṟu - aruneṟikaḷaiyuṭaiya paranta pālaiyaikkaṭantu
ceṉṟu, cēṇiṭaiyār āyiṉum -cēymaikkaṇ uḷḷār āyiṉum, naṉṟum nīṭalareṉṟi - mikavum
tāḻkkātu varuvar eṉkiṉṟāy;
5-10. iruṅkiḷaikkoṇmū - periya kūṭṭamāya mēkaṅkaḷ, kuvavu tirai -vaḷainta
alaikaḷaiyum, paṉittuṟai - kuḷirnta tuṟaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, peruṅkaṭal iṟantu - periya
kaṭalilēmikkuc ceṉṟu, nīr paruki aruntu koḷḷaiya -nīriṉaip parukiyuṇṭamikutiyai
yuṭaiyaṉavāy, kuṭakkuērpu - mēṟkē eḻuntu, vayavu piṭi iṉattiṉ - cūluṟṟapeṇyāṉaik
kūṭṭampōla, vayiṉ vayiṉ tōṉṟi- iṭantōṟum tōṉṟi, orucēra pāṭu āṉṟu -olimikku,
oruṅku uṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi - peytaṟkuneruṅka, kār kālai vantaṉṟu - kār kālamkālaiyē
vantatu eṉiṉum;
10-15. mālai - mālaippoḻutil, kuḷirkoḷ piṭaviṉ kūr mukai alari -kuḷircci
poruntiya piṭāviṉatu kūriya arumpualar taṟkuriyataṉai, vaṇṭu vāy tiṟakkum
taṇṭānāṟṟam - vaṇṭu vāyiṉai nekiḻttalāl eḻumamaiyāta maṇattiṉai, kūtir aṟcirattu
ūtai tūṟṟa -kūtir muṉpaṉik kālaṅkaṭku uriya vāṭaikkāṟṟāṉatu tūṟṟā niṟka, paṉi alai
kalaṅkiyaneñcamoṭu - paṉi alaittalāl kalaṅkiyaneñcamuṭaṉ, pirinticiṉōr tiṟattu -
pirintuceṉṟārāya talaivariṉ poruṭṭu, varuntuvamallamō - nām varuntutaṟ kuriyōm
allēmō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! talaivar nattuṟantu cēṇiṭaiyarāyiṉumnīṭalar eṉṟi; kār kālai vantaṉṟu;
mālai,piṭaviṉatu alariyiṉ nāṟṟattai ūtai tūṟṟa paṉialaik kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu nām
varuntuvamallamō?
(vi - rai.) iḻai - mēkalai. nam tuṟantu - nattuṟantu eṉavikāramāyiṟṟu. tuvaṉṟi -
tuvaṉṟa eṉat tirikka.kārpparuvam vanta tākaliṉ, talaivar nīṭātuvantuviṭuvar eṉṟu kūṟi
āṟṟuvitta tōḻikku,avar varuntuṇaiyum nām eṅṅaṉamāṟṟiyirukkaṟpālam eṉpāḷ,
'kārkālaivantatu eṉiṉum mālai kūtir aṟcirattu ūtaitūṟṟap paṉi alaik kalaṅkiya
neñcamoṭuvaruntuvam allamō' eṉṟu talaivi kūṟiṉāḷ.kārkālai vantateṉiṉum mālai kūtir
aṟcirattuūtai tūṟṟa eṉṟatu, ap paruvaṅkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttuviraintuvarum eṉpatai
uṇarttiyavāṟu.kārpparuvam kuṟittuc ceṉṟa talaivar,apparuvam vantum iṉṉum
vantilāmaiyiṉ,aṭuttuvarum kūtir aṟcirap paruvaṅkaḷiṟṟāṉavar varutal eṅṅaṉam
teḷiyappaṭum eṉṟāḷumāyiṟṟu.
---------
184. mullai
[talaimakaṉ viṉaivayiṟpirintu vantu eytiyaviṭattu tōḻi pullumakiḻvu uraittatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 19. cīrmiku kuricil - ciṟappumikka talaivaṉē!
5-19. veṇpiṭavuaviḻtta vī kamaḻ puṟavil - veḷḷiya piṭavamarattiṉ virinta malar maṇakkum
mullainilattē, kuṇṭaik kōṭṭa kuṟumuḷ kaḷḷi - kuṟiyakiḷaikaḷaiyum kuṟiya
muṭkaḷaiyumuṭaiya kaḷḷiyiṉ, puṉtalaiputaitta koḻu koṭi mullai - pulliyaucciyiṉai
mūṭiya vaḷaviya mullaikkoṭiyiṉ, ārkaḻal putu pū uyirppiṉ nīkki - ārkkukkaḻaṉṟa
putiya pūkkaḷai mūcceṟitalāl otukki, teḷaṟal parukiya tiri maruppu eḻil kalai -
teḷintanīraik kuṭitta muṟukkuṇṭa kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiyamāṉ kalai, puḷḷi am piṇaiyoṭu
vatiyum āṅkaṇ -puḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya piṇaiyuṭaṉ taṅkumavviṭattē, kōṭu uṭaik
kaiyar - kaḷaikkoṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya kaiyiṉarāy, tuḷar eṟi viṉaiñar -kaḷaiyiṉai veṭṭi eṟiyum
toḻilaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ,ariyal ārkaiyar viḷaimakiḻ tūṅka - kaḷḷiṉainiṟaiya uṇṭu ataṉālāya
kaḷippu mikkuṟa, celkatirmaḻukiya uruva ñāyiṟṟu - cellum katir vemmaikuṟainta
cenniṟattiṉatāya ñāyiṟṟaiyuṭaiya,cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟa poḻutil - cevvāṉamparaviya
kālattē, kalpāl aruviyiṉ olikkumnaṟṟēr - malaiyiṉpāl vīḻumaruviyaippōlaolikkum
nalla tēriluḷḷa,tārmaṇi pala uṭaṉ iyampa - mālaiyākiya maṇikaḷpalavum oruṅkē olikka,
curumpu imir malara kāṉampiṟpaṭa - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭupiṉṉēpōka,
aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaluḷḷamoṭu - ariya viṉaiyai muṭitta talaimai mēviyaūkkattuṭaṉ,
nī vantu niṉṟatu - nī iṅku vantuniṉṟatu;
1-4. kaṭavuṭ kaṟpoṭu- teyvak kaṟpuṭaṉ, kuṭikku viḷakku ākiya - kuṭikkuviḷakkam
ākiya, putalvaṉ payanta pukaḻmikuciṟappiṉ naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum - makaṉaippeṟṟa pukaḻ
mikka ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya naṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyatalaivikkē yallāmalum, eṉakkum iṉitu ākiṉṟu
-eṉakkum iṉimaiyait taruvatākiṉṟatu; niṉ āyuḷciṟakka - niṉ āyuḷ ciṟantiṭuvatāka.
(muṭipu) cīrmiku kuricil! cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟapoḻutil, naṟṟēr maṇi iyampa, kāṉam
piṟpaṭacemmal uḷḷamoṭu nī vantu niṉṟatu, naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum, eṉakkum
iṉitākiṉṟu; niṉ āyuḷ ciṟakka.
(vi - rai.) kaṭavuṭ kaṟpu - teyvattaṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya kaṟpu.kaṟpuṭai makaḷir vēṇṭuṅkāl
maḻaiyumpeyvippārākaliṉ avaratu kaṟpu teyvattaṉmaiyuṭaiyatāyiṟṟu. 1'kaṟpukkaṭampūṇṭa
itteyvamallatu, poṟpuṭait teyvamyāmkaṇ ṭilamāl, vāṉam poyyātu vaḷampiḻaippaṟiyātu,
nīṇila vēntar koṟṟam citaiyātu,pattiṉip peṇṭi rirunta nāṭu' eṉpatuṅ kāṇka.kaṭavuṭ
kaṟpoṭu putalvaṉ payanta ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiyanaṉṉarāṭṭi eṉṟu iyaiyum. putalvaṉ
kuṭiyaiviḷaṅkac ceypavaṉ ākaliṉ, kuṭikku viḷakkākiyaputalvaṉ eṉṟāḷ. maṉaivāḻkkai
makaḷirkkukkaṟpoḻukkamum, naṉmakkaṭpēṟumiṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa eṉpatu, 2'maṅkalameṉpa maṉaimāṭci
maṟṟataṉ-naṉkalam naṉmakkaṭpēṟu' eṉṉum poyyā moḻiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum.3'maṉaikkuviḷakkam
maṭavār' eṉpatupaṟṟik kuṭikkuviḷakkākiya eṉpataṉait talaivikkuēṟṟiyuraittalumām.
naṉṉarāṭṭi - maṉaimāṭciyāyanaṉmaiyellām uṭaiyavaḷ. kaṇavaṉ kūṟiyacoṟpiḻaiyātu
āṟṟiyirunta arumaippāṭutōṉṟat talaivi iṅṅaṉam ciṟappittuk kūṟappeṟṟāḷ.
pirivāṟṟātu talaivi varuntuntōṟumtāṉum uṭaṉ varunti, aritiṉ
āṟṟuvittukkoṇṭiruntavaḷ ākaliṉ, nī vantu niṉṟatueṉakkum iṉitākiṉṟatu eṉṟu tōḻi
kūṟiṉāṉ.pirinta talaivaṉ mīṇṭu vantatēyaṉṟi avaṉ ariyaviṉaiyai muṭitta perumitattuṭaṉ
tēriṉ maṇiolikkavantu niṉṟa tōṟṟam, talaivaṉatu ākkattaivirumpum talaivikkuk
kaḻipēriṉpampayappatākaliṉ, cemmaluḷḷamoṭu maṇi iyampa nīvantu niṉṟatu iṉitākiṉṟu
eṉṟāḷ. talaivaṉ tērvanta viraivu tōṉṟak kāṉam piṟpaṭa eṉṟāḷ.celkatir - mēṟṟicaikkaṭ
ceṉṟu maṟaiyumkatir. tuḷar - kaḷai. tār maṇi - mālaiyākak kōttamaṇikaḷ.
talaivaṉ pōntakāṭṭiṉkaṇ mullai, kaḷḷiyiṉ puṉtalaiyaimaṟaittiruppatu; talaivi taṉ
pirivāluḷatākiyavaruttam puṟantōṉṟāmal kaṟpiṉālāṟṟiyiruttalaiyum, maṟaitta pūvai
uyirppiṉālnīkki nīriṉaip parukiya kalai piṇaiyoṭu vatital,tāṉ talaiviyiṉ meliviṉai
nīkki avaḷuṭaṉiṉpuṟṟu vatiyap pōvataiyum avaṉukkuppulappaṭutti maṉaveḻucci taruvaṉa
eṉka.

185. pālai
[piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭatalaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 5-14. vāṭal oli kaḻai nivanta nel uṭaineṭuvetir-vāṭutaluṟṟa olikkiṉṟa
mēlnōkkieḻunta taṇṭiṉaiyum neṟkaḷaiyumuṭaiya nīṇṭamūṅkil, kalikoḷ maḷḷar vil vicaiyiṉ
uṭaiya- āravāram koṇṭa maṟavaratu villiṉiṉ ṟeḻumampiṉ vicaiyāṟ piḷantiṭa, paitu aṟa
vempiyakal poru parappiṉ - pacumai aṟak kāyntaparukkaikaḷ porukiṉṟa iṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya,
vēṉilattattu āṅkaṇ - vēṉilāṉ uḻanta kāṭṭiṭattē,vāṉ ulantu aruvi āṉṟa uyar cimai
maruṅkiṉ -mēkam peyyā toḻitaliṉ aruvikaḷ illaiyākiyauyarnta cikaraṅkaḷil, peruviḻā
viḷakkampōla -periya kārttikai viḻāviṟku iṭumviḷakkukaḷaip pōla, pala uṭaṉ ilai ila
malarntailavamoṭu - ilaiyē illaṉavāyp palavum oruṅkēmalarnta ilava maraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu, nilai
uyarpiṟaṅkal malai iṟantōr - uyarnta nilaiyaiyuṭaiyapakka malaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya perumalaiyiṉait
tāṇṭicceṉṟōrāya nam talaivar;
1-5. āy iḻai naleḻil paṇaittōḷ - āynteṭutta aṇikaḷaiyumnalla aḻakiṉaiyumuṭaiya
mūṅkilpōlum namatu tōḷ,elvaḷai ñekiḻac cāay - oḷiporuntiya vaḷainekiḻumpaṭi
melintu, iruṅkaviṉ aḻiya -periya aḻaku keṭumāṟu, peru kai aṟṟa neñcamoṭu
namtuṟantu - periya ceyalaṟṟa neñcamōṭirukkumnammaik kaiviṭṭu, irumpiṉ iṉ
uyiruṭaiyōrpōla - irumpiṉāl iyaṉṟa iṉiya uyiraiuṭaiyārpōla, valittuvalliṉar -
valittirukkavaṉmaiyuṭaiyōrāyiṉar.
(muṭipu) malaiyiṟantōr āya nam kātalar, nam paṇaittōḷcāayk kaviṉ aḻiya nattuṟantu
irumpiṉiṉṉuyir uṭaiyōr pōla valittu valliṉar.
(vi - rai.) neñcamoṭu irukka eṉa orucol viritturaikka.tām eytum tuṉpam karuti
iraṅkit talaivarvārātiruttaliṉ irumpiṉ iṉṉuyiruṭaiyōrpōla valittu valliṉar
eṉṟāḷ.nammai irumpāliyaṉṟa uyiruṭaiyārpōlak karutivārātirukkiṉṟār
eṉṟuraittalumām.

186. marutam
[talaimakaṭkuppāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpa illiṭaip parattaicolli neruṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. vāṉam vēṇṭā vaṟaṉ il vāḻkkai -maḻaipeytalai vēṇṭāta vaṟumaiyuṟuta
lillātavāḻkkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, nōṉ ñāṇ viṉaiñar - valiyatūṇṭiṟ kayiṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya mīṉ
piṭippōr,kōḷ aṟintu īrkkum - (mīṉ irai kōttamuḷḷiṉaip) paṟṟiyatu uṇarntu
iḻukkum, mīṉmutir ilañci - mīṉ mikka nīrnilaiyil,kalitta tāmarai nīr micai nivanta
neṭu tāḷ akalilai-taḻaitta tāmaraiyiṉ nīrmītu uyarnta neṭiyakāmpiṉaiyuṭaiya akaṉṟa ilaiyai,
irukayam tuḷaṅka kāluṟutōṟum-periya kuḷam alaiyak kāṟṟuaṭikkuntōṟum, peru
kaḷiṟṟuc ceviyiṉalaikkum ūraṉoṭu - periya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉaiyiṉkātupōla acaivikkum
ūraṉāl, eḻunta kauvaiyōperitē - eṉakku uṇṭāya alarō peritākumāyiṉum;
7-9. naṭpu-avaṉnaṭpō, koḻu kōl vēḻattup puṇai tuṇai āka -ceḻitta koṟukkaṅkaḻiyālāya
puṇaiyiṉaittuṇaiyākak koṇṭu, puṉal āṭu kēṇmai aṉaittē -puṉal viḷaiyāṭum naṭpiṉ
aḷaviṉatē;
9-13. avaṉē - attalaivaṉṟāṉ, oḷ toṭi makaḷir paṇṭaiyāḻpāṭa-oḷḷiya toṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
makaḷirpaḻaiyayāḻiṉaip pāṭavum, īr taṇ muḻaviṉeṟikuṇil vitirppa-mikka taṇmai
vāyntamuḻaviṉaik kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭikkavum. taṇ naṟumcāntam kamaḻum tōḷ maṇantu-taṇṇiya
naṟiyacantaṉam nāṟum tōḷaik kūṭi, iṉṉum piṟaḷvayiṉāṉ - iṉṉum piṟaḷiṭattēyuḷḷāṉ
āvaṉ;aṅṅaṉamākavum ;
13-14 maṉaiyōḷemmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa - avaṉ maṉaivi emmuṭaṉveṟukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpar;
14-17. māri ampiṉmaḻait tōl paḻaiyaṉ-veṟṟi poruntiyavēlaiyum maḻaittuḷi pōṉṟa mikka
ampiṉaiyummēkampōlum kariya kēṭakattiṉaiyum uṭaiya paḻaiyaṉeṉpāṉatu, kāviri vaippiṉ
pōor aṉṉa -kāviri nāṭṭiluḷḷa pōr eṉṉuma ūriṉai otta,eṉ ceṟi vaḷai uritiṉiṉ
uṭaittalō ilaṉ-eṉatu neruṅkiya vaḷaikaḷaiurimaiyāl uṭaitta loṉṟuñ ceytilēṉ; eṉavē,
18-20. yām taṉpakaiyēm allēm - yām avaḷukkup pakaiyāvēmallēm, cērntōr tirunutal
pacappa nīṅkum- taṉṉaic cērntōratu aḻakiya neṟṟi pacantiṭaavarkaḷai oḻittu nīṅkum,
koḻunaṉum - avaḷkaṇavaṉ, taṉ uṭaṉ uṟaipakai cālum - taṉṉuṭaṉkūṭiyuṟaiyum pakaivaṉātaṟkup
poruntuvāṉāvaṉ.
(muṭipu) ūraṉoṭu eṉakku eḻunta kauvaiyō peritē: naṭpēvēḻattup puṇai tuṇaiyākap
puṉalāṭu kēṇmai aṉaittē;avaṉē iṉṉum piṟaḷ vayiṉāṉē; maṉaiyōḷ emmoṭupulakkum eṉpa;
ceṟivaḷai uṭaittalō ilēṉ; yāmtaṉ pakaiyēm allēm; koḻunaṉ taṉ uṭaṉuṟai pakaicālum.
(vi - rai.) mīṉ piṭikkum viṉaiñar uḻaviṉāl nel mutaliyaṉaviḷaittal ilarākaliṉ avaratu
vāḻkkai vāṉamvēṇṭā vāḻkkai eṉavum, aṉṉarāyiṉum avarpiṭikkum mīṉē avarukkup
periyatoruvaruvāyātaliṉ, vaṟaṉil vāḻkkai eṉavumkūṟappaṭṭatu. nōṉñāṇ - tūṇṭiliṉ
valiya kayiṟu;atukoṇṭu viṉai ceytaliṉ avar nōṉñāṇviṉaiñar eṉappaṭṭār. kōḷ -
irumpiṟkōttairaiyaip paṟṟukai. iṉi, valiya kayiṟṟaiyuṭaiyavalaiyeṉak koṇṭu, aḵtu
akappaṭuttamai aṟintuīrkkum eṉṟuraittalumām. puṇai tuṇaiyākanīrāṭuvār
nīrāṭṭumuṭintavuṭaṉ ap puṇaiyaiviṭuttu akaṟalpōla, talaivaṉ eṉpāl vantuakaṉṟaṉaṉ eṉpāḷ,
naṭpukēṇmai aṉaittueṉṟāḷ. kēṇmai - puṇaiyiṉiṭattuḷatāya kēṇmai;kuṟippu moḻi. iṉi,
naṭpu oru nāḷ puṇai tuṇaiyākappuṉalāṭiya avvaḷaviṟṟē eṉṟuraittalumām; 1"nīveyyōḷoṭu
vēḻaveṇpuṇai taḻīi.......nerunalāṭiṉaipuṉalē" eṉa muṉ vantamaiyuṅkāṇka.
oṇṭoṭimakaḷir - viṟaliyar; parattaiyarumām, talaivaṉcēripparattaiyoṭu kūṭi yoḻukutalaip
parattaiyarcēriyiṉiṉ ṟeḻum yāḻoliyum muḻavōcaiyumēyāvarumaṟiyap pulappaṭuttum:
aṅṅaṉamākavum talaiviemmoṭu pulattal poruntā teṉpatu tōṉṟa, pāṭavitirppat tōḷ maṇantu
piṟaḷvayiṉāṉeṉavum, emmoṭu pulakku meṉpa eṉavum iṟparattaikūṟiṉāḷeṉka. maṟṟum,
talaivaṉ talaiviyaiyumeṉṉaiyum kaiyakaṉṟu cēripparattaiyar māṭṭumaruvi yoḻukutalāl
eṉakkuḷatāya veṟuppiṉai eṉvaḷaiyai uṭaittuk kāṭṭiṟṟilēṉ; atu koṇṭēavaṉ eṉṉuṭaṉ
uḷaṉākat talaivikarutiṉāḷpōlumeṉpāḷ, “ceṟivaḷai yuṭaittalōilaṉē eṉṟāḷ"

kulamakaḷirallāta alavaṟ peṇṭirmutalāyiṉōr veṟuppiṉāl vaḷaiyuṭaittalceyvareṉpatu,
1"eṉṉōṭutiriyēṉāyiṉ....uṭaikaveṉ, nēriṟai muṉkaivīṅkiya vaḷaiyē" eṉpataṉāla ṟikā
ilaṉ: aṉvikuti taṉmaikkaṇ vantatu. koḻunaṉum; um: acainilai, iṉi,
cēripparattaiyaraiyum viṭuttuttalaiviyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu uṟaivāṉeṉpatu tōṉṟat 'taṉuṭaṉuṟai
pakai' eṉṟāḷ; uṭaṉuṟainta koḻunaṉpakaiyātal cālum eṉak kūṭṭiyurait talumām,

187. pālai
[pirivuṇarttiyatōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu. talaimakaṉpiriviṉkaṇ talaimakaṭkut tōḻi
colliyatūumām.]

(co - ḷ.) 10. tōḻi-,
5. kātalar - namkātalar;
1-4. tōḷ pulampuakala nammoṭu tuñci - namatu tōḷ taṉimai yakalanammuṭaṉ tuyiṉṟu, nāḷ
pala nīṭiya karantu uṟaipuṇarcci - palanāḷ nīṭṭitta kaḷavuppuṇarcciyai,nāṇ uṭaimaiyiṉ
nīṅki - viṉaiyiṉṟiyiruttaṟku nāṇutal uṭaimaiyāṉē nīṅki, cēynāṭṭu arum poruḷ
valitta neñcamoṭu - tūranāṭṭilē ariya pōruḷai īṭṭat tuṇintaneñcattuṭaṉ;
14-24. maṉai uṟai kōḻi- maṉaiyil vatiyuṅ kōḻiyiṉ, aṇal tāḻpu aṉṉa -toṅkukiṉṟa
tāṭiyai yotta, kavai oṇ taḷirakaruṅkāl yāattu - kavartta oḷḷiya taḷiriṉaiyumkariyaaṭi
yiṉaiyum uṭaiya yā maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya, vēṉilveṟpil kāṉam kāya - vēṉil veppam
vāyntamalaiyai yaṭutta kāṭu kāyntiṭa, muṉai eḻuntu ōṭiyakeṭunāṭṭu ār
iṭai-kāṭṭaraṇiluḷḷārkuṭiyeḻuntōṭiya pāḻ nāṭṭiṉ ariya iṭattē, paṉaiveḷiṟu aruntu
paiṅkaṇ yāṉai - paṉaṅkuruttaittiṉṉum paciya kaṇṇiṉai yuṭaiya yāṉai, oḷ cuṭarmutirā iḷam
katir amaiyattu - oḷḷiya cuṭarmutirāta iḷaiya ñāyiṟṟaiyuṭaiya kālaiyilē, kaṇpaṭupāyal
kai oṭuṅku acai nilai - tuyilumiṭattēceyaloṭuṅki acaintu koṇṭirukkum
nilaiyāṉatu,vāḷ vāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉi tuṟai nīnti -vāḷpōlum vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya cuṟā
mīṉkaḷuḷḷakuḷirnta tuṟaikaḷai nīntic ceṉṟu, nāḷ vēṭṭueḻunta nayaṉil paratavar - nāṭ
kālattē mīṉvēṭṭai kuṟittu eḻunta nayamaṟṟa paratavaruṭaiya, vaikukaṭal ampiyil tōṉṟum -
kaṭaliṭattēporuntiya tōṇi pōlat tōṉṟāniṟkum, maipaṭu mā malai vilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkam
poruntiyaperiya malai kuṟukkiṭṭa pālaiyiṉ kaṇṇē;
10-13. cēṇ ōṅkualantalai ñemaiyattu - neṭuntūram uyarntakāynta ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya ñemai
maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya,āḷ il āṅkaṇ - āḷ vaḻakkaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, kalcērpu iruta cilkuṭi
pākkattu - malaiyaiccārntirunta cila kuṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya pākkattē, elviruntu ayara -
iravil viruntu ceyya, ēmattualki - pātukāvalākat taṅki;
4-5. ēki -toṭarntu ceṉṟu, nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉar - namatuuyarcciyaik karutiṉar;
(ataṉāl).
5-10. kaṟuttōr -ciṉanteḻunta pakaivaratu, tevmuṉai citaittakaṭumparip puravi -
pakaippulam tolaitta kaṭiyacelaviṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷaiyuṭaiya, vārkaḻalpolinta - nīṇṭa
kaḻalāṟ polivuṟṟa, vaṉkaṇmaḻavar - taṟukaṇmaiyiṉai yuṭaiya maḻavar ceyyum,pūntoṭai viḻaviṉ
talaināḷ aṉṉa - pūntoṭaiviḻā eṉṉum viḻāviṉ mutalnāḷai yottapoliviṉaiyuṭaiya, taru
maṇal ñemiriya tirunakarmuṟṟam - koṇarntiṭṭa maṇal paranta aḻakiyamaṉaiyiṉ muṟṟam,
pulampuṟum kollō -taṉimaiyuṟumō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar, nammoṭu nīṭiya karantuṟai puṇarcciyai nāṇuṭaimaiyiṉ nīṅki,
arumporuḷ valittaneñcamoṭu, malai vilaṅkiya curattiṉkaṇṇē,pakkattē elviruntu ayara
ēmattu alki, ēki, namuyarvu uḷḷiṉar; ataṉāl tirunakar muṟṟampulampuṟuṅ kollō?
(vi - rai.) tōḷ pulampu akalat tuñci eṉṟatu, tōḷilētuyiṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi, nāṇuṭaimai -
pōkam vēṇṭiāṭavarkku uyirākiya viṉaiyai yoḻintiruttaṟkunāṇutaluṭaimai. cēy nāṭṭu
arumporuḷ - cēynāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tēṭum ariya poruḷ, nam uyarvu -vaṟiyōrkku
aḷittal, viruntu puṟantarutalmutaliya illaṟam purintu nām mēmpaṭutal;ataṟkup poruḷ
iṉṟiyamaiyātākaliṉ,poruḷvayiṟ pirintārai nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉareṉṟāḷ. ēki uḷḷiṉar
eṉpataṉai uḷḷi ēkiṉareṉa māṟuka. pūntoṭai viḻavu, paṭaikkalam payiṉṟaiḷaiya vīrarai
araṅkēṟṟuvikkum viḻā.toṭai-ampu toṭuttal, talai nāḷaṉṉapoliviṉaiyuṭaiya muṟṟameṉa
viritturaikka. talaivarpiriviṟkuḷatām varuttattai muṟṟamtaṉimaiyuṟutalāl varuntuvāḷ
pōṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.alantalai - alanta talai; vikāram. ñemaiyattu; attu,cāriyai. curattaik
kaṭantu ēkuṅkāl, pākkattuelviruntayara ēmattu alki ēkuvār eṉṟāḷ.kāṉaṅkōḻiyiṉ
vēṟupaṭukka, maṉaiyuṟai kōḻieṉappaṭṭatu. muṉai - kāṭṭaraṇ; vēṭṭuvar ūr.
keṭunāṭu-vāḻvā roḻintu pāḻpaṭṭa nāṭu.vāḷvāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉittuṟai nīnti
eṉṟatu,paratavariṉ añacāmaiyaip pulappaṭuttiyavāṟām. paṉaiveḷiṟu aruntu eṉṟatu, vēṟu
uṇaviṉmaikuṟittapaṭiyām. yāṉai acainilai. kaṭal ampiyiṟṟōṉṟum curam eṉavum, malai
vilaṅkiya curameṉavum taṉittaṉi iyaiyum.

188. kuṟiñci
[iravil ciṟaippuṟamākattōḻi colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1.peruṅkaṭal mukanta iru kiḷai koṇmū - periya kaṭalnīrai mukantukoṇṭa
periya kūṭṭamākiya mēkamēnītāṉ;
2-8. iruṇṭu -iruṭciyuṟṟu, uyar vicumpiṉ valaṉ ērpu vaḷaii -uyarnta vāṉil valamāka
eḻuntu cuṟṟi, pōrppuuṟumuraciṉ iraṅki - tōṟpōrvaiyuṟṟa muracupōlamuḻaṅki. muṟai
purintu aṟaṉ neṟi piḻaiyāt tiṟaṉaṟi maṉṉar - ceṅkōṉmai mēṟkoṇṭu aṟaneṟivaḻuvāta
ceyyuḷ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉai aṟinta maṉṉaratu,arum camattu etirnta perum cey āṭavar -
ariyapōril māṟṟāruṭaṉ etirttup pōrceyyum pērāṇmaic ceykaiyaiyuṭaiya
vīrarkaḷ,kaḻittu eṟi vāḷiṉ naḷippaṉa viḷaṅkum -uṟaiyiṉiṉṟum uruvi vīcum vāḷaip
pōlacceṟivaṉavāy viḷaṅkum, miṉ uṭaik karuviyai ākimiṉṉiṉ tokutiyai uṭaiyai āki:
nāḷum aravamkoṉṉē ceytiyō - nāṭōṟum payaṉiṉṟiāravāram ceytu oḻitiyō? aṉṟi;

8-14. poṉ eṉamalarnta vēṅkai mali toṭar aṭaicci - poṉpōla malarnta vēṅkaip pūkkaḷālāya
niṟaintamālaiyait tarittu, polinta āyamoṭu kāṇtakaiyali - polivuṟṟa āyattāruṭaṉ aḻaku
poruntanaṭantu, taḻalai vāṅkiyum taṭṭai ōppiyum -taḻaliṉaic cuṟṟiyum taṭṭaiyiṉait
taṭṭiyum,aḻal ēr ceyalai amtaḻai acaiiyum - tīyiṉkoḻuntiṉai otta acōkiṉatu aḻakiya
taḻaiyālāyauṭaiyiṉai yuṭuttum, kuṟamakaḷ kākkum - kuṟamakaḷāyaem talaivi kākkum, ēṉal
puṟamum maḻaitarutiyō-tiṉaiyiṉiṭattu maḻaiyum poḻivaiyō!vāḻiya.
(muṭipu) koṇmūvē!nītāṉ iruṇṭu ērpu vaḷaii iraṅki viḷaṅkummiṉṉuṭaik karuviyai
āki, aravam koṉṉēceytiyō; kuṟamakaḷ kākkum ēṉalpuṟam maḻaiyumtarutiyō vāḻiya!
(vi - rai.) muṟaipurintu piḻaiyā maṉṉar eṉavum, tiṟaṉ aṟimaṉṉar eṉavum taṉittaṉi
kūṭṭuka. iṟaimāṭciyāvum aṭaṅkat tiṟaṉ aṟi maṉṉar eṉṟaṉaḷ.camam-am maṉṉar tam māṟṟāruṭaṉ
ceyyumpōr. peruñ cey āṭavar - vañciṉaṅkūṟippōr ceyyum pērāṇmaicceykaiyuṭaiyavīrar.
malarnta vēṅkai malitoṭaraṭaicci eṉṟataṉāl tiṉai viḷaiyuṅ kālampeṟappaṭṭatu. taḻal -
pirampu pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṟpiṉṉic cuṟṟi oliyuṇṭākkik kiḷiyaiōṭṭuvatōr karuvi. taṭṭai -
mūṅkiṟ ṟuṇṭaippiḷantu taṭṭi oliyuṇṭākkik kiḷi kaṭivatorukaruvi. ivaṟṟait “taḻalum
taṭṭaiyuṅ kuḷirumpiṟavum, kiḷikaṭi maramiṉa ūḻūḻ vāṅki" eṉpataṉuraiyāl aṟika. taḻai -
taḻai uṭai. ēṉal puṟam maḻaiyuntarutiyō eṉa ummaiyai māṟuka.
ummaiyaiacaiyākkiyum maḻaiyai viḷiyākkiyum ēṉalaippuṟantarutiyō eṉṟuraittalumām.
itaṟkuk koṇmūākiya maḻaiyē eṉka. iraviṟ ciṟaippuṟamākavuḷḷatalaivaṉait tōḻi iṅṅaṉam
maḻaiyākakkoṇṭukuṟippiṉālē nī iṅṅaṉam vantu oḻukutalāṟpayaṉiṉṟi alar viḷaikkiṉṟāyō?
aṉṟivaraintukoḷvaiyō? eṉak kūṟi varaivu kaṭāyiṉāḷeṉka.
(mē - ḷ.) 2'uṭaṉuṟaiyuvamañ cuṭṭunakai ciṟappeṉak keṭalaru marapiṉuḷḷuṟai aintē' eṉṉuñ
cūttirattuc cuṭṭueṉṉum uḷḷuṟaikku icceyyuḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi,'itaṉuḷ' koṉṉē
ceytiyō aravam eṉpataṉālpayaṉiṉṟi alar viḷaittiyō eṉavum kūṟi, ēṉaṟpuṟamum tarutiyō
eṉpataṉāl, varaintu koḷvaiyōeṉavum kūṟit talaimakaṉai maḻaimēl vaittu
kūṟaliṉcuṭṭāyiṟṟu. koṉṉē ceytiyō eṉṟataṉālvaḻuvāyiṉum varaital vēṭkaiyāṟ
kūṟiṉamaiyiṉamaintatu' eṉpar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

189. pālai
[makaṭ pōkkiya cevilicolliyatu]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. pacu paḻam palaviṉ kāṉam vempi - cevvivāynta paḻattiṉaikkoṇṭa
palāmaraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyakāṭu vetumpa, vicumpu kaṇ aḻiya vēṉil nīṭi -mēkam vāṉiṭattiṉiṉ
ṟoḻitaliṉ vēṉilveppam mika, kayam kaṇ aṟṟa kal ōṅku vaippiṉ- kuḷaṅkaḷ tammiṭattē
nīraṟṟirukkum kaṟkaḷuyarnta iṭaṅkaḷil, nāṟu uyir maṭa piṭi taḻīi -perumūccut tōṉṟum
iḷaiya piṭiyait taḻuvi, kaḷiṟu -kaḷiṟukaḷ, muḻavu eṭuttu uyari vēṟu nāṭṭu
viḻavuppaṭarmaḷḷariṉ - taṅkaḷ muḻavukaḷai eṭuttuuyarttukkoṇṭu vēṟṟunāṭṭil
nikaḻumviḻāviṉai niṉaittuc cellummaḷḷarkaḷaippōla, atarppaṭutta -neṟippaṭutticceṉṟa,
kal uyar - kaṟkaḷuyarnta, vevvarai kavāaṉ attam cuṭṭi - koṭiyapakka malaiyaic cārnta
curattiṉaic cellattuṇintu;
8-15. vayalaiampiṇaiyal vārnta kavāaṉ - vayalaikkoṭiyālākiya aḻakiya taḻaiyuṭai
tāḻntatuṭaiyiṉaiyum, titalai alkul - tēmal paṭarntaalkuliṉaiyum uṭaiya, kuṟumakaḷ -
iḷaiyaḷāya eṉ makaḷ,paiyeṉa avaṉoṭu ceṉṟu - melleṉat taṉtalaivaṉoṭuceṉṟu, piṟaḷ ākiya
aḷavai - eṅkaṭkup piṟaḷorutti ākiyapōtu, eṉṟum paṭarmalievvamoṭu - ekkālattum
niṉaivu mikkatuṉpamoṭu, mātiram tuḻaii - ticaiyellāmtēṭivantu, maṉai maruṇṭu irunta
eṉṉiṉummaṉaiyiṉkaṇ mayaṅkiyirunta eṉṉaik kāṭṭilum,naṉai makiḻ naṉṉarāḷar
kūṭukoḷiṉṉiyam-kaḷḷiṉ kaḷippiṉaiyuṭaiya nallapāṇarkaḷatu oṉṟukūṭi yolikkum
vācciyaṅkaḷ,tēr ūr teruvil tatumpum-tēr ōṭum perunterukkaḷil aṟātu olikkum,
ūr-ivvūrāṉatu, taṉvīḻvu uṟu poruḷ - taṉatu viruppam mikkatoruporuḷai, iḻantaṉṟu -
iḻantatāyiṟṟu.
(muṭipu.) kavāaṉ attam cuṭṭi, kuṟumakaḷ, avaṉoṭu ceṉṟuemakkup piṟaḷākiya aḷavai,
eṉṉiṉum ūr taṉvīḻvuṟu poruḷ iḻantaṉṟu.
(vi - rai.) vempi, nīṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟai vempa, nīṭaeṉattirikka. kayam-cuṉaiyumām. maḷḷar
eṉṟatuīṇṭuk kūttarai. muḻaveṭut tuyari vēṟunāṭṭuviḻavuppaṭar maḷḷar eṉa māṟuka.
poruḷiṉkaṇmaṭappiṭi taḻuvi eṉpataṉai uvamaiyuṭaṉ kūṭṭiviṟaliyaruṭaṉ cellum kūttar
eṉavum,uvamaiyiṉkaṇ muḻaveṭuttuyari eṉpataṉaipporuḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭi muḻavu pōlum
palāppaḻattiṉaiēntic celluṅ kaḷiṟu eṉavum uraikka. piṟantanāṭṭoṭṭup pēṇi vaḷartta
emmaik kaiyakaṉṟuētilāṉoṭu ceṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, 'avaṉoṭu ceṉṟupiṟaḷ ākiya aḷavai, eṉṟāḷ.
avaḷ aṉṉaḷ āyiṉumavaḷ pirivāl yāṉ eytum tuṉpam iṟappavumperitu eṉpāḷ, 'eṉṟum,
paṭarmali evvamoṭu,,mātirin tuḻaii maṉai maruṇṭirunta eṉṉiṉum'eṉṟum tāṉ eytun
tuṉpattiṉum nāṟṟāyumāyattārum mutaliyōr, avaḷai iḻantu eytumtuṉpam periteṉpāḷ,
'eṉṉiṉum,ūriḻantaṉṟutaṉ vīḻvuṟu poruḷē' eṉṟumkūṟiṉāḷ. ūr eṉṟatu īṇṭu, naṟṟāy
āyattārmutaliyōraik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.

190. neytal
[tōḻi,cevilittāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6. aṉṉai - tāyē, vāḻi-, vēṇṭu-nāṉ kūṟuvataṉaivirumpik kēṭpāyāka;
6. 10. uyarcimaipotumpil - uyarnta ucciyaiyuṭaiyacōlaiyilpuṉṉai ciṉai cērpu
irunta - puṉṉaimarattiṉkiḷaiyaic cērntirunta, vampa nārai iriya - putiyanārai
oḻintiṭa, oru nāḷ - muṉpu oru nāḷ, poṅkuvaral ūtaiyoṭu puṇari alaippavum -
poṅkutaluṭaṉvarum vaṭakāṟṟuṭaṉ alaikaḷ alaittukkoṇṭirukkavum, uḻai kaṭal vaḻaṅkalum
uriyaṉ - orutalaivaṉ kaṭalōrattē tēril varutaṟkum uriyaṉāyiṉaṉ;
10-17. ataṉ talai -ataṉmēlum, iru kaḻip pukāar - periyakaḻiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
tuṟaimukattē, vayaccuṟā poruntattākki eṟinteṉa - valiya cuṟāmīṉ tam uṭampiṟporuntat
tākki eṟinta tāka, valavaṉ aḻippa -tērppākaṉ celaviṉai niṟuttalāl, eḻil
payamkuṉṟiya - eḻucciyum payaṉum kuṉṟiyaṉavum, ciṟaiaḻi toḻila - pūṭṭu aḻinta
ceykaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉavumākiya; nirai maṇip puravi - niraittamaṇimālai pūṇṭa kutiraikaḷ,
virai naṭai tavira -viraintu cellum celavu oḻintu taṅkiṉavāka, iḻumeṉ kāṉal -
iḻumeṉṉum oliyaiyuṭaiya kāṉaliṭattē,viḻumaṇal acaii-ciṟanta maṇalil taṅki, āyntapariyaṉ
ivaṇ vantu - vēṟu ciṟanta puraviyuṭaṉ iṅkuvantu, māṉṟa mālai cērntaṉṟu ilaṉ -
mayaṅkiyamālaip poḻutil taṅkiyatum ilaṉ;
5. alamaralmaḻaikkaṇ amarntum nōkkāḷ - avaṉaiccuḻalkiṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya niṉ
makaḷnōkkiṉāḷum allaḷ; aṅṅaṉamākavum;
1-4. tirai uḻantuacaiiya - tiraiyiṉ viḷaiyāṭi uḻantamaiyāltaḷarcciyuṟṟa, niraivaḷai
āyamoṭu - niraittavaḷaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya makaḷir kūṭṭattuṭaṉ, (maintarkaḷ)uppiṉ kuppai ēṟi -
uppu mēṭṭil ēṟi niṉṟu,elpaṭa - iruḷvara, varu timil eṇṇum tuṟaivaṉoṭu -karaikku
mīṇṭu varum paṭakukaḷai eṇṇum tuṟaiyaiyuṭaiyaat talaivaṉoṭu niṉ makaḷaic cārtti
ūr-intaūrāṉatu, oru taṉ koṭumaiyiṉ - oppaṟṟa taṉatukoṭumaiyāl, alar pāṭum - alar
kūṟā niṟkum;
6. alaiyal - ātalālnī avaḷai varuttātēkoḷ.
(muṭipu) aṉṉai! vāḻi! vēṇṭu; oru talaivaṉ; orunāḷ ūtaiyoṭupuṇari alaippavum
uḻaikkaṭal vaḻaṅkalum uriyaṉ;ataṉ talai vayaccuṟā eṟinteṉa; valavaṉ aḻippapuravikaḷ virai
naṭai tavira, acaiiyaāyntapariyiṉaṉ, mālai ivaṇ cērntaṉṟō ilaṉ,maḻaikkaṇ niṉmakaḷ avaṉai
amarntunōkkiṉāḷallaḷ; aṅṅaṉamākavum niṉmakaḷaiattuṟaivaṉoṭu cārtti; ivvūr
taṉkoṭumaiyiṉ alarpāṭum; ātaliṉ nī ivaḷaialaiyal.
(vi - rai.) āyamoṭu eṉṟamaiyāl, maintar eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. eṇṇum eṉṉum
peyareccam, tuṟai eṉṉumiṭappeyar koṇṭatu. tuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉoṭucārtti eṉa
viritturaikka. ūr eṉpatu -kauvaip peṇṭirai. maḻaik kaṇ: āku peyar.vaḻaṅkalum eṉṟa ummai
varutalarumaiyai viḷakkiniṉṟatu. ataṉtalai eṉṟatu; ūtaiyoṭu puṇari ataitalēaṉṟi
ataṉmēlum eṉṟapaṭi, vayac cuṟā eṟinteṉavalavaṉ aḻippa eṉṟataṉāl, kutirai
puṇpaṭṭamaipeṟṟām. puṇpaṭṭamaiyāl valavaṉpūṭṭaviḻttuviṭṭa kutirai naṭaitavirntirukka,avaṉ
maṇalil ōriṭattē taṅkiyiruntataṉṟi vēṟukutirai pūṇṭa tēruṭaṉ yāṅkaḷ
irukkumiṭattiṟkuvantu iruṇṭa mālaippoḻutil taṅkiṉāṉallaṉeṉṟum, talaiviyum avaṉai
virumpinōkkiṉāḷallaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi, aṅṅaṉamākaliṉūr vāḷātu alar kūṟuvatu paṟṟi nī
talaiviyaivaruttātē eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷpōl, talaivaṉetirppāṭṭaik kuṟippiṟ
pulappaṭuttittōḻi. cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷ eṉka.

191. pālai
[talaimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc collic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13. eṉ neñcē vāḻi-,
1-12. attam pātirituy talai putu vī - pālaiyiluḷḷa tuyyiṉai ucciyiṟkoṇṭa putiya
pātirip pūvai, eri itaḻ alari yoṭuiṭai paṭa viraii - neruppup pōṉṟa
itaḻiṉaiyuṭaiyaalarippūvuṭaṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kalantu, veṇtōṭṭuttoṭutta vaṇṭupaṭu kaṇṇi -
veṇmai poruntiyatāḻampūviṉ tōṭṭil vaittuk kaṭṭiya vaṇṭuporuntum kaṇṇiyiṉait
taritta, tōl putaiciraṟṟu aṭi-potinta ceruppu olikkumaṭiyiṉaiyum,
kōl-kōliṉaiyumuṭaiya, umaṇar-uppuvāṇikaruṭaiya, ūr kaṉṭaṉṉa-ūrtiraṇṭuvantālotta, āram
vāṅki-vaṇṭikaḷaiiḻuttu, arum curam ivarnta-ēṟutaṟku ariyamēṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭilēṟi
vanta, acaivu il nōṉtāḷ tiruntu pakaṭṭu iyampumkoṭumaṇi-taḷarvillāta valiya
tāḷiṉaiyuṭaiyacevviya erutukaḷiṉ olikkum vaḷainta maṇikaḷiṉoli, purintu avar maṭi
viṭu vīḷaiyoṭu-avarkaḷvirumpi vāyiṉai maṭittu eḻuppum cīḻkkaiyoliyoṭu kaṭitu etir
ōṭi-viraintu etirēceṉṟu,ōmai am peruṅkāṭṭu varuum vampalarkku-ōmaimaraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya
periya kāṭṭil varum putiya
( pāṭam ) 1.vaṇṭōṭṭu.
2. vavvi.
3. uraiyiṉi.
4.urōṭōkakkavuṇiyaṉ cēntaṉ.
----------
rāya vaḻic celvārkku, ēmamceppum-pātukāvalaik kūṟum, eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭai-veppam mikka
neṭiya kāṭṭilē, arumporuḷnacaii-aritu īṭṭum poruḷai virumpi, pirintuuṟai
valli-nam talaiviyaip pirintu uṟaiyumvaṉmai yuṭaiyaiyāy, ceṉṟu
viṉaieṇṇutiyāyiṉ-ceṉṟu poruḷ īṭṭum viṉaiyaieṇṇuvaiyāṉāl;
13-17. uṟal iṉcāyal-uṟutaṟkiṉiya meṉmaittaṉmaiyaiyum, niraimukai mullai
aruntu-mullaiyiṉ niraitta arumpukaḷainiṟaiyac cūṭi,
melliyavāki-meṉmaittaṉmaiyuṭaiyavāki, aṟal eṉa virinta oli irumkūntal-karumaṇalpōla
virinta taḻaitta kariyakūntalaiyumuṭaiya nam talaivi, tēṟumeṉa-teḷintiruppāḷ eṉṟu,
valiya kūṟavumvallaiyō-pirivuṇarttum valiya coṟkaḷaikkūṟavum vaṉmai yuṭaiyaiyō?
vallaiyāyiṉ;
12-13. naṉṟumuraitticiṉ-avaḷiṭattē ceṉṟu peritum kūṟiavaḷait tēṟṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) umaṇaratu ūrkaṇṭaṉṉa āram vāṅkic curamivarnta pakaṭṭu iyampum koṭumaṇi;
avarvīḷaiyoṭu etir ōṭi vampalarkku ēmam ceppumeṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭaip poruḷ nacaiic ceṉṟu
viṉaieṇṇutiyāyiṉ; iṉcāyalum kūntalumuṭaiya ivaḷtēṟum eṉa valiya kūṟavum vallaiyō?
vallaiyāyiṉnaṉṟum uraitticiṉ.
(vi-rai.) tōṭu-tāḻampūviṉ itaḻ. ciraṟṟu tōl eṉakkoṇṭu citaṟiya ceruppu
eṉṟalumām. kōl-pakaṭuōṭṭuṅkōl. āram-vaṇṭiyiṉ uṟuppu; vaṇṭikku-ākupeyar. mikap
palavākiya vaṇṭikaḷuṭaṉ umaṇartiraṇṭu cellun tōṟṟam ūr eḻuntu celluvatupōlumākaliṉ,
umaṇar ūrkaṇṭaṉṉa āram eṉṟār.pakaṭṭiṉ maṇi yōcaiyaiyum umaṇar vīḷaiyoliyaiyum cevi yēṟṟa
vampalar ikkāṭṭilētam illai yeṉṟu uṇarvarākaliṉ, avvolikaḷvampalarkku ēmam ceppum
eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vallivaliyaiyāy eṉṉum poruṭṭu ; vavvi eṉpatupāṭamāyiṉ, pirintuṟaitalai
maṉattiṟ koṇṭueṉka. poruḷ nacaii nīḷiṭaic ceṉṟu viṉaieṇṇutiyāyiṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka.
aruntu - ārntueṉpataṉ vikāram. tēṟu meṉa eṉṟataṉāl, valiyaeṉṟatu. poruḷīṭṭi
viraintu varuvēm eṉṉuñcoṟkaḷātal vēṇṭum. kūṟavum vallaiyō eṉṟatukūṟutaṟku arumaiyai
viḷakki niṉṟata. maṟṟu,vallaiyallai eṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ viṉaimāṟṟu; acaiyumām.
---------
192. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi, talaimakaṉaic 1ceṟippuaṟivuṟīi iravukkuṟi maṟuttatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13-15. uravup peyal urum iṟaikoṇṭa-valiya maḻaiyuṭaṉiṭi taṅkutal koṇṭa,
uyarcimai perumalai nāṭa -uyarnta ucciyaiyuṭaiya periya malaināṭṭaiyuṭaiyatalaivaṉē;
3-8. vicumpileyyā varivil aṉṉa paintār cevvāy ciṟukiḷi - vāṉiluḷḷa eyyap
peṟāta aḻakiya villaiotta paciya mālaiyiṉaiyum civanta vāyiṉaiyumuṭaiya ciṟiya kiḷi,
citaiya vāṅki tiṉaicitaiyumpaṭi koytu, poṟai melintiṭṭapulpuṟap peru kural -
cumakkalāṟṟātu pōkaṭṭapulliya puṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya periya katiriṉai, vaḷai ciṟaivāraṇam
kiḷaiyoṭu kavara-vaḷainta ciṟakukaḷai yuṭaiyakāṉaṅkōḻi taṉ iṉattuṭaṉ kavarntuṇṇumāṟu,ēṉalum
iṟaṅku poṟai uyirttaṉa - tiṉaiyumvaḷainta katirkaḷai īṉṟaṉa (ākaliṉ em talaivi
iṉiiṟceṟikkappaṭuvaḷ);
8-12. pālnāḷ nīvantatu aḷikkuvai eṉiṉē. pāti iravil nī vantuaḷippāy eṉṟu kūṟiṉ,
māl varai mai paṭu viṭarakamtuḻaii - periya malaiyiṉ iruḷ poruntiya kukaiyiṉiṭaṅkaḷait
tuḻāvi, oy eṉa aruvitanta aravu umiḻtirumaṇi - viraiya aruvikoṇṭuvanta
pāmpuumiḻnta aḻakiya maṇi, peruvarai ciṟu kuṭi maṟukuviḷakkuṟuttaliṉ - periya
malaiyiluḷḷa ematucīṟūriṉ teruviṉai iruḷakaṟṟi viḷaṅkacceytalālē,
15. niṉ malarntamārpu - niṉ akaṉṟa mārpiṉ kūṭṭattiṉai;
13. iravum iḻantaṉaḷ- iraviṉum iḻantāḷ āvaḷ;
1-3. mati arumpu aṉṉa- iḷaiya matiyiṉai yotta, mācu aṟu cuṭar nutal ivaḷatukuṟṟamaṟṟa
oḷḷiya neṟṟi, poṉ nēr vaṇṇamkoṇṭaṉṟu-poṉṉai yotta niṟattiṉaikkoṇṭatu; aṉṉō -
antō, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kol -eṅṅaṉam ākuvaḷō;
11. aḷiyaḷ -iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) perumalai nāṭa! ēṉal poṟai uyirttaṉa; (ākaliṉem talaivi iṉi
iṟceṟikkappaṭuvāḷ;) nīpālnāḷ vantu aḷikkuvai eṉiṉē, aravumiḻtirumaṇi maṟuku
viḷakkuṟuttaliṉ, niṉmār piṉaiiravum iḻantaṉaḷ; cuṭarnutal poṉ nēr vaṇṇamkoṇṭaṉṟu;
aṉṉō yāṅkākuvaḷ kol; aḷiyaḷ.
(vi - rai.) nutal poṉnēr vaṇṇam koṇṭaṉṟu eṉṟatu pacalaipūttatu eṉṟapaṭi. vicumpiṉ
vil eṉka. eyyāvil eṉṟatu vāṉa villiṟku veḷippaṭai. vari -aḻaku. ēṉal poṟai
uyirttaṉa eṉṟatu,tiṉaiyaṟukkuṅ kālamākaliṉ talaiviiṟceṟikkappaṭuvaḷ eṉpatu kūṟi,
ataṉālpakaṟkuṟi ariteṉṟu uṇarttiyavāṟām. tirumaṇimaṟuku viḷakkuṟuttaliṉ eṉpataṉāl
iravukkuṟiyiṭaiyīṭu uṇartti, ataṉāl aḵtum ariteṉauṇarttuvāḷ niṉ mārpiṉai iravu
iḻantaṉaḷeṉṟāḷ. iravum eṉṉum ummai eccap poruṭṭu.tāṉē eṉpatu kaṭṭuraic cuvaipaṭa
niṉṟatu.
193. pālai
[poruḷa valittaneñciṟkuc collit talaimakaṉ celavuaḻuṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-11. neñcē, kāṉ uyar maruṅkil kavalai allatu -uyarnta kāṭṭiṉiṭattēyuḷḷa
kavartta neṟikaḷaiyaṉṟi, vāṉam vēṇṭā vil ēr uḻavar-maḻaiyaivēṇṭutalillāta villākiya
ērāl uḻutalaicceyyumāṟalaippōr, kiḷai eḻa vāytta-tamkiḷaiñaruṭaṉ eḻāniṟkak kiṭaitta,
perunāḷvēṭṭam - naṉṉāḷ vēṭṭattil, poru kaḷattuoḻinta-avarkaḷ poruta kaḷattil āṟu
celvāruṭampiṉiṉṟum ukka, kuruti cevvāy -kurutiyiṉai uṇṭamaiyāl civanta
vāyiṉaiyum,poṟitta pōlum vāl niṟa eruttiṉ -poṟittu vaittāṟpōlum veṇṇiṟam
vāyntakaḻuttiṉaiyum, aṇinta pōlum ceñcevi eruvaiceytu cūṭṭiyatu pōlum civanta
ceviyiṉaiyumuṭaiyakaḻuku, kuṟumpoṟai eḻunta neṭuntāḷ yāattu - kuṟiyamalaiyiṭattē
vaḷarnta neṭiya aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya yāmarattiṉ, aruṅ kavaṭṭu uyarciṉaip piḷḷai ūṭṭa- ariya
kavaṭṭiṉniṉ ṟeḻunta uyarntakiḷaikaḷiluḷḷa taṉ kuñciṉai uṇpikka, viraintuvāy vaḻukkiya
koḻu kaṇ ūṉ taṭi - viraitaliṉālēak kuñciṉ vāyiṉiṉṟum tavaṟi vīḻntakoḻumaiyuṟṟa
kaṇṇākiya ūṉiṉ tuṇṭu, tolpacimutunari valci ākum curaṉ - paḻaiya
paciyiṉaiyuṭaiyamutiya narikku uṇavākum kāṭukaḷ, namakku eḷiya maṉ -nām cellutaṟku
mikavum eḷiyaṉavē āyiṉum;
11-4. nam maṉai-namatunalla maṉaiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa, pal māṇ taṅkiya- palamāṇpum uṟṟa, cāyal -
meṉmaittaṉmaiyiṉaiyum,iṉmoḻi - iṉiya moḻiyiṉaiyum, muruntu ērmuṟuval -
mayiliṟakiṉ aṭiyiṉai otta paṟkaḷaiyumuṭaiya,iḷaiyōḷ - iḷaiyōḷāya nam talaiviyiṉ,perutōḷ
iṉ tuyil - periya tōḷiṉpāleytum iṉiya tuyiliṉai, kaiviṭukalaṉ -
kaiviṭutalaiāṟṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! curaṉ namakku eḷiyamaṉ; āyiṉum, iḷaiyōḷ peruntōḷ iṉtuyil
kaiviṭukalaṉ.
(vi - rai.) uḻutoḻil ceyvārkku maḻai tuṇaik kāraṇamāmeṉpatu, 1'ēriṉuḻāar uḻavar
puyaleṉṉum, vāri vaḷaṅkuṉṟikkāl eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām; āyiṉum,āṟalaippārākiya villēr
uḻavarkkuk kāṭṭiṉkavarneṟi yallatu maḻai tuṇaiyātal illaiyākaliṉ,'kavalai yallatu vāṉam
vēṇṭā villēr uḻavar'eṉṟār. avarkku vēṭṭai peritum vāyttanāḷ ciṟanta nāḷ ākaliṉ,
atu perunāḷeṉappaṭṭatu. kiḷai yeḻa eṉpataṟku āṟu celvārkiḷaiyuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu vara
eṉṟuraittalumām.kurutic cevvāy - kurutiyai yuṇṭamaiyāṟ civantavāy eṉa
viritturaikka. aṇintapōlumceñcevi eṉṟa karuttu, 2'ūṉpatittaṉṉa veruvaru ceñcevi
eruvaic cēval' eṉamuṉṉarp pōntamaiyuṅ kāṇka.
yāmarattiṉuyarcci mikuti kūṟuvār neṭuntāḷ yāattueṉṟum, aruṅkavaṭṭu uyar ciṉai
eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.viraintu ūṭṭa eṉṟu kūṭṭi yuraittalumām.
eruvai kuñciṟkukkaṇṇiṉaik koṇarntu uṇavākak koṭuppatu, 3'kaṇṇumiḻkaḻukiṉ kāṉam'
eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum, tolpaci - palnāḷ uṇaviṉmaiyāl eytiya paci. kolpaci eṉṉum
pāṭattiṟku varuttum paciyeṉṟuraikka. talaivaṉ curattiṟ cellutaṟkuañcukiṉṟilēṉ
eṉpāṉ, 'curaṉ namakku eḷiyamaṉ' eṉṟum, eṉiṉum talaiviyiṉ tōḷiṟpeṟum iṉiya
tuyilaik kaiviṭṭup pōtal iyalāteṉpāṉ 'iḷaiyōḷ peruntōḷ iṉṟuyilkaiviṭukalaṉē'
eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. avaḷkaiviṭaṟkuariyāḷ eṉpataṉaip 'paṉ māṇ taṅkiya cāyaliṉmoḻi
muruntēr muṟuval iḷaiyōḷ'eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ.

194. mullai
[paruvaṅ kaṇṭu āṟṟāmaimītūrat talaimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16-9. tōḻi-, pōrmika-pōrmikku mūṇṭatāka,koṭuñci neṭutēr
pūṇṭa-koṭuñci yeṉṉumuṟuppiṉai yuṭaiya neṭiya tēriṟ pūṭṭappeṟṟa,kaṭumpari viri uḷai
nalmāṉ kaṭaiiya-viraintacelaviṉaiyum virinta piṭari mayiriṉaiyum uṭaiyanalla kutiraiyaic
celutti, varutum eṉṟu avarteḷitta pōḻtu - iṉṉa pōḻtil varuvēm eṉṟunam talaivar
terivitta kālam;
1-16. pēr uṟaitalaiiya periya maḻaipeyta, perum pular vaikaṟai-iruḷperitum pularnta
kālaip poḻutilē, ēr iṭampaṭutta irumaṟu-ērkaḷāl iṭamuṇṭāka uḻappeṟṟaperiya
vaṭuviṉaiyuṭaiya, pūḻi puṟam māṟupeṟṟa - kīḻmēlākap puraṇṭa puḻutiyaiyuṭaiya, pūval
īrattu -cemmaṇnilattiṉ īrampaṭṭa cevviyil, ūṉkiḻittaṉṉa cem cuval neṭucāl -
ūṉaikkiḻittālotta cevviya mēṭṭunilattaippiḷantu ceṉṟa neṭiya paṭaiccāliṉiṭattu,
vittiyamaruṅkil - vitaitta iṭaṅkaḷil, vitai palanāṟiya-vitaikaḷ palavum muḷaittu vaḷara,
iṟalainalmāṉ iṉam parantavaipōl - nalla kalaimāṉiṉkūṭṭam parantaṉapōla, kōṭu uṭai talaikkuṭai
cūṭiyaviṉaiñar - kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya ōlaik kuṭaiyaittalaiyiṟ cūṭiya kollai uḻavar, kaṟaṅku
paṟaiccīriṉ - olikkum paṟaiyiṉ oliyoṭu, iṟaṅkavāṅki kaḷai kāl kaḻīiya - payirkaḷ
vaḷaiyumpaṭiotukkik kaḷaiyaip paṟittut tūymai ceyta,perum puṉa varakiṉ - periya
kollaiyil viḷaintavarakiṉ, kavai katir irupuṟam katūu uṇṭa - kavaittakatirkaḷiṉ kariya
puṟattiṉaip paṟṟi uṇṭa, kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭu mā tōkai- kuṭumi poruntiya
talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa kariyamayil, kāmar kalavam parappi - aḻakiya tōkaiyiṉaivirittu, ēm
uṟa - iṉpam uṟa, kollai uḻavarkūḻ niḻal oḻitta - kollaiyai uḻumviṉaiñarkaḷ kūḻ uṇṭaṟku
niḻalāka viṭṭuvaitta,val ilaik kuruntiṉ vāṅkuciṉai iruntu - valiyailaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
kuruntamarattiṉ vaḷaintakiḷaiyiliruntu, kiḷikaṭi makaḷiriṉ viḷipaṭapayirum - kiḷikaḷai
ōṭṭum makaḷirpōlaoliyuṇṭāka akavum, kār itu - kār kālam ākiyaituvēyākum. maṉ -
aṅṅaṉamākavum avar iṉṉumvantilarē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talaivar varutum eṉṟu teḷittapōḻtukār itumaṉ; varakiṉ katir uṇṭa
tōkai kalavamparappik kurun tiṉ ciṉaiyiruntu kiḷikaṭimakaḷiriṉ viḷipap payirum kār
eṉka.
(vi - rai.) ēriṭam paṭutta irumaṟup pūḻip puṟamāṟu peṟṟa pūvaleṉṟatu, puḻuti
kīḻmēlākap puraṇṭu naṉku pularumāṟupaṉ muṟai uḻuta cemmaṇ nilam eṉṟapaṭi;
1'toṭippuḻutikaḵcāuṇak kiṟ piṭitteruvum-vēṇṭātu cālappaṭum' eṉa vaḷḷuvar kūṟu
vatuṅ kāṇka. aṅṅaṉamuṇakkiya puḻutiyil maḻaipeytu īram paṭṭa cevvipārttu mīṭṭum
uḻutu vitaiteḷippār eṉpatu,'pūval īrattu, ūṉkiḻit taṉṉa ceñcuvalneṭuñcāl,
vittiya' eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.īramuṭaiya cemmaṇnilattē uḻupaṭai kiḻitta vaṭuviṉtōṟṟam,
ūṉiṉaik kiḻittu vaittārpōlumeṉṟār. nāṟi - nāṟa eṉat tirikka; nāṟa-muḷaittavaḷara
eṉka. maḻaikkut taṭaiyāka viṉaiñar talaiyiṟcūṭiya ōlaikkuṭaiyiṉ iru muṉaiyum
nīṇṭuuyarntirukku mākaliṉ ataṉaiyuṭaiya viṉaiñarkaḷiralaiyiṉam pōlvārāyiṉar eṉka. kaṟaṅku
paṟaiccīriṉ eṉṟa iralaiyiṉam pōlvārāyiṉar eṉka.kaṟaṅku paṟaic cīriṉ eṉṟataṉāl,
avarkaḷkaḷaipaṟikkuṅkāl, paṟaiyai olippippar eṉpatupeṟṟām. kaḷaikāl kaḻīiya-kaḷaiyai
aṭiyuṭaṉ kaḻittaeṉṟumām. tōkai-mayil kāriṉaik kaṇṭa mayilkaḷippuṟṟu akavutal iyal
pākaliṉ, tōkai kalavamparappi ēmuṟap payirum eṉṟār. mayil kuruntiṉciṉaiyiliruntu
akavuvatu kiḷikaṭiyum makaḷir,marattiṉ kiḷaimītiruntu olippatu pōlum eṉka.avar
teḷitta pōḻtu ituvākavum iṉṉumvantilarē eṉpatupaṭa niṉṟamaiyiṉ, maṉoḻiyicaiyākum.

195. pālai
[makaṭpōkkiya naṟṟāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5.arum curam iṟanta eṉ perum tōḷ kuṟumakaḷ-ariyacuraneṟiyaik kaṭantu
ceṉṟa eṉ periyatōḷiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiyaḷāya makaḷ, tiruntu vēlviṭalaiyoṭu varum eṉa-
tiruntiya vēliṉaiyuṭaiyatalaivaṉuṭaṉ varum eṉṟu, tāy - avaḷ tāy, puṉaimāṇiñci pūval
ūṭṭi - puṉaital māṭcimaippaṭṭamāṇpuṟṟa puṟaccuvaril cemmaṇ pūci, maṉai maṇalaṭuttu -
maṉaiyiṉ muṟṟattē maṇalaip peytu, mālaināṟṟi - mālaikaḷait toṅka viṭṭu, uvantu
iṉituayarum eṉpa - makiḻntu iṉitē (maṉaiyiṉ kaṇkōlam ceyyum eṉpa ;
5-10. yāṉum-, māṉpiṇai nōkkiṉ maṭa nallāḷai-peṇ māṉ pōlumpārvaiyaiyuṭaiya maṭappam
vāynta avaḷai, īṉṟa naṭpiṟku aruḷāṉāyiṉum-īṉṟāḷ eṉṉum aṉpupaṟṟi eṉpālaruḷ
ceyyāṉāyiṉum, iṉ nakai muṟuval ēḻaiyai -iṉiya nakaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya avaḷai,
pal nāḷkūntal vāri nucuppu ivarntu ōmpiya - palanāḷumkūntalai vārimuṭittu
maruṅkilē ēṟṟippātukātta, nalampuṉai utaviyum maṉ uṭaiyaṉ - naṉmaiporuntiya utaviyum
peritum uṭaiyaḷ āvēṉ;
11. aḵtuaṟikiṟpiṉō naṉṟu - avaṉ ataṉai aṟiyiṉnaṉṟām;
12-14. aṟuvai tōyumoru peruṅkuṭumi - āṭai cūḻnta oru periyaucciyiṉaiyum, ciṟu
pai nāṟṟiya pal talai koṭukōl-ciṟiya pai toṅkaviṭappeṟṟa
palatalaiyaiyuṭaiyavaḷaintakōliṉaiyum uṭaiyaṉāy, ākuvatuaṟiyum mutuvāy vēla - mēl nikaḻ
vataṉai aṟintukūṟum aṟivu vāynta vēlaṉē!
15-19. kaṅkuliṉāṉātu tuyarum - iravil amaiyātu tuyaruṟum,em-emmuṭaiya, māṟā
varupaṉi kaluḻum kaṇ - iṭaiyaṟātuvarum nīruṭaṉ kalaṅki aḻum kaṇkaḷ, iṉitupaṭīiyar-iṉitu
tuyilum poruṭṭu, emmaṉai muntuṟattarumō-ematu maṉaiyiṉkaṇ muṟpaṭak koṇarntutarumō,
(aṉṟi) taṉ maṉai uykkumō - taṉmaṉaiyiṉkaṇ muṟpaṭak koṇṭu celvāṉō, yātuavaṉ kuṟippu
kūṟuvāyāka - at talaimakaṉ kuṟiyai, kūṟuka.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ viṭalaiyoru varumeṉat tāy, ūṭṭi, aṭuttunāṟṟi ayarum eṉpa:
yāṉum ōmpiya utaviyumuṭaiyaṉ; aḵtaṟiciṟpiṉō naṉṟu; vēla! emmaṉaimuntuṟat tarumō taṉ
maṉai muntuṟa uykkumō, avaṉkuṟippu yātu? niṉ kaḻaṅkiṉ tiṭpam kūṟuka.
(vi-rai) iñci - maṉaiyai aṭuttup puṟattē cūḻnta matilpōlum cuvar. ayartal -
koṇṭāṭutalumām. yāṉumeṉṟa ummai, tāy eṉṟataṉaik karuti niṟṟaliṉeccavummaiyākum.
utaviyum eṉṟa ummaiyum, eccavummaiyē. aḵtu aṟikiṟpiṉ - utavi yākiya ataṉaiaṟiyiṉ
īṉṟa naṭpiṟku aruḷāṉāyiṉumaḵtaṟikiṟ piṉō naṉṟu eṉka. aṟikiṟpiṉōnaṉṟu eṉṟatu,
aṟintu eṉ maṉaikkaṭ koṇṭuvariṉnaṉṟa eṉṟapaṭi. aṟuvai tōyum oru peruṅkuṭumi
eṉṟatu,talaiyilē mayirkkaṭṭākac cuṟṟi oru puṟattētoṅkaviṭṭa āṭai tōynta kuṭumi
eṉṟavāṟu,kuṭumiyiṉaiyum kōliṉaiyum uṭaiya vēlaeṉka.piḻaiyāta kuṟi eṉṉum poruṭṭu
maṉ, tilla,mātu, ō: acaikaḷ.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'taṉṉumavaṉum avaḷuñ cuṭṭi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,ippāṭṭiṉaik kūṟi, itu
teyvattoṭu paṭuttuppulampiyatu eṉṟum,

1'kaḷavalarāyiṉum kāmameyp paṭuppiṉum' eṉṉuñcūttirattu, 'pōkkuṭaṉ
aṟintapiṉtōḻiyoṭu keḻīik, kaṟpiṉ ākkattuniṟṟaṟkaṇṇum' eṉṉum tuṟaikku 'emmaṉai
muntuṟat tarumō, taṉmaṉai yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē'eṉṟa pakutiyai utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi,
(itu) 'kaṟpiṉākkattukkaruttu nikaḻtal' eṉṟum 2'veḷippaṭavaraitalpaṭāmai varaital' eṉṉuñ
cūttirattu, 'emmaṉai.. . . . .yātavaṉ kuṟippē' (itu) 'pōlvaṉaveḷippaṭuvataṉ muḷṉark
koṇṭu talaikkaḻintuḻik koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇamnikaḻntamaiyiṉ atuvum veḷippaṭāmal
varaintatām' eṉṟum, 3'koṭuppōriṉṟiyum karaṇamuṇṭē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,icceyyuḷ
koṭuppō riṉṟik karaṇamnikaḻntatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
4'maṟaiveḷippaṭutalum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'emmaṉai. . . . . .yātavaṉ kuṟippē' eṉpatu
maṟai veḷippāṭueṉṟaṉar, pērā.

196. marutam
[parattaiyiṟpirintuvanta talaimakaṟkuk kiḻatti colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. neṭu koṭi nuṭaṅkumnaṟavumalipākkattu-neṭiyakoṭikaḷ acaiyum kaḷ
malinta pākkattilē, nāḷtuṟaip paṭṭa mōṭṭu iru varāal-viṭiyaṟkālaivēṭṭattut tuṟaikkaṇ
akappaṭṭa periyaakaṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya varāl mīṉiṉ, tuṭikaṇkoḻukuṟainoṭuttu - tuṭiyiṉ
kaṇpōṉṟu akaṉṟa vaḷaviyatuṇṭat tiṉai viṟṟu, uṇṭu āṭi-kaḷḷuṇṭu
āṭi,vēṭṭammaṟantu-mīṇṭum vēṭṭam pōtalai maṟantu,tuñcum koḻunarkku-uṟaṅkik
kiṭakkumkaṇavaṉmārkku, pāṭṭi - (avaravar maṉaiviyarāya)pāṇ makaḷir, āmpal akalilai -
āmpalatu akaṉṟailaiyil, amalai vemcōṟu tiraṇṭa viruppantarumcōṟṟai, tīmpuḷi pirampiṉ
tiraḷ kaṉi peytu -pirampiṉ iṉippuṭaṉ kūṭiya puḷippiṉaiyuṭaiyatiraṇṭa paḻattiṉaip
peytu, viṭiyal vaikaṟai iṭūumūra - iruḷ pularum viṭiyaṟ kālattē iṭum ūraṉē!
8-13. tantaikaṇkaviṉ aḻittataṉ tappal-taṉ tantaiyiṉkaṇṇiṉ eḻilaik keṭuttatākiya
tavaṟṟiṟkāka,oṉṟu moḻik kōcar - neṭumoḻiyiṉaiyuṭaiyakōcarkaḷai, kaṭutēr titiyaṉ -
viraintatēriṉaiyuṭaiya titiyaṉatu, aḻuntai - aḻuntūreṉṉumiṭattu, teṟuvarakkoṉṟu
muraṇ pōkiya -accam uṇṭākak kolvittu māṟupāṭu tīrnta,koṭuṅkuḻai aṉṉi
miñiliyiṉ - vaḷainta kuḻaiyiṉaiaṇinta aṉṉi miñili eṉpāḷaippōla, iyalum -kaḷippuṟṟu
naṭakkum, niṉnala takuviyai-niṉnalattiṟkut takkāḷāya parattaiyai, muyaṅkiyamārpu -
taḻuviya mārpiṉai, toṭu kalam-tīṇṭēm,kuṟuka vāral - emmai aṇuka vāraṟka.
(muṭipu) ūra! aṉṉi miñiliyiṉ iyalum niṉnalat takuviyaimuyaṅkiya mārpiṉait
toṭukalam; kuṟukavāral:
vēṭṭam maṟantutuñcum koḻunarkkup pāṭṭi amalai veñcōṟupirampiṉ kaṉipeytu vaikaṟai
iṭūum ūra eṉka.
(vi - rai.) noṭuttu - viṟṟu. pāṭṭi pāṇmakaḷ ; pāṇaṉmaṉaivi. pāṭṭiyar eṉap
paṉmaiyākakkoḷka.viṭiyal vaikaṟai-viṭiyaṟku muṉṉarttākiya vaikaṟaieṉpar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
1tappal-tappaṟkueṉa nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. kōcar eṉpār. taṉtantaiyai aruḷiṉṟik kaṇ
kaḷaintamaiyiṉ, aṉṉimiñili eṉpāḷ ciṉam koṇṭu, kuṟumpiyaṉtitiyaṉ eṉpavarkaḷāl ak
kōcaraikkolvittu māṟupāṭutīrntu kaḷippuṟṟāḷeṉpatu, 2'vāymoḻittantaiyaik kaṇkaḷaintu
aruḷātu, ūrmutu kōcarnavaitta ciṟumaiyil, kalattum uṇṇāḷ vālitumuṭāaḷ, ciṉattiṉ
koṇṭa paṭivam māṟāḷ,maṟaṅkeḻu tāṉaik koṟṟakkuṟumpiyaṉ, ceruviyalnaṉmāṉ titiyaṟ
kuraittavar, iṉṉuyirceruppak kaṇṭuciṉa māṟiya, aṉṉi miñili'eṉpataṉāṟ pōtarum.
koṉṟu-kolvittu eṉappiṟaviṉaiyākak koḷka.

iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatu aḻuntūrileṉpatu, titiyaṉ aḻuntai eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.niṉ pirivāl
yāṉumiṟparattai mutalāyiṉārumvaruntumpaṭi ceytu, cērip parattaiyāṉavaḷperumitaṅ
koṇṭirukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, aṉṉimiñiliyiṉ iyalum niṉṉalat takuviyai eṉattalaimakaḷ
kūṟiṉāḷ. niṉ nalattai nukarvataṟkuttakkavaḷākiya ap parattaiyai muyaṅkiya mārpaiyām
tīṇṭutalum takāteṉpāḷ toṭu kalameṉṟum, kuṟukavāral eṉṟum kūṟiṉaḷ.
(u -ṟai) 'varāaṟ koḻuṅkuṟai noṭuttu uṇṭu āṭi vēṭṭammaṟantu
tuñcumkoḻunarkkuppāṭṭiyaramalaiveñcōṟu vaikaṟaiiṭūum ūra' eṉṟatu, talaivaṉ
iravellāmparattaik kut taṉ mārpiṉai nalki avaḷ tarumiṉpattai nukarntu, talaiviyai
maṟantoḻuki,viṭiyalil talaiviyai niṉaintu varukiṉṟāṉeṉṟapaṭi.
197. pālai
[piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭatalaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, māmalar vaṇṇam iḻanta kaṇṇum -kariya kuvaḷai malariṉ
aḻakiṉai iḻanta kaṇkaḷum, pūnekiḻ aṇaiyil cāaya tōḷum - aḻaku nekiḻntutirainta aṇai
pōṉṟa melinta tōḷkaḷum(koṇṭu), naṉṉar mākkaḷ viḻaivaṉar āyntatol nalam iḻanta
tuyaramoṭu - naṉmaiyuṭaiyaayamakkaḷ virumpi ārāyum paḻaiya nalattiṉaiiḻantataṉālāya
tuyarattuṭaṉ, eṉṉatūum iṉaiyal -ciṟitum varuntātē ;
10-7. tōḷtāḻpuiruḷiya kuvai iru kūntal - tōḷkaḷil tāḻntuiruṇṭa tiraṇṭa
periyakūntalaiyuṭaiya, maṭavōḷtaḻīiya viṟalōṉ mārpil-maṭappattaiyuṭaiyamaṉaiviyait taḻuviyuḷḷa
mēmpaṭṭa talaivaṉmārpil, puṉtalaip putalvaṉūrpuiḻintāṅku-pulliya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
putalvaṉēṟi iṟaṅkuvatu pōṉṟu, kaṭucūl maṭappiṭi taḻīiyaveṇkōṭṭu iṉam cāl
vēḻam-niṟaintacūliṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya piṭiyiṉait taḻuvikkiṭantaveḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya
iṉattuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaḷiṟu,kaṉṟu ūrpu iḻitara-kaṉṟu tammītuēṟi iṟaṅka,paḷḷikoḷḷum
paṉiccuramnīnti-paṭuttirukkum naṭukkattiṉaittarumcuraneṟiyaik kaṭantu, oḷ iṇark koṉṟai
ōṅkumalai attam - oḷḷiya pūṅkottukkaḷaiyuṭaiyakoṉṟai maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya ōṅkiya
malaiyaiyaṭuttaneṟiyil, viṉaivali yuṟūm neñcamoṭu -poruḷīṭṭum viṉaiyai vaṟpuṟukkum
neñcattāl;
9. niṉ nirai vaḷainekiḻa-niṉatu niraittu vaḷaikaḷ nekiḻum paṭi;
18. iṉaiyar ākinampirinticiṉōr - iṅṅaṉam aruḷilarākinammaip pirintu ceṉṟa talaivar;
5 - 9. muṉai eḻamuṉṉuvar ōṭṭiya-pōrceyya muṟpaṭṭōraiōṭṭiya, muraṇmiku
tiruviṉ-valimikka vīraccelvattiṉaiyum, māmiku tāṉai-vīramikkapaṭaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, kaṇṇaṉ
eḻiṉi-kaṇṇaṉ eḻiṉieṉpāṉatu, tēmmutu kuṉṟamiṟantaṉarāyiṉum-tēṉmikka
mutukaṉṟattiṉaikkaṭantuḷarāyiṉum, nīṭalar-nīṭṭit tirātu vallēvantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! toṉṉalamiḻanta tuyaramoṭu iṉaiyal;paṉiccuram nīnti. ōṅkumalai
attamviṉaivaliyuṟūum neñcamoṭu, niṉ niraivaḷai nekiḻaiṉaiyarāki. nap pirintaciṉōr,
kaṇṇaṉ eḻiṉimutukuṉṟam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum nīṭalar.
(vi - rai.) pūnekiḻ aṇai - nekiḻcciyuṟṟa malaraṇai eṉṟumām. aṇai - talaiyaṇai, kaṇṇum
tōḷum koṇṭu eṉaorucol varuvitturaikka. viḻaivaṉar āyntaeṉṟatu, talaiviyiṉ nalattai
ārāytal avarkkuōr viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉṟapaṭi. yāḻa, acai. maṭap piṭikkumaṭavōḷum ataṉait
taḻuviya vēḻattiṟku viṟalōṉumataṉmītu ēṟi iṟaṅkum kaṉṟukkup putalvaṉamuvamam. kaṭuñcūl
maṭappiṭi eṉṟamaiyāl cūluṟṟamaṭavōḷ eṉṟa koḷka. kaṭuñcūl mutaṟ cūlumām.

198. kuṟiñci
[puṇarntu nīṅkiyatalaimakaḷatu pōkku nōkkiya talaimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 18. eṉ neñcē-,
1-11. karanta kāmam - naṅmamuḷmaṟaintuṟaiyum kāmattiṉai, kūṟuvam kollōkūṟulam kol
eṉa - ivaṭkuk kūṟuvēmō aṉṟikkūṟātu viṭuvēmō eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eṇṇi eṇṇi,
kainiṟukkallātu-(muṭivil) aṭakka iyalātu, nayantu nāmviṭṭa naṉ moḻa nampi - virumpi
nāmveḷiyiṭṭiranta iṉiya moḻayai virumpi, arai nāḷyāmattu - pātiyiravil, viḻumaḻai
karantu-peyyum mikka maḻaiyil maṟaintu, kārvirai kamaḻum kūntal-kār kālattu maṇam
kamaḻumvantaluṭaṉ, tū viṉai naṇ nūl ākam poruntiṉaḷ -tūya toḻiṟpāṭamainta nuṇṇiya
nūlālākiya āṭai taṉuṭampiṉkaṇṇē poruntap peṟṟavaḷāy,veṟpiṉ iḷamaḻai cūḻnta maṭamayilpōla
-malaiyiṭattuḷḷa iḷamukil cūḻnta maṭappattaiyuṭaiyamayilpōla, vaṇṭu vaḻip paṭara taṇ malar
vēyntu -vaṇṭukaḷ piṉṉē toṭarak kuḷirnta malarkaḷaiccūṭi, vil vakuppu uṟṟa nalvaṅku
kuṭaccūl -villiṉaippōlum vakai yamaintanaṉmaiporuntiya kuṭaccūl ākiya, am
cilampuoṭukki - aḻakiya cilampiṉai oliyātu aṭakki,añciṉaḷ vantu - accattuṭaṉ vantu,
ūr tuñcuyāmattu - ūrmuḻutum tuyilum yāmat tilē,muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ -
nammaittaḻuimīḷvōḷ;
12 - 3. -ṉṟa kaṟpilcāṉṟa periya am mā arivaiyō allaḷ - niṟaintakaṟpiṉāl uyarnta
perumaiyuṟṟa aḻakiya māmainiṟamuṭaiya peṇṇō allaḷ;
13 - 7. teṉāatuāayanalnāṭṭu aṇaṅka uṭai cilampil -teṟkiṉkaṇ uḷḷatākiya āy eṉpāṉatu
nallanāṭṭil teyvattaiyuṭaiya malaiyil, kavirampeyariya urukeḻu kavāṉ - kaviram eṉum
peyaraiyuṭaiyaaccam poruntiya pakkamalaiyil, nērmalarniṟai cuṉai uṟaiyum - melliya
malarkaḷaiyuṭaiyaniṟainta cuṉaiyil uṟaiyum, cūrmakaḷ eṉṉum -cūraramakaḷē yāvaḷ eṉpēm.
(muṭipu) eṉ neñcē! nām viṭṭa naṉmoḻi nampi, taṇmalar vēyntu añcilam
poṭukki,maṭamayilpōla añciṉaḷ vantu yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷpeyarvōḷ arivaiyō allaḷ;
caṉaiyuṟaiyumcūrmakaḷ eṉṉum.
(vi - rai.) kūntalum nuṇnūlāṭaiyum, uṭampiṉ piṉṉammuṉṉum poruntiyuḷḷa talaiviyiṉ
tōṟṟam,iḷamēkam cūḻnta mayil pōlvatāyiṟ ṟeṉka.kuṭaccūl-cūluṟṟatupōl
puṭaipaṭaṭirukkum cilampu.kuṭaccūl ākiya cilampu eṉa irupeyaroṭṭākakkoḷka. kuṭaiccūl
eṉap pāṭaṅ koḷḷalumām; 1'pacumpoṉkuṭaiccūl cittirac cilampu' eṉpatu kāṇka.
'veḷippāṭṭiṟkuañcik karantu cilampoṭukki ūrtuñcu yāmattuvantu muyaṅkip peyarvāḷ
āyiṉaḷ; aṅṅaṉamvantu celvōḷai ōr arivai yeṉṟu kūṟutaṟkueḷiyaḷ allaḷ; ākaliṉ avaḷaic
cuṉaiyuṟaiyumteyvam eṉpēm;' eṉṟu talaivaṉ taṉneñciṟku viyantu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. tuñcu
ūryāmam eṉpataṉai ūr tuñcu yāmam eṉa māṟuka. ērmalar eṉakkeṇṭu aḻakiya malar
eṉṟalumām.eṉṉum-eṉpēm eṉṉum poruṭṭu.

(mē - ḷ.) 1'paṇpilpeyarppiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu icceyyuḷaikāṭṭi, itu iravuk
kuṟikkaṇ avaṭpeṟṟumalintatueṉṟum, 2'iravukkuṟiyē illakat tuḷḷum, maṉaiyōr
kiḷavikēṭkum vaḻiyatuvē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, 'añcilampoṭukki yaṅciṉaḷ vantu,
tuñcūr yāmattumuyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ' eṉa varuvatu 'maṉaiyōrkiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu'
eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

199. pālai
[poruḷ kaṭaikkūṭṭiyaneñciṟkut talaimakaṉ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16. neñcē-,
1-13. karai pāy veṇtiraikaṭuppa - karaikkaṇṇē pāyum veḷḷiya alaiyaioppa, nirai kāl
oṟṟaliṉ - varicaiyāka varumkāṟṟu mōtutaliṉ, pala uṭaṉ cērpu kal utirum -palavum
oruṅkē cērntu pāṟaiyil utirkiṉṟa,varai cēr marāattu ūḻ malar - malaiyiṉkaṇporuntiya
veṇkaṭampiṉ muṟṟiya malarai, peyalcettu - maḻaiyeṉṟu karuti, uyaṅkal yāṉai nīrnacaikku
alamara-nīr vēṭkaiyāl varuntutalaiyuṭaiṭayayaṉai ataitai taṇit taṟkuc ceṉṟa cuḻala,
cilampivalanta vaṟuciṉai vaṟṟal - cilampi nūlpiṉṉappeṟṟa vaṟiya ciṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
vaṟṟalākiya, alaṅkal ulavai ari niḻal acaii-acaikiṉṟamaraṅkaḷiṉ aṟalpaṭṭa niḻalil taṅki,
tiraṅkumaral kavviya kaiyaṟu tokunilai - vāṭiya maraikaikavviya ceyalaṟṟa kūṭṭattil
(pukuntu), aram tiṉūci tiraḷ nuti aṉṉa - arattāl arāvappaṭṭaūciyiṉ tiraṇṭa muṉaiyai
yotta, tiṇ nilaieyiṟṟa cennāy eṭuttaliṉ-uṟuti poruntiyapaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya
cennāytākkutaliṉ, vaḷimuṉaippūḷaiyiṉ oy eṉṟu alaṟiya - kāṟṟiṉmuṉ pūḷaippūveṉa
viraintu alaṟi yōṭiya, keṭumāṉ iṉaniraitarīiya - kāṇātoḻinta taṉ iṉa
mākiyamāṉkūṭṭattaik kūṭṭaṟku, kalai - kalaimāṉ,katirmāy mālai - ñāyiṟu
maṟaintamālaippōḻtil, āṇ kural viḷikkum - taṉatuāṇ kural tōṉṟa aḻaikkum, kaṭal ēāl
kāṉampiṟpaṭa-kaṭalpōl akaṉṟa kāṭu piṉṉē pōka;
13-6. piṟarpōl -ētilaraippōla, celvēm āyiṉ em celavu naṉṟueṉṉum - nam talaiviyaip
pirintu pōvēmāyiṉematu pōkku naṉṟākum eṉṟeṇṇum, ācai uḷḷamacaivu iṉṟu turappa -
poruḷācaiyuṟṟa niṉ uḷḷamtaḷartaliṉṟic celutta, nī celaṟku uriyai nīcellutaṟku uriyai
āvāy;
16-24. yāṉē - yāṉō,vēypōl taṭaiyiṉa maṉṉum-mūṅkilaippōlavaḷaintaṉavākip poruntum,
taṇṇiya tiraṇṭaperutōḷ arivai oḻiya - kuḷirnta tiraṇṭa periyatōḷiṉaiyuṭaiya nam talaivi
(ivaṇ taṉittu) oḻiya,valampaṭu koṟṟam tanta vāy vāḷ - veṉṟaveṟṟiyiṉait tanta vāytata
vāḷiṉaiyuṭaiya,kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēral-kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral eṉṉum
cēra maṉṉaṉ,kuṭāatu irumpoṉ vākai perantuṟaic ceruvil - mēṟkiṉkaṇṇatākiya
periyapoṉṉiṉaiyuṭaiya vākai maramniṟkam peruntuṟai eṉṉumiṭattu nikaḻntapōril,
polampūṇ naṉṉaṉ - poṟpūṇ aṇintanaṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ, porutu kaḷattu oḻiya-pōrceytu kaḷattil
maṭiya, iḻanta nāṭu tantaṉṉa - tāṉmuṉpu iḻanta nāṭṭaip peṟṟālotta,vaḷamperitu
peṟiṉum - periya celvattaippeṟuvatāyiṉum, vāraleṉ - varuvēṉ allēṉ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! piṟarpōl (talaiviyaip pirintu)celvē māyiṉ em celavu
naṉṟeṉṉumācaiyuḷḷam turappa; kaṭalpōl kāṉam piṟpaṭa nīcelaṟkariyai; yāṉ kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi
nārmuṭiccēral vākaip peruntuṟaic ceruvil naṉṉaṉāṉavaṉporutu kaḷattu oḻintamaiyāl,
tāṉ iḻanta nāṭutantaṉṉa vaḷam peritu peṟiṉum peruntōḷarivai oḻiya vāraleṉ.
(vi - rai.) nirai niraiyāka marāmarattiṉmēṟ pāyntukalakkum kāṟṟiṟku muṟai muṟaiyākak
karaiyiṟpāyayum tirai uvamaiyākum. marāattu utiruma malareṉak vaṭṭuka. nīr nacaikku-nīr
nacaiyāl.vaṟuñciṉai - taḻaiyaṟṟa kiḷai. vaṟṟal - vaṟṟiyamaraṅkaḷ. ulavai-maracceṟivu. kāṭṭiṉ
virivutōṉṟak kaṭalpōl kāṉam eṉṟār.aṉpuṭaiyār piriyak karutārākaliṉ
piṟarpōlcelvōmāyiṉ eṉṟāṉ. piṟarpōl nīcelaṟku uriyai eṉṟa iyait turait
talumām.vākaimaram niṟṟaliṉ, peruntuṟaiyāṉatu vākaippaṟantalai eṉavum paṭum;
ivvākaiyāṉatu naṉṉaṉkāvalmaram eṉpatum, ataṉai nārmuṭiccēraltaṭintāṉ eṉpatum,
1'poṉṉaṅkaṇṇip polantēr naṉṉar, cuṭarvī vākaikkaṭimutal taṭinta, tārmika maintiṉ
nārmuṭiccēral' eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. 2'uruḷpūṅkaṭampiṉ peruvāyil naṉṉaṉai, nilaic
ceruviṉāṟṟalai yaṟattavaṉ, poṉpaṭuvākai muḻumutal taṭintu'eṉpataṉāl kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil
eṉpatumvākaipperuntuṟai eṉpatum ōriṭattaiyēkuṟippaṉapōlum. kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil
naṉṉaṉūr eṉpar patiṟṟappattu uraiyāciriyar.kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral
eṉṉumpeyarkkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvārāyp patiṟṟuppattuuraiyāciriyar 3'kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi
nārmuṭi eṉṟatu, kaḷaṅkāyāṟ ceytakaṇṇiyum nārāṟ ceyta muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu'eṉavum,
'tāṉ muṭi cūṭukiṉṟa kālattu orukāraṇattāl muṭittaṟkut takka kaṇṇiyum
muṭiyumutavāmaiyiṟ kaḷaṅkāyāl kaṇṇiyum nārālmuṭiyum ceytu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟavāṟu'
eṉavumuraittār. iḻantanāṭu tantaṉṉa eṉṟataṉāl, muṉ naṉṉaṉpāl iḻanta nāṭṭai mīḷappeṟṟaṉaṉ
eṉpata pōtarum. nāṭu tantaṉṉa vaḷameṉṟiruppiṉum tanta nāṭu pōlum vaḷam eṉpatukaruttākak
koḷka.

(mē - ḷ.) 1'celaviṭaiaḻuṅkal cellāmai yaṉṟē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,'kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi. .

kuṟittuccelavaḻuṅkutaliṉ pālaiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟār. nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

200. neytal
[talaimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintatōḻi talaimakaṟkuk kuṟainayappak kūṟiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 7. vaḷaimēy parappa - caṅkukaḷ mēyum kaṭaṟparappiṉaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!
1-7. nilāviṉilaṅku maṇal mali maṟukiṉ - nilāvaip pōlaviḷaṅkum maṇal mikka
teruviluḷḷa, pulvēykurampai - pullāl vēyappeṟṟa kuṭicaikaḷaiyuṭaiya,pulāl am
cēri-pulāl nāṟum cēriyākiya empati, ūr eṉa uṇarāc ciṟumaiyoṭu-ōr ūr
eṉṟuuṇaralākāta ciṟumaiyiṉatāy, nīr uṭuttu - nīrkuḻappeṟṟu, iṉṉā uṟaiyuṭṭu
āyiṉum- pelivaṟṟa uṟaiyuḷaiyuṭaiya tāyiṉum, iṉpam-iṉpamtarumāṟṟāl,
orunāḷuṟainticiṉōrkkum-orunāḷtaṅkiyiruntārkkum, vaḻi nāḷ-piṟṟai nāḷ,tampati
maṟakkum paṇpiṉ - tammuṭaiya patiyiṉaiyēmaṟantiṭac ceyyum paṇpiṉaiyuṭaimaiyāl, empati -
ematu appatikku, vantaṉai ceṉmō - vantucelvāyāka;
8-14. pommalpaṭutirai kam eṉa uṭaitarum-polivuṟṟa olikkumalai viraintu citarntiṭum,
maraṉ ōṅku oruciṟai -maram ōṅkiya oru pakkattē, palapārāṭṭi - palavāyaniṉ kuṇaṅkaḷai
yāṅkaḷ pārāṭṭa, ellai emmoṭukaḻippi - pakaṟpoḻutiṉai emmuṭaṉiruntu kaḻittu,el
uṟa - iravu uṟaṅkāl, naltēr pūṭṭalumuriyīr - numatu nalla tēriṉaip
pūṭṭiccellutaṟkum uriyīrāvir;aṟṟaṉṟu-atuvēyumaṉṟi, cērntaṉir celkuvirāyiṉ -
iravil em patiyil taṅkiccelluvīrāyiṉ, yāmum emvarai aḷavaiyilpeṭkuvam - yāṅkaḷum
eṅkaḷukku iyaṉṟa aḷavilpēṇutal ceyvēm; num oppatuvō - (itu)
numkaruttiṟkicaivatāmō? emakku uraitticiṉ -emakkuk kūṟuvīrāka.
(muṭipu) em cēri iṉṉā uṟaiyuṭṭu āyiṉum, iṉpam orunāḷ uṟainticiṉōrkkum
vaḻināḷ tampatimaṟakkum paṇpiṉaiyuṭaiya maiyāl, empati vantaṉaiceṉmō; ellai emmoṭu
kaḻappi elluṟa naltērpūṭṭalum uriyīr; aṟṟaṉṟu, cērntaṉircelkuvir āyiṉ, yāmum em
varai aḷavaiyiṟpeṭkavam; num oppatuvō emakku uraitticiṉ.
(vi - rai.) pulvēy kurampai: pul - paṉaimaṭṭai mutaliyaṉa. pulālnāṟutalāṉum, pulvēy
kurampai yuṭaimaiyāṉum,nīruṭutta lāṉum, cēri iṉṉā uṟaiyuṭṭu āyiṟṟu.aṉṉatāyiṉum
aṉpuṭaiyāruṭaṉ kūṭi nukarum iṉpamperituṭaiyatu eṉpāḷ. 'iṉpam, oru
nāḷuṟainticiṉōrkkum vaḻināḷ, tam patimaṟakkam paṇpiṉ em pati' eṉṟāḷ. oru
nāḷuṟainticiṉōrkkum eṉpataṉāl, palanāḷuṟaivār peṟum iṉpam
kūṟavēṇṭātāyiṟṟu.pārāṭṭi-pārāṭṭa eṉat tirikka. naltērpūṭṭalum eṉṟatu, ataṉ kāriya
mākiya celkaiyaiuṇarttiṟṟu. aṟṟaṉṟu-aḵtaṉṟi, vantucellutaleṉṉum potumai karuti
iraṇṭiṉaiyum oppatuvō eṉaorumaiyāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.

201. pālai
[piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭatalaimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-12. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉaikkēṭpāyāka; poṉṉiṉ avir
eḻilnuṭaṅkum-poṉṉiṉālākiya viṭṭu viḷaṅkumaḻakiyaoḷi acaiyum, aṇikiḷar ōṭai -
aḻukumikkaneṟṟip paṭṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya, viṉai navil yāṉai -ēārc ceyaliṟ payiṉṟa
yāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya,viṟalpōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ - pōrilveṟṟikoḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉatu, pukaḻmali
ciṟappiṉkoṟkai muṉtuṟai-pukaḻmikka ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiyakoṟkaip patiyiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟaiyilē,
avir katirmutamoṭu valampuri corintu-viḷaṅkumoḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya muttukkaḷuṭaṉ
valampuriccaṅkiṉaiyum corintu, taḻai aṇip polintakōṭu ēntu alkul paḻaiyar makaḷir -
taḻaiyuṭaiaṇitalāṟ polivuṟṟa pakkam uyarntaalkuliṉaiyuṭaiya paḻaiyaratu peṇṭir, paṉittuṟai
parava -kuḷirntatuṟaikkaṇ teyvattiṉaip paravāniṟka,pakalōṉ maṟainta anti āriṭai-ñāyiṟu
maṟaintaantiyākiya ariya pōḻtilē, urukeḻu peruṅ kaṭaluvavuk kiḷarntāṅku-accam
poruntiya periyakaṭalāṉatu niṟaimati nāḷil āravāritteḻuntāṟpōla, alarum maṉṟu
paṭṭaṉṟu-
(ūrārkūṟum) alarum maṉṟiṉkaṇ paraviyatu, aṉṉaiyumporuntāk kaṇṇaḷ veyya
uyirkkum-tāyumkaṇtuyilāḷāy vemmaiyuṭaiyaṉavāka mūcceṟiyum,eṉṟu-eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam vaṟi,
evaṉ kaiyaṟṟaṉai-ēṉceyalaṟṟaṉai?
---------
12. ikuḷai - tōḻiyē!
14-9. muṉāatu - paḻaiyatākiya, vāṉ puku talaiya kuṉṟat tuk kavāaṉ-vāṉil ōṅkiya
ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya malaiyiṉ cāraṟ kaṇṇē, peruṅkai eṇkiṉ pēḻvāy ēṟṟai - periya
kaiyiṉaiyum piḷanta vāyiṉaiyumuṭaiya āṇ karaṭi, iruḷ tuṇintaṉṉa kuvavu mayirkkuruḷai -
iruḷait tuṇittu vaittāṟ pōṉṟa tiraṇṭa mayiriṉaiyuṭaiya kuṭṭiyuṭaṉum, tōlmulaip
piṇavoṭu - tiraṅkiya mulaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya peṇ karaṭiyuṭaṉum, tiḷaikkum -
makiḻntirukkum, vēṉil nīṭiya curaṉ iṟantōr-vemmaimikka pālai nilattaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa
nam talaivar;
12-4. cōḻar veṇnel vaippiṉ nalnāṭu peṟiṉum - cōḻa maṉṉaratu veṇṇel viḷaiyum
ūrkaḷaiyuṭaya nalla nāṭṭiṉaiyē peṟuvatāyiṉum, āṇṭu amaintuuṟainar allar-aṅkē
maṉamuvantu taṅkiviṭuvārallar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! amma: ‘peruṅkaṭal uvavuk kiḷarn tāṅku alarum maṉṟu paṭṭaṉṟu;
aṉṉaiyum veyya uyirkkum’ eṉṟu evaṉ kaiyaṟṟaṉai; ikuḷai! curaṉiṟantōr, cōḻar nalnāṭu
peṟiṉum, āṇṭu amaintu uṟainar allar.
(vi - rai.) muttamoṭu valampuri corintu eṉṟamaiyāl, avaṟṟaip peṟutaṟkiṭaṉākiya
koṟkait tuṟaiyiṉciṟappukkūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu. paḻaiyar-neytal nilamākkaḷ: kaḷ viṟpavar.
paḻaiyar makaḷir uvānāḷilē muttaiyum valampuriyaiyuñ corintu kaṭaṟṟeyvattaip
paravuvāreṉka. uvā nāḷiṟ kaṭal poṅki eḻumā kaliṉ peruṅkaṭal uvavuk kiḷirntāṅku
eṉṟār. uvavu:kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉai keṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintatu. alarum,
aṉṉaiyum eccavummaikaḷ. cōḻaratu nalnāṭu pōlum poruḷai āṇṭup peṟuvatāyiṉum eṉpatu
karuttākakkoḷka. eṇkiṉ ēṟṟai kuruḷaiyoṭum piṇavoṭum tiḷaikkum eṉṟatu, aṉpuṟu
takuna vākiya avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu, talaivar viraivil mīḷvar eṉṟu kuṟittu vāṟākum.
202. kuṟiñci
[iravuk kuṟikkaṇ vantu nīṅkum talaimakaṟkut tōḻi colli varaivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-8. vayaṅku veḷ aruviya kuṉṟattu kavāṉ-viḷaṅkumveḷḷiya
aruviyiṉaiyuṭaiya malaiyiṉ cāraṟkaṇṇē,kayam talai maṭappiṭi iṉaṉ ēm ārppa -
melliyatalaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiya piṭi iṉattuṭaṉ iṉpamaṭaiya, pulipakai veṉṟa puṇkūr
yāṉai-puliyākiya pakaiyaiveṉṟapuṇ mikka āṇ yāṉai, kal akac cilampil kaieṭuttu
uyirppiṉ - kaṟkaḷai iṭattē koṇṭapakka malaiyil kaiyai yuyarttipperumūcceṟitalāl,
naliṇar vēṅkai naṟuvī - vēṅkaimarattiṉatu nalla kottukkaḷiluḷḷa naṟumaṇamuṭaiyapūkkaḷ,
kollaṉ kuruku ūtu mitiulaippitirvil poṅki - kollaṉ kuruku ūtumitiulaip
pitirvil poṅki - kollaṉturuttiyai mitittu ūtum ulaiyiṟ pitirntueḻum tīppoṟi
pōlap poṅki eḻuntu, ciṟupalmiṉmiṉi pōla-ciṟiya palavāya miṉmiṉippūccikaḷaip pōla,
palavuṭaṉ maṇi niṟa irumputaltāvum-palavum oruṅkē nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟattiṉaiotta periya
putaril paravi viḻum,nāṭa-nāṭṭaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!
9-15. pāṉāḷ - naṭuiravil, araviṉ utti pai talai tumiya - pāmpiṉatupuḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya
paṭam poruntiya talai tuṇi paṭa, uravuurumu uraṟam uṭkuvaru naṉantalai - valiya
iṭiyāṉatumuḻaṅkum accam varum akaṉṟa iṭattē, tavirvuil uḷḷamoṭu eḵku tuṇaiyāka -
ūkkamkuṉṟutalillāta uḷḷattuṭaṉ vēlē tuṇaiyāka, kaṉaiiruḷ paranta kalatar ciṟuneṟi -
ceṟinta iruḷparaviya
kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya vaḻiyākiyaoṭuṅkiya neṟiyil,tērātuvarūum niṉvayiṉ - ētattai ārāyātu
varumniṉṉiṭattu, ār añar arum
-----------
(pāṭam) 1. āvūrkiḻār maḷḷaṉākaṉār.
---------
paṭar nīntuvōr -poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpamākiya ariya niṉaiviṉainīntuvōr, yāmē aṉṟiyum
uḷarkol -emmaiyaṉṟip piṟarum uḷarō?
(muṭipu) nāṭa! ciṟuneṟi tōratu varum niṉvayiṉ, ārañararumpaṭar nīntuvōr yāmē
aṉṟiyum uḷarō?yāṉaiuyirppiṉ vēṅkai naṟuvī poṅki miṉmiṉipōlap putal tāvum nāṭa
eṉka.
(vi - rai.) uyirppiṉ - uyirttalāl. pitirvu -pitirkiṉṟa tīppoṟi. paṭar-tuyar
niṉaivu.nīntuvōr
eṉṟataṉāl niṉaivākiya veḷḷameṉpatu peṟṟām. nī iravuk kuṟikkaṇ varutalālyām
tuṉpuṟutalpōlat tuṉpuṟuvār piṟar ilareṉpāḷ, nīntuvōr yāmē yaṉṟiyumuḷarkol
eṉṟāḷ; ataṉāl, ematu tuṉpam nīṅkaviraivil varaintu koḷka eṉa varaivu kaṭāyiṉāḷām.
(u - ṟai.) pulippakai veṉṟa yāṉai yuyirppiṉ vēṅkaivīmiṉmiṉipōlap putal tāvum
eṉṟatu, talaivaṉiṭaiyīṭṭiṉaik kaṭantu iravuk kuṟikkaṇ varutalālūreṅkum alar eḻukiṉṟata
eṉṟapaṭi.
203. pālai
[makaṭ pōkkiya 1tāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. uvakkunaḷ āyiṉum uṭalukaḷ āyiṉum-uvappuaṭaivāḷ āyiṉum
māṟupaṭuvāḷ āyiṉum, yāyaṟintu uṇara eṉṉār-tāy āyntuuṇarka eṉṟu karutārāy, alar
viṉai mēval ampalpeṇṭir-koṭiya vāyāl alar kūṟuntoḻilaiyēviruputaluṭaiya puṟaṅkūṟum
peṇṭirkaḷ, niṉmakaḷiṉṉaḷiṉaiyaḷ eṉa-niṉ makaḷ iṉṉa taṉmaiyaḷiṉaiya taṉmaiyaḷ eṉṟu, pal nāḷ
eṉakku vantuuraippavum - pala nāḷum eṉpāl vantu kūṟavum;
5-7. nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉa- ivaḷ nāṇuvāḷ eṉṟu eṇṇi; taṉakku uraippuaṟiyēṉ - avaḷukku
uraittal ceyyēṉāy,naṉikarantu uṟaiyum-mikavum maṟaittu oḻukum,yāṉiv vaṟumaṉai oḻiya -
yāṉ inta vaṟiya maṉaiyiloḻintiṭa;
7-11. tāṉ - eṉmakaḷ, aṉṉai aṟiyiṉ ivaṇ uṟai vāḻkkai - eṉaṉṉai aṟiyiṉ iṅkat
talaivaṉuṭaṉ kaḷaviṟkūṭiyuṟaiyum vāḻkkai, eṉakku eḷitākail eṉa -eṉakku eḷiya toṉṟātal
illai eṉṟueṇṇiṉamaiyāl, kaḻaṟkāl miṉ oḷir neṭuvēliḷaiyōṉ muṉuṟa-kaḻaliṉait taritta
kālaiyummiṉ pēla oḷiviṭum nīṇṭavēlaiyumuṭaiya iḷamaipparuvamuṭaiya taṉ talaivaṉ muṉṉē ēka,
pal malai aru curampōkiya taṉakku-pala malai kaḷaiyuṭaiya ariya kāṭṭiṉaikkaṭantu ceṉṟa
avaḷukku;
11-4. yāṉ aṉṉēṉaṉmai naṉ vāyāka-yāṉ attaṉ maiyiṉēṉallāmai mikka uṇmaiyātal tōṉṟa,
māṉ atarmayaṅkiya malaimutal ciṟu neṟi-vilaṅkukaḷcellumneṟikaḷ piṉṉik
kiṭakkummalaiyaṭiyiṟciṟiya neṟikaḷil, veytuiṭaiyuṟātumuṉṉar eyti - iṭaiyē tīṅku
uṇṭākātavāṟuavarkaṭku muṉṉarē ceṉṟu cērntu, pul eṉ māmalai pulampu koḷ cīṟūr-
polivaṟ ṟirukkumperiya malaiyaiccārnta taṉimaikoṇṭa ciṟiyaūril, cel viruntu āṟṟi -
varum viruntākaēṟṟu avarkaḷai uṇpittu, tuccil irutta -taṅkumaṭattil irukkac ceyya;
17-8. nuṉai kuḻaittualamarum nocci - muṉaikaḷ taḷirkkap peṟṟuacaintiṭum nocci
cūḻnta, maṉaikeḻu peṇṭayāṉ ākuka - maṉaikkuriya peṇṭāka yāṉ āvēṉāka.
(muṭipu) ampal peṇṭir iṉṉaṉ niṉ makaḷeṉap paṉṉāḷeṉakku vantu uraippavum (avaḷ)
taṉakkuuraippaṟiyēṉ karantuṟaiyum yāṉ vaṟumaṉai oḻiya,iḷaiyōṉ muṉṉuṟa aruñcuram pōkiya
taṉakku, yāṉaṉṉēṉ aṉmai vāyāka, ciṟuneṟi eyticcīṟūrc cel viruntāṟṟit
tuccilirutta,maṉaikeḻu peṇṭu yāṉākuka.
(vi - rai.) iṉṉaḷ iṉaiyaḷ: oru poruḷ kuṟitta coṉṉaṭai.ampaṟ peṇṭiratu koṭumai
kūṟuvāḷ, avar puṟattēuraittalaṉṟip piṉṉāḷ eṉpāl vantu,niṉmakaḷ iṉaiyaḷeṉa uraittār
eṉṟum,ataṉaikkēṭṭum taṉ makaḷ nāṇāl varuntāta paṭi,avaḷukku uraiyāmaiyē yaṉṟi
evvāṟṟālumatupulappaṭā mal tāṉ maṟaittalum ceyta arumaitōṉṟat, ‘taṉak kuraippaṟiyēṉ
nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷeṉanaṉikaran tuṟaiyum yāṉ’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taṉmakaḷ nīṅkiya maṉai ciṟitum
poliviṉṟi vaṟuviyamaṉaiyākat tōṉṟaliṉ, yāṉ iv vaṟumaṉai yoḻiyaeṉṟāḷ. aruñcuram
pōyiṉāḷ; aṅṅaṉam pōkiyataṉakku eṉa vēṟu aṟuttu uraikka. avaḷ talaivaṉoṭunukarum
iṉpattai uṇarnta nāṉ veṟāmaiyē yaṉṟiuvattalum ceytal muḻu uṇmaiyātalai
ivvāṟaṉṟiveḷippaṭuttal ariteṉpāḷ, ‘yāṉaṉṉēṉaṉmai naṉvāyākac, cīṟūrc
celviruntāṟṟit tuccilirutta maṉaikeḻu peṇṭi yāṉākuka’eṉṟāḷ. maṉ: acainilai;
oḻiyicaiyumām.
204. mullai
[viṉaimuṟṟiya talaimakaṉtērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9.pāka - pākaṉē!
1-4. ulaku uṭaṉniḻaṟṟiya tolaiyā veṇkuṭai - ulaka muḻutum niḻalceyta keṭāta
veṇkoṟṟak kuṭaiyiṉaiyum,kaṭalpōl tāṉai-kaṭalpōṉṟa cēṉaiyiṉaiyum, kalimā-cerukkiya
kutiraiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, vaḻuti - pāṇṭiyaḷ,veṉṟu amar uḻanta viyaṉ peru pācaṟaic ceṉṟu
-pōril muyaṉṟu veṉṟa akaṉṟa periya pācaṟaikkaṇṇē ceṉṟu, viṉai muṭittaṉam āyiṉ -
viṉaiyaiveṟṟipeṟa muṭittōm ātalāṉum;
5-8. kār peyaṟkuetiriya kāṇtaku puṟavil-kārkālattiṉ maḻaiyaiēṟṟukkoṇṭu (taḻaitta)
kāṇṭaṟku iṉiyakāṭṭil, kaṇam koḷ vaṇṭiṉ am ciṟaittoḻuti - kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka varum
aḻakiyaciṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭiṉ tokuti, maṇam kamaḻa mullaimālai ārppa-maṇam vīcum mullai
malaril mālaikkālattē āravārikka, poḻutu utukkāṇvantaṉṟu-utō, kār kālam vantu
viṭṭatu(ātalāṉum);
10-4. veṇnelarinar-veṇṇellai arivōr (aṭikkum),maṭivāy taṇṇumai-tōl
maṭaṅkiyavāyiṉaiyuṭaiyakiṇaiyiṉ oli, pal malarp poykaipaṭupuḷ oppum-pala malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya
poykaiyilporuntiya paṟavaikaḷai ōṭṭum, kāy nelpaṭappai-viḷainta neṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya
vayalkaḷaiyuṭaiya,vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭit taṇṭalai kamaḻum kūntal-vāṇaṉatuciṟukuṭiyiluḷḷa cōlai
eṉa maṇakkum kūntalaiyum, oḷtoṭi-oḷi poruntiya vaḷaiyalaiyum uṭaiya, maṭantaitōḷ
iṇaipeṟa - nam talaiviyiṉ tōḷiṇaiyaimuyaṅkutaṟku;
4. iṉṟē -ippoḻutē;
8-9. niṉ nal viṉaineṭu tēr - niṉata nalla toḻiṟ ṟiṟam amaintanīṇṭa tēr, val viraintu
celka - mika viraintucelluvatāka;
(muṭipu) pāka! vaḻuti pācaṟai viṉai muṭittaṉam ātalāṉum, utōpoḻutu vantaṉṟu
(ātalāṉum), maṭantai tōḷiṇai peṟa,neṭuntēr viraintu celka.
(vi - rai.) aracaṉatu veṟṟiyaik kuṭaikku ēṟṟi, koṟṟak kuṭaieṉpavākaliṉ tolaiyā
veṇkaṭai eṉṟār. veṉṟuamar uḻanta-amar uḻantu veṉṟa eṉa māṟuka.muṭittaṉam
āyiṉ-muṭittaṉam ātalāl eṉka. ātalāleṉpataṉaip piṉṉuṅkūṭṭi, muṭittaṉam
ātalāṉum,poḻutu vantaṉṟu ātalāṉam eṉa uraittuk koḷka.vaṇṭākiya toḻuti eṉka.
utukkāṇ - utō tērval viraintu celka eṉṟatu, cellumāṟu celuttukaeṉṟapaṭi.
205. pālai
[talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talaivi vaṟpuṟukkum tōḻikkuccolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. (tōḻi!), tām - nam talaivar, uyir kalantuoṉṟiya toṉṟupaṭu
naṭpil-uyiruṭaṉ kalantuporuntiya pala piṟavikaḷiṉum toṭarntu varumnaṭpiṉāl, ceyirtīr
neñca moṭuceṟintōrpōla-kuṟṟamaṟṟaneñcattālkalantōrpōla, taiyal - peṇṇē,niṉvayiṉ
piriyalam yām eṉa -niṉṉiṭattiṉiṉ ṟum yām eṉṟum piriyēmeṉṟu, poy val uḷḷamoṭu
purivuṇakkūṟi-poymikka uḷḷattāl yām virumpumāṟukūṟi, tuṇivuil koḷkaiyarāki -
uṟutiyillātakoḷkaiyiṉarāki, iṉi-ippoḻutu, nōymalivaruttamoṭu nutal pacappu
ūra-tuṉpam mikkavaruttattāl namatu neṟṟiyiṟ pacalai parakkavum,nāmaḻa-nām aḻavum,
tuṟantaṉarāyiṉum-nammaippirintu ceṉṟārāyiṉum;
15-24. maḻai kālaṟcirattu māl iruḷ nīṅki - maḻaiyeṉṉappeyyum muṉpaṉip paruvattu
mayakkattiṉait tarumiruḷ nīṅka, nīṭu amai nivanta niḻalpaṭucilampil-nīṇṭa mūṅkil
uyarntaniḻalporuntiya pakka malaiyil, kaṭāa yāṉaikkavuḷ maruṅku uṟaḻa-matakkaḷiṟṟiṉatu
kavuḷiṉpakkattiṉai yoppa, -m ūrpu iḻitarukāmarceṉṉi-nīr ūrntu iṟaṅkum
aḻakiyauccimalaiyil, puli uri vari ataḷ kaṭuppa -puliyiṉ uriyākiya varikaḷaiyuṭaiya
tōlai yoppa,nāḷpū vēṅkai naṟumalar utira-maṇanāḷaik kāṭṭumpūkkaḷāya vēṅkaiyiṉ naṟiya
pūkkaḷ utirntiṭa,kaliciṟantu mēkku eḻu peru ciṉai ēṟi - cerukkumikku
alvēṅkaiyiṉ mēṉōkki yeḻunta periyakiḷaiyil ēṟi, kaṇa kalai - kūṭṭamāya āṇ
kuraṅkukaḷ,kūppiṭūu ukaḷum-tam iṉaṅkaḷaik kūppiṭṭuttāvum, kuṉṟakac
ciṟuneṟi-kuṉṟiṭaiyēcellumciṟiya neṟiyākiya, kal piṟaṅku -riṭaivilaṅkiya-kaṟkaḷ
viḷaṅkiya ariya iṭaṅkaḷkuṟukkiṭṭa, colpeyar tēetta curaṉiṟantōr-moḻi vēṟupaṭaṭa
tēyaṅkaḷiluḷḷapālaiyaik kaṭantu ceṉṟa avar;
8-14. vāymoḻinilaiiya cēṇ viḷaṅku nal icai-uṇmai nilaipeṟṟaneṭuntūrattu
viḷaṅkumnalla pukaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya,vaḷaṅkeḻu kōcar-celvam mikka kōcarkaḷatu,viḷaṅku paṭai
nūṟi - viḷakkamuṟṟa paṭaiyiṉai aḻittu,nilamkoḷa veḵkiya polampūṇ kiḷḷi -
avarkaḷnilattaik kaikkoḷḷa virumpiyapoṟpūṇaiyuṭaiya cōḻaṉatu, pūviri neṭuṅkaḻināppaṇ -
pūkkaḷ virinta nīṇṭa kaḻiyiṉ naṭuvē,paṭappai - tōṭṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya, perum peyark
kāvirippaṭṭiṉattu aṉṉa - periya pukaḻaiyuṭaiyakāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattai yotta, ceḻunakar
-vaḷaviya nam illil, nalviruntu ayarmār - nallaviruntāṟṟataṟku, ēm uṟa - iṉpam
uṇṭāka,viḻuniti eḷitiṉiṉ eytuka-ciṟanta poruḷaieḷitiṉil aṭaivārāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talaivar, ‘taiyal! niṉ vayiṟpiriyalam yām’ eṉakkūṟi, nutal
pacappūra nāmaḻat tuṟantaṉarāyiṉum, coṟpeyar tēetta curaṉiṟantōrāya avar, kāvirip
paṭṭiṉattaṉṉa ceḻunakar nalvirun tayarmār ēmuṟa viḻunitieḷitiṉiṉ
eytuka;cilampilāmūrpu iḻitaru ceṉṉiyil vēṅkai malar utira,peruñciṉai ēṟik kaṇakkalai
ukaḷum kuṉṟakacciṟuneṟiyāya āriṭai vilaṅkiya curaṉ eṉka.
(vi - rai.) toṉṟupaṭu naṭpu-paḻamai poruntiya naṭpu; palapiṟavikaḷiṉum toṭarntu varum
naṭpu eṉṟapaṭi.kaḻināppaṇ paṭap paiyaiyuṭaiya kāvirippaṭṭiṉameṉka. tilla:
viḻaivupporuṭṭu. aṟcirattumāliruḷ nīṅki eṉavē piṉpaṉiyum vēṉilumpōntamai
peṟṟām. nīṅki-nīṅka eṉattirikka. aruvi nīroḻukum malaiyiṉ ceṉṉikkumatañ cintum
yāṉaiyiṉ kavuḷ uvamaiyākakkūṟappaṭṭatu; 1'malaipaṭukaṭāam mātirattu iyampa’ eṉṉum
toṭarum,ataṉ ciṟappāl uttarāṟṟuppaṭai malaipaṭukaṭāmeṉap peyar eytiyatum īṇṭu
aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. nāṭpū- putiyapū eṉṟumām.

206. marutam
[vāyil vēṇṭic ceṉṟaviṟalikkut talaimakaḷ vāyil maṟuttu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-6. tōḻi-, nal makiḻ - nalla kaḷippuṭaṉ,pēṭippeṇ kāṇṭu-pēṭiyākiya
peṇaṇiṉ uruvampūṇṭu, āṭu kai kaṭuppa - āṭuvāṉatu piṉceṉṟumēlvaḷainta kaiyai yoppa,
narakuvap paṇaitta tirimaruppu erumai - viḷaṅkutaluṟap perutta muṟukkuṇṭakompiṉaiyuṭaiya
erumaiyiṉ, mayirkkaviṉ koṇṭa mā tōl iru puṟam-mayirāl aḻakupeṟṟakariya tōliṉaiyuṭaiya
periya mutukil, ciṟutoḻilmakāar ēṟi- kuṟuntoḻilkaḷaic ceyyumciṟuvarkaḷ ēṟi,
cēṇōrkku tuṟa kal mantiyiltōṉṟum ūraṉ – cēymaiyiluḷḷārkku uruṇṭaik kalliṉ mītuḷḷa
mantipōlakkāṇappaṭum ūraṉākiya nam talaivaṉ;
7-12. māri īṅkai mātaḷir aṉṉa - mārik kālattu īṅkaic ceṭiyiṟṟōṉṟum ciṟanta
taḷiriṉai otta, am mā mēṉiāy iḻai makaḷir - aḻakiya māmai
niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyamēṉiyiṉaiyum āynta aṇiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya parattaiyaratu,āram tāṅkiya alar
mulai ākattu - muttumālaiyaiēntiya paranta mulaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mārpa kattē, ārākkātaloṭu
tār iṭai kuḻaiya-amaiyāta kātaluṭaṉ pūmālai iṭaippaṭṭuk kuḻaiya, muḻavu
mukampularāviḻavu uṭai viyaṉ nakar - muḻaviṉ oli ōytalillātaviḻaviṉaiyuṭaiya periya
maṉaiyil, vatuvai mēvalaṉākaliṉ - maṇattiṉaip poruntutaluṭaiyaṉātaliṉ;
12-16. atu pulantu acceliṉai veṟutta, aṭupōr vēḷir vīrai muṉtuṟai -vellum
pōriṉai
valla vēḷirkaṭku uriya vīraieṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa tuṟaiyiṉ muṉṉiṭattē, neṭuveḷ uppiṉ
nirampāk kuppai-nīṇṭukiṭakkum veḷḷai yuppiṉ aḷavilaṭaṅakāta kuviyal, perum peyaṟku
urukiyāṅku-mikka maḻaiyāl urukiṉāṟpōla, taṭa mel tōḷ - eṉatu periyamelliya tōḷkaḷ
melintu, tiruntu iaiānekiḻntaṉa - tiruntiya vaḷaikaḷ nekiḻappeṟṟaṉa;
1. eṉ eṉappaṭumkol-ivvāṟākavum iv viṟaliyiṉ kūṟṟuyāteṉappaṭum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! ūraṉ vatuvai mēvalaṉ ākaliṉ, meṉ tōḷiḻai nekiḻntaṉa; (ivvāṟākavum
ivviṟaliyiṉkūṟṟu) eṉ eṉappaṭum!
(vi - rai.) pēṭippeṇ - pēṭiyākiyapeṇṇeṉa iru peyaroṭṭu. peṇkoṇṭu -
peṇṇiṉatuuruvu koṇṭu. āṭu kai kaṭuppa eṉṟamaiyāṟpiṉceṉṟu mēl vaḷainta maruppu eṉka.
erumaiyiṉpuṟattiṟkut tuṟukallum mākaarukku mantiyumuvamam. tār iṭai kuḻaiya mēvalaṉ
eṉavum,
atu pulantutōḷ iḻai nekiḻntaṉa eṉavum kūṭṭuka. muḻuvu mukampulāra viḻavuṭai viyaṉakar
vatuvai mēvalaṉ eṉṟamaiyāl,avaṉ parattaiyar illil vatuvai ayartalai avvāravāramē
pulappaṭuttum
eṉṟāḷāyiṟṟu. uppiṉkuppai peyaṟku urukiyāṅku eṉṟa karuttu, 1'kaṭalviḷaiyamutam
peyaṟkēṟṟāaṅ kuruki yukutal’ 2'uppiyalpāvai yuṟaiyuṟṟatapōla, ukku viṭumeṉṉuyir’
3'uppoy cakaṭam, perum peyaṟalaiya vīntāṅku’eṉap palaviṭattum varutal kāṇka.

207. pālai
[makaṭ pōkkiya 4tāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. aṇaṅka uṭai munnīr paranta ceṟuviṉtayvattaiyuṭaiya kaṭaliṉnīr paraviya uppu
viḷaiyumvayalil, uṇaṅku tiṟam peyarnta veṇkalamiḻtam - nīr kāynta taṉmaiyāl
vēṟākiyaveḷḷiya uppākiya amiḻtiṉai, kuṭavula maruṅkiṉuymmār - mēṟkut ticaiyiluḷḷa
iṭaṅkaḷukkucceluttum poruṭṭu, puḷ ōrttu - nimittampārttu, paṭai amaittu eḻunta
peruñcey āṭavar -paṭaikkalaṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭepanta periyavīrac ceyalkaḷaip puriyum
āṭavar, niraiparappoṟaiya narai puṟak kaḻutai-aṭukkiya uppuppotiyāya cumaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
veḷḷiya puṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyakaḻutaikaḷiṉ, kuṟaik kuḷampu utaitta kalpiṟaḻiyaviṉ-tēynta
kuḷampu utaittalālparaṟkaṟkaḷ piṟaḻntu kiṭakkum vaḻiyāya, vemcuram pōḻnta añcuvaru
kavalai - koṭiya
pālaiyiṉaiūṭaṟuttuc cellum accantōṉṟum kavarttaneṟikaḷil;
8-12. miñiṟaārkaṭāam karantuviṭu kavuḷa - vaṇṭukaḷ poruntummatam karantoḻinta
kaṉṉattiṉaiyuṭaiya, veyiltiṉa varuntiya nīṭu maruppu oruttal - veyilnalitalāl varuntiya
nīṇṭa kompiṉaiyuṭaiyakaḷiṟṟiṉ, piṇar aḻi peruṅkai puraṇṭa kūval -caruccarai aḻinta
periya kai nīrvēṇṭittuḻāvip pārtta kiṇaṟṟiṉai, aṟaṉ ilāḷaṉtōṇṭa - aṟam illāṉākiya
taṉ talaivaṉtōṇṭutalāl ūṟiya, teḷ uvari kuṟaikkuṭa mukavai -teḷinta uvaraiyuṭaiyatākiya
kuṟaikkuṭamāka mukakkappaṭṭa nīrai;
14-17. tēm kalantuaḷaiiya tīmpāl ēnti-tēṉaip peytu naṉkukalanta iṉiya pālai
ēntikkoṇṭu, kūḻai uḷarntumōḻaimai kūṟapum - kūntalaik kōti nayamoḻikaḷaikkūṟavum,
maṟutta collaḷ āki - maṟutturaippāḷāki, veṟutta uḷḷa moṭu uṇṇātōḷ -
veṟuppuṟṟauḷḷattuṭaṉ ataṉai uṇṇātōḷākiya eṉmakaḷ;
12-13. veytuuyirttu - veppattuṭaṉ mūcceṟintu, piṟainutal viyarppa uṇṭaṉaḷ
kol-piṟaimati pōṉṟaneṟṟi viyarttiṭap parukiṉaḷō!
(muṭipu) añcuvaru kavalaiyil, kūvalai aṟaṉilāḷaṉ tōṇṭa,kuṟaikkuṭa mukavai nīrai,
tēmkalantu aḷaiiya tīmpālaiēnti uḷarntu mōḻaimai kūṟavum uṇṇātōḷ,veytuyirttu nutal
viyarppa uṇṭaṉaḷ kol.
(vi - rai.) kuṭapula maruṅkiṉ uymmār eṉṟamaiyāl, uppuviḷaintatu kuṇakaṭaṟ ceṟuvil
eṉpatu pōtarum.paṭai-cēṉaiyu mām. cey-vīracceykai; mutaṉilaittoḻiṟpeyar. kavuḷavākiya
oruttal eṉavum,uvariyākiya mukavai eṉavum iyaiyum. mukavai-mukantanīr. taṇkaṇ: kaṇ acai.
1'mīṉkaṇ aṟṟataṉcuṉaiyē’ eṉpuḻippōla. talaivipāl mikkaaṉpuṭaiyaṉāyiṉum tamariṟ
pirittukkoṇṭuceṉṟamaiyiṉ aṟaṉilāḷaṉ eṉṟāḷ mōḻaimai -tāḻnta moḻi.
